"use client";

import React, { Suspense, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { signIn } from "next-auth/react";
import { Building2, Eye, EyeOff, Loader2, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { saveOfflineSession } from "@/lib/offline/db";

function LoginForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  // SÃ©curitÃ© anti-open redirect : n'accepte que les chemins relatifs
  const rawCallbackUrl = searchParams.get("callbackUrl") || "/dashboard";
  const callbackUrl =
    rawCallbackUrl.startsWith("/") && !rawCallbackUrl.startsWith("//")
      ? rawCallbackUrl
      : "/dashboard";

  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) {
      setErrorMessage("Veuillez renseigner votre email et votre mot de passe.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMessage(null);

    try {
      const res = await signIn("credentials", {
        email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),
        password,
        redirect: false,
        callbackUrl,
      });

      if (res?.error) {
        setErrorMessage("Email ou mot de passe incorrect.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      // RÃ©cupÃ©ration de la session fraÃ®che pour hydrater le stockage Dexie hors-ligne
      try {
        const sessionRes = await fetch("/api/auth/session");
        if (sessionRes.ok) {
          const sessionData = await sessionRes.json();
          if (sessionData?.user?.id) {
            await saveOfflineSession({
              id: sessionData.user.id,
              name: sessionData.user.name || "Utilisateur",
              email: sessionData.user.email || email,
              role: sessionData.user.role || "RESIDENT",
              expiresAt: Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1000,
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[Login] Sauvegarde de session hors-ligne diffÃ©rÃ©e :", err);
      }

      // Redirection fluide
      window.location.href = callbackUrl;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Une erreur est survenue lors de la connexion.";
      setErrorMessage(msg);
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full flex-col justify-center px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md">
        {/* En-tÃªte avec logo SyndicPro */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center">
          <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-600 to-sky-400 text-white shadow-lg shadow-sky-500/30">
            <Building2 className="h-7 w-7" />
          </div>
          <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-3xl dark:text-white">
            Syndic<span className="text-sky-500">Pro</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            Connectez-vous pour accÃ©der Ã  votre espace de copropriÃ©tÃ©
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Carte de formulaire */}
        <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl shadow-slate-200/40 sm:p-8 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/90 dark:shadow-none">
          {errorMessage && (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="mb-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-3.5 text-sm text-rose-800 dark:border-rose-900/50 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-300"
            >
              <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400" />
              <div className="flex-1 font-medium">{errorMessage}</div>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
            {/* Champ Email */}
            <div>
              <label
                htmlFor="email"
                className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
              >
                Adresse email
              </label>
              <div className="mt-1.5">
                <input
                  id="email"
                  name="email"
                  type="email"
                  autoComplete="username"
                  required
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="nom@exemple.com"
                  className="block w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3.5 py-3 text-base text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-sky-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sky-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:text-white"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Champ Mot de passe */}

              <label
                htmlFor="password"
                className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
              >
                Mot de passe
              </label>
              <div className="relative mt-1.5">
                <input
                  id="password"
                  name="password"
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  autoComplete="current-password"
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
                  className="block w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3.5 py-3 pr-11 text-base text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-sky-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sky-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:text-white"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  aria-label={showPassword ? "Masquer le mot de passe" : "Afficher le mot de passe"}
                  className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 flex items-center pr-3.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-300"
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="h-5 w-5" /> : <Eye className="h-5 w-5" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="flex min-h-[48px] w-full items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-sky-500 px-4 py-3 text-base font-bold text-white shadow-md shadow-sky-500/25 transition-all hover:bg-sky-600 active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
                  Connexion en cours...
                </>
              ) : (
                "Se connecter"
              )}
            </button>
          </form>

          {/* Lien vers inscription */}
          <div className="mt-6 text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              Pas encore de compte ?{" "}
              <Link
                href="/register"
                className="font-semibold text-sky-600 hover:text-sky-500 dark:text-sky-400"

        </div>

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [name, setName] = useState("");

  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMessage(null);

    if (password.length < 8) {
      setErrorMessage("Le mot de passe doit comporter au moins 8 caractÃ¨res.");
      return;
    }

    if (!/[a-zA-Z]/.test(password) || !/[0-9]/.test(password)) {
      setErrorMessage("Le mot de passe doit contenir au moins une lettre et un chiffre.");
      return;
    }

      setErrorMessage("Les mots de passe ne correspondent pas.");
      return;

          name: name.trim(),
          email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),
          password,
          confirmPassword,
          phone: phone.trim() || undefined,
        }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(errorData.error || "Une erreur est survenue lors de l'inscription.");
      }

      // 2. Connexion automatique suite Ã  la crÃ©ation

        email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),

      });

          const sessionRes = await fetch("/api/auth/session");

            if (sessionData?.user?.id) {
              await saveOfflineSession({
                id: sessionData.user.id,
                name: sessionData.user.name || name,
                email: sessionData.user.email || email,
                role: sessionData.user.role || "RESIDENT",
                expiresAt: Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1000,
              });

"use client";

import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, PieChart } from "lucide-react";
import { ResourcePage } from "@/components/crud/ResourcePage";
import { apartmentConfig } from "@/lib/resources/registry";
import type { ApartmentDTO } from "@/types/api";

export default function ApartmentsPage() {
  return (
    <ResourcePage
      config={apartmentConfig}
      headerExtra={(items: ApartmentDTO[]) => {
        const totalShares = items.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (Number(curr.shares) || 0), 0);
        const isOver = totalShares > 1000;
        const isExact = totalShares === 1000;

        return (
          <div
            className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 transition-all ${
              isOver
                ? "border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-50/80 text-rose-900 dark:border-rose-500/30 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-200"
                : isExact
                ? "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-50/80 text-emerald-900 dark:border-emerald-500/30 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-200"
                : "border-sky-500/20 bg-sky-50/60 text-sky-900 dark:border-sky-500/30 dark:bg-sky-950/30 dark:text-sky-200"
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                {isOver ? (
                  <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-rose-500 text-white shadow-sm">
                    <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 stroke-[2.25]" />
                  </div>
                ) : isExact ? (
                  <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-500 text-white shadow-sm">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 stroke-[2.25]" />
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-sky-500 text-white shadow-sm">
                    <PieChart className="h-5 w-5 stroke-[2]" />
                  </div>
                )}
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold">
                    {isOver
                      ? "Attention : DÃ©passement de la base des tantiÃ¨mes !"
                      : isExact
                      ? "RÃ©partition complÃ¨te et conforme (1 000 / 1 000)"
                      : "RÃ©partition courante des tantiÃ¨mes"}
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-xs opacity-85">
                    {isOver
                      ? `Le total des tantiÃ¨mes est de ${totalShares.toLocaleString("fr-FR")} / 1 000. Il excÃ¨de la quote-part maximale lÃ©gale de ${totalShares - 1000} tantiÃ¨mes.`
                      : isExact
                      ? "La somme de l'ensemble des quotes-parts atteint exactement la base lÃ©gale de 1 000 tantiÃ¨mes."
                      : `Total actuel : ${totalShares.toLocaleString("fr-FR")} / 1 000 tantiÃ¨mes (${1000 - totalShares} tantiÃ¨mes restants Ã  allouer).`}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="hidden shrink-0 text-right sm:block">
                <span className="text-lg font-extrabold">{totalShares}</span>
                <span className="text-xs opacity-75"> / 1000</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      }}
    />
  );
}

import { HttpError, handle, isDuplicateKeyError, json, parseBody } from "@/lib/api";
import { User } from "@/models/User";
import { registerSchema } from "@/lib/validations/auth";
import { hashPassword } from "@/lib/password";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

/**
 * POST /api/auth/register
 * Inscription publique d'un utilisateur.
 * RÃ¨gle de sÃ©curitÃ© stricte : Le rÃ´le crÃ©Ã© est STRICTEMENT et TOUJOURS Role.RESIDENT.
 * Tout champ "role" transmis par le client est sciemment ignorÃ© (protection anti-Ã©lÃ©vation de privilÃ¨ges).
 */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  return handle(async () => {
    const data = await parseBody(req, registerSchema);
    const normalizedEmail = data.email.toLowerCase().trim();

    const existing = await User.findOne({ email: normalizedEmail }).lean();
    if (existing) {
      throw new HttpError(409, "Un compte avec cette adresse email existe dÃ©jÃ ");
    }

    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(data.password);

    try {
      const user = await User.create({
        name: data.name,
        email: normalizedEmail,
        passwordHash,
        phone: data.phone,
        role: Role.RESIDENT, // Toujours RESIDENT
        isActive: true,
      });

      return json(
        {
          id: user._id.toString(),
          name: user.name,
          email: user.email,
          role: user.role,
        },
        201
      );
    } catch (err) {
      if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
        throw new HttpError(409, "Un compte avec cette adresse email existe dÃ©jÃ ");
      }
      throw err;
    }
  });
}

import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { HttpError, isValidObjectId, json, parseBody } from "@/lib/api";
import { withAuth, getSyndicResidenceIds, type AuthUser } from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import { Apartment } from "@/models/Apartment";
import { Charge } from "@/models/Charge";
import { Residence } from "@/models/Residence";
import { Role, ChargeStatus } from "@/types/enums";
import { z } from "zod";
import { objectId, period } from "@/lib/validations/common";

const generateChargesSchema = z.object({
  residenceId: objectId,
  period,
  totalBudget: z.number().positive("Le budget total doit Ãªtre strictement positif"),
  dueDate: z.string().optional(),
});

/**
 * POST /api/charges/generate
 * GÃ©nÃ¨re automatiquement les charges d'une rÃ©sidence pour un mois donnÃ©,
 * au prorata des tantiÃ¨mes de chaque lot, sans doublon sur {apartment, period}.
 * RÃ´les : ADMIN, SYNDIC
 */
export const POST = withAuth(
  async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser) => {
    const data = await parseBody(req, generateChargesSchema);

    if (!isValidObjectId(data.residenceId)) {
      throw new HttpError(400, "Identifiant de rÃ©sidence invalide");
    }

    // VÃ©rification des droits du syndic
    if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
      const syndicResidences = await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id);
      if (!syndicResidences.includes(data.residenceId)) {
        throw new HttpError(403, "Vous n'administrez pas cette copropriÃ©tÃ©");
      }
    }

    const residence = await Residence.findById(data.residenceId).lean();
    if (!residence) {
      throw new HttpError(404, "RÃ©sidence introuvable");
    }

    const apartments = await Apartment.find({ residence: data.residenceId }).lean();
    if (apartments.length === 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, "Aucun lot enregistrÃ© dans cette rÃ©sidence");
    }

    const totalShares = apartments.reduce((sum, apt) => sum + (Number(apt.shares) || 0), 0);
    if (totalShares <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(
        400,
        "La somme des tantiÃ¨mes de la rÃ©sidence est nulle. Veuillez renseigner les tantiÃ¨mes des lots."
      );
    }

    // Date d'Ã©chÃ©ance par dÃ©faut : le 15 du mois de la pÃ©riode
    const [yearStr, monthStr] = data.period.split("-");
    const year = parseInt(yearStr, 10);
    const month = parseInt(monthStr, 10);
    const defaultDueDate = new Date(year, month - 1, 15);
    const dueDate = data.dueDate ? new Date(data.dueDate) : defaultDueDate;

    let createdCount = 0;
    let skippedCount = 0;

    for (const apt of apartments) {
      // DÃ©tection de doublon sur { apartment, period }
      const exists = await Charge.findOne({
        apartment: apt._id,
        period: data.period,
      }).lean();

      if (exists) {
        skippedCount++;
        continue;
      }

      // Calcul au prorata strict des tantiÃ¨mes
      const amount = Math.round(((data.totalBudget * (apt.shares || 0)) / totalShares) * 100) / 100;

      await Charge.create({
        apartment: apt._id,
        period: data.period,
        amount,
        paidAmount: 0,
        dueDate,
        status: ChargeStatus.PENDING,
      });

      createdCount++;
    }

    return json(
      {
        message: `${createdCount} charge(s) gÃ©nÃ©rÃ©e(s) pour la pÃ©riode ${data.period}`,
        createdCount,
        skippedCount,
        totalApartments: apartments.length,
        totalBudget: data.totalBudget,
        period: data.period,
      },
      201
    );
  },
  { roles: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC] }
);

import { json } from "@/lib/api";
import { withAuth, type AuthUser, getSyndicResidenceIds, getResidentApartmentIds, getResidentResidenceIds } from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import { Role, IncidentStatus, ChargeStatus } from "@/types/enums";
import { Apartment } from "@/models/Apartment";
import { Charge } from "@/models/Charge";
import { Payment } from "@/models/Payment";
import { Expense } from "@/models/Expense";
import { Incident } from "@/models/Incident";
import { Announcement } from "@/models/Announcement";

/**
 * GET /api/dashboard
 * Endpoint unique retournant les mÃ©triques et chiffres adaptÃ©s selon le rÃ´le connectÃ© :
 * - ADMIN / SYNDIC : KPI (taux recouvrement, trÃ©sorerie, incidents ouverts, impayÃ©s) + graphiques
 * - RESIDENT : Ce que je dois, derniÃ¨res annonces, mes incidents
 * - TECHNICIAN : Mes interventions (assignÃ©es, en cours, rÃ©solues)
 */
export const GET = withAuth(
  async (_req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser) => {
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. SYNDIC & ADMIN
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    if (user.role === Role.ADMIN || user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
      let residenceFilter: any = {};
      let residenceIds: string[] = [];

      if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
        residenceIds = await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id);
        residenceFilter = { residence: { $in: residenceIds } };
      }

      // Lots de la copropriÃ©tÃ©
      const aptIds = await Apartment.find(residenceFilter).distinct("_id");
      const currentMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);

      // Charges du mois et taux de recouvrement
      const monthCharges = await Charge.find({
        apartment: { $in: aptIds },
        period: currentMonth,
      }).lean();

      let totalMonthAmount = 0;
      let totalMonthPaid = 0;
      monthCharges.forEach((c) => {
        totalMonthAmount += c.amount || 0;
        totalMonthPaid += c.paidAmount || 0;
      });

      const collectionRate =
        totalMonthAmount > 0
          ? Math.round((totalMonthPaid / totalMonthAmount) * 100)
          : 100;

      // TrÃ©sorerie = Recettes encaissÃ©es - DÃ©penses
      const chargeIds = await Charge.find({ apartment: { $in: aptIds } }).distinct("_id");
      const [paymentSum] = await Payment.aggregate<{ total: number }>([
        { $match: { charge: { $in: chargeIds } } },
        { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: "$amount" } } },
      ]);
      const [expenseSum] = await Expense.aggregate<{ total: number }>([
        { $match: residenceFilter },
        { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: "$amount" } } },
      ]);

      const totalPayments = paymentSum?.total || 0;
      const totalExpenses = expenseSum?.total || 0;
      const treasury = Math.round((totalPayments - totalExpenses) * 100) / 100;

      // Incidents ouverts
      const incFilter: any = {
        status: { $in: [IncidentStatus.REPORTED, IncidentStatus.ASSIGNED, IncidentStatus.IN_PROGRESS] },
      };
      if (residenceIds.length > 0) incFilter.residence = { $in: residenceIds };
      const openIncidents = await Incident.countDocuments(incFilter);

      // ImpayÃ©s globaux
      const unpaidCharges = await Charge.find({
        apartment: { $in: aptIds },
        status: { $in: [ChargeStatus.PENDING, ChargeStatus.PARTIAL, ChargeStatus.OVERDUE] },
      }).lean();
      const unpaidAmount = Math.round(
        unpaidCharges.reduce(
          (acc: number, c: any) => acc + Math.max(0, (c.amount || 0) - (c.paidAmount || 0)),
          0
        ) * 100
      ) / 100;

      // Graphique 1 : Recettes vs DÃ©penses par mois (6 derniers mois)
      const months: string[] = [];
      const now = new Date();
      for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {
        const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - i, 1);
        months.push(d.toISOString().slice(0, 7));
      }

      // AgrÃ©gation dÃ©penses par mois
      const expensesByMonth = await Expense.aggregate<{ _id: string; total: number }>([
        { $match: residenceFilter },
        {
          $group: {
            _id: { $substr: [{ $dateToString: { date: "$expenseDate" } }, 0, 7] },
            total: { $sum: "$amount" },
          },
        },
      ]);
      const expMap = new Map(expensesByMonth.map((e) => [e._id, e.total]));

      // AgrÃ©gation paiements par mois
      const paymentsByMonth = await Payment.aggregate<{ _id: string; total: number }>([
        { $match: { charge: { $in: chargeIds } } },
        {
          $group: {
            _id: { $substr: [{ $dateToString: { date: "$paymentDate" } }, 0, 7] },
            total: { $sum: "$amount" },
          },
        },
      ]);
      const payMap = new Map(paymentsByMonth.map((p) => [p._id, p.total]));

      const financialByMonth = months.map((m) => {
        const dateObj = new Date(m + "-01");
        const monthLabel = dateObj.toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", { month: "short" });
        return {
          month: monthLabel,
          period: m,
          recettes: Math.round((payMap.get(m) || 0) * 100) / 100,
          depenses: Math.round((expMap.get(m) || 0) * 100) / 100,
        };
      });

      // Graphique 2 : Incidents par statut
      const incAggregation = await Incident.aggregate<{ _id: string; count: number }>([
        { $match: residenceIds.length > 0 ? { residence: { $in: residenceIds } } : {} },
        { $group: { _id: "$status", count: { $sum: 1 } } },
      ]);
      const statusMap = new Map(incAggregation.map((i) => [i._id, i.count]));

      const incidentsByStatus = [
        { status: "REPORTED", label: "SignalÃ©", count: statusMap.get(IncidentStatus.REPORTED) || 0, color: "#38bdf8" },
        { status: "ASSIGNED", label: "AssignÃ©", count: statusMap.get(IncidentStatus.ASSIGNED) || 0, color: "#f59e0b" },
        { status: "IN_PROGRESS", label: "En cours", count: statusMap.get(IncidentStatus.IN_PROGRESS) || 0, color: "#6366f1" },
        { status: "RESOLVED", label: "RÃ©solu", count: statusMap.get(IncidentStatus.RESOLVED) || 0, color: "#10b981" },
      ];

      return json({
        role: user.role,
        kpi: {
          period: currentMonth,
          collectionRate,
          treasury,
          openIncidents,
          unpaidAmount,
          totalApartments: aptIds.length,
        },
        charts: {
          financialByMonth,
          incidentsByStatus,
        },
      });
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. RESIDENT
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    if (user.role === Role.RESIDENT) {
      const aptIds = await getResidentApartmentIds(user.id);
      const resIds = await getResidentResidenceIds(user.id);

      // Ce que je dois
      const unpaidCharges = await Charge.find({
        apartment: { $in: aptIds },
        status: { $in: [ChargeStatus.PENDING, ChargeStatus.PARTIAL, ChargeStatus.OVERDUE] },
      })
        .populate("apartment", "number floor")
        .sort({ dueDate: 1 })
        .lean();

      const totalDue = Math.round(
        unpaidCharges.reduce(
          (acc: number, c: any) => acc + Math.max(0, (c.amount || 0) - (c.paidAmount || 0)),
          0
        ) * 100
      ) / 100;


      // DerniÃ¨res annonces
      const announcements = await Announcement.find({
        residence: { $in: resIds },
      })
        .populate("author", "name")
        .sort({ isPinned: -1, publishedAt: -1 })
        .limit(5)
        .lean();

      // Mes incidents signalÃ©s
      const incidents = await Incident.find({
        reportedBy: user.id,
      })
        .sort({ reportedAt: -1 })
        .limit(5)
        .lean();

      const totalIncidents = await Incident.countDocuments({ reportedBy: user.id });
      const openIncidents = await Incident.countDocuments({
        reportedBy: user.id,
        status: { $in: [IncidentStatus.REPORTED, IncidentStatus.ASSIGNED, IncidentStatus.IN_PROGRESS] },
      });

      return json({
        role: user.role,
        whatIOwe: {
          totalDue,
          unpaidCharges,
        },
        latestAnnouncements: announcements,
        myIncidents: {
          items: incidents,
          total: totalIncidents,
          open: openIncidents,
        },
      });
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. TECHNICIAN
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    if (user.role === Role.TECHNICIAN) {
      const interventions = await Incident.find({
        assignedTo: user.id,
      })
        .populate("residence", "name city address")
        .populate("apartment", "number floor")
        .sort({ reportedAt: -1 })
        .lean();

      const pendingInterventions = interventions.filter(
        (i) => i.status !== IncidentStatus.RESOLVED

      const resolvedInterventions = interventions
        .filter((i) => i.status === IncidentStatus.RESOLVED)
        .slice(0, 5);

      const assignedCount = interventions.filter(
        (i) => i.status === IncidentStatus.ASSIGNED
      ).length;
      const inProgressCount = interventions.filter(
        (i) => i.status === IncidentStatus.IN_PROGRESS
      ).length;
      const resolvedCount = interventions.filter(
        (i) => i.status === IncidentStatus.RESOLVED
      ).length;

      return json({
        role: user.role,
        myInterventions: {
          pending: pendingInterventions,
          recentResolved: resolvedInterventions,
          counts: {
            assigned: assignedCount,
            inProgress: inProgressCount,
            resolved: resolvedCount,
            total: interventions.length,
          },
        },
      });
    }

    return json({ role: user.role, message: "Aucune donnÃ©e spÃ©cifique pour ce rÃ´le" });
  },
  { roles: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC, Role.RESIDENT, Role.TECHNICIAN] }

import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { writeFile, mkdir } from "fs/promises";
import path from "path";
import { HttpError, json } from "@/lib/api";
import { withAuth, type AuthUser } from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // 5 Mo
const ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES = new Set([
  "application/pdf",
  "image/jpeg",
  "image/png",
  "image/webp",
]);

const EXTENSIONS: Record<string, string> = {
  "application/pdf": ".pdf",
  "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
  "image/png": ".png",
  "image/webp": ".webp",
};

/**
 * POST /api/documents/upload
 * TÃ©lÃ©versement simple d'un fichier (PDF ou image, max 5 Mo).
 * StockÃ© dans public/uploads/ avec validation de type et de taille cÃ´tÃ© serveur.
 */
export const POST = withAuth(
  async (req: NextRequest, _user: AuthUser) => {
    const contentType = req.headers.get("content-type") || "";

    let buffer: Buffer;
    let mimeType: string;
    let originalName = "document";

    if (contentType.includes("multipart/form-data")) {
      const formData = await req.formData();
      const file = formData.get("file");

      if (!file || typeof file === "string") {
        throw new HttpError(400, "Fichier manquant dans le champ 'file'");
      }

      const blob = file as File;
      mimeType = blob.type || "application/octet-stream";
      originalName = blob.name || "document";

      if (blob.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
        throw new HttpError(400, "Le fichier dÃ©passe la taille maximale autorisÃ©e (5 Mo)");
      }

      const arrayBuffer = await blob.arrayBuffer();
      buffer = Buffer.from(arrayBuffer);
    } else if (contentType.includes("application/json")) {
      const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
      const base64Data = body.base64 || body.file;
      mimeType = body.mimeType || "";
      originalName = body.filename || "document";

      if (!base64Data || typeof base64Data !== "string") {
        throw new HttpError(400, "Contenu base64 manquant");
      }

      // Extraction du data URL si prÃ©sent : data:image/png;base64,xxxx
      const match = base64Data.match(/^data:([^;]+);base64,(.+)$/);
      let rawBase64 = base64Data;
      if (match) {
        mimeType = mimeType || match[1];
        rawBase64 = match[2];
      }

      buffer = Buffer.from(rawBase64, "base64");
      if (buffer.length > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
        throw new HttpError(400, "Le fichier dÃ©passe la taille maximale autorisÃ©e (5 Mo)");
      }
    } else {
      throw new HttpError(400, "Format de requÃªte non supportÃ© (attendu: multipart/form-data ou JSON)");
    }

    if (!ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
      throw new HttpError(
        400,
        `Type de fichier non autorisÃ© (${mimeType}). Formats acceptÃ©s : PDF, JPEG, PNG, WEBP.`
      );
    }

    // RÃ©pertoire cible public/uploads
    const uploadsDir = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", "uploads");
    await mkdir(uploadsDir, { recursive: true });

    const ext = EXTENSIONS[mimeType] || path.extname(originalName) || ".bin";
    const filename = `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}${ext}`;
    const filePath = path.join(uploadsDir, filename);

    await writeFile(filePath, buffer);

    const fileUrl = `/uploads/${filename}`;

    return json(
      {
        fileUrl,
        filename: originalName,
        size: buffer.length,
        mimeType,
      },
      201
    );
  },
  { roles: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC] }
);

import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { json } from "@/lib/api";
import { withAuth, type AuthUser } from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import { equipmentScope } from "@/lib/maintenance/service";
import { Equipment } from "@/models/Equipment";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

/**
 * Vue simplifiÃ©e Â« Ã©tat seulement Â» pour les rÃ©sidents (et autres rÃ´les).
 * N'expose aucun champ sensible (numÃ©ro de sÃ©rie, prestataire, notes, garantie).
 */
export const GET = withAuth(
  async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser) => {
    const { searchParams } = req.nextUrl;

    const rawPage = parseInt(searchParams.get("page") || "1", 10);
    const rawLimit = parseInt(searchParams.get("limit") || "50", 10);
    const page = Number.isNaN(rawPage) || rawPage < 1 ? 1 : rawPage;
    const limit =
      Number.isNaN(rawLimit) || rawLimit < 1 ? 50 : Math.min(rawLimit, 50);
    const skip = (page - 1) * limit;

    const conditions: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
    const scoped = await equipmentScope(user);
    if (scoped && Object.keys(scoped).length > 0) {
      conditions.push(scoped);
    }

    for (const key of ["residence", "status", "category"] as const) {
      const val = searchParams.get(key)?.trim();
      if (val) {
        conditions.push({ [key]: val });
      }
    }

    const filtre =
      conditions.length > 1 ? { $and: conditions } : conditions[0] || {};

    const [data, total] = await Promise.all([
      Equipment.find(filtre)
        .select("residence name category location status")
        .sort({ name: 1 })
        .skip(skip)
        .limit(limit)
        .lean(),
      Equipment.countDocuments(filtre),
    ]);

    return json({ data, meta: { page, limit, total } });
  },
  { roles: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC, Role.RESIDENT, Role.TECHNICIAN] }
);

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import {
  withAuth,
  getResidentApartmentIds,
  getSyndicResidenceIds,
  getResidentResidenceIds,
  type AuthUser,
} from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import { json } from "@/lib/api";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

// Types d'entitÃ©s pris en charge pour la recherche globale
const ALL_TYPES = [
  "incident",
  "announcement",
  "charge",
  "apartment",
  "resident",
  "document",
  "meeting",
] as const;

type SearchType = (typeof ALL_TYPES)[number];

export interface SearchResult {
  type: SearchType;
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  badge?: string;
  href: string;
}

// Formate une date au format JJ/MM/AAAA
function formatDateFR(dateValue: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (!dateValue) return undefined;
  const d = new Date(dateValue as string | number | Date);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return undefined;
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const year = d.getFullYear();
  return `${day}/${month}/${year}`;
}

export const GET = withAuth(
  async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser) => {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const rawQ = (searchParams.get("q") || "").trim();

    // Minimum 2 caractÃ¨res requis aprÃ¨s trim
    if (rawQ.length < 2) {
      return json({ data: [] });
    }

    // Filtrage des types demandÃ©s (dÃ©faut = tous)
    const typesParam = searchParams.get("types");
    const requestedTypes: SearchType[] = typesParam
      ? (typesParam
          .split(",")
          .map((t) => t.trim().toLowerCase())
          .filter((t): t is SearchType =>
            (ALL_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(t)
          ) as SearchType[])
      : [...ALL_TYPES];

    if (requestedTypes.length === 0) {
      return json({ data: [] });
    }

    // Ã‰chappement des caractÃ¨res spÃ©ciaux regex et recherche insensible Ã  la casse
    const escaped = rawQ.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
    const regex = new RegExp(escaped, "i");

    // PrÃ©paration des pÃ©rimÃ¨tres par rÃ´le
    let syndicResIds: unknown[] = [];
    let syndicAptIds: unknown[] = [];
    let residentAptIds: unknown[] = [];
    let residentResIds: unknown[] = [];
    let techResIds: unknown[] = [];

    if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
      syndicResIds = await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id);
      if (
        requestedTypes.includes("charge") ||
        requestedTypes.includes("resident")
      ) {
        syndicAptIds = await mongoose
          .model("Apartment")
          .find({ residence: { $in: syndicResIds } })
          .distinct("_id");
      }
    } else if (user.role === Role.RESIDENT) {
      const needsApt =
        requestedTypes.includes("charge") ||
        requestedTypes.includes("apartment");
      const needsRes =
        requestedTypes.includes("announcement") ||
        requestedTypes.includes("document") ||
        requestedTypes.includes("meeting");

      const [aptIds, resIds] = await Promise.all([
        needsApt ? getResidentApartmentIds(user.id) : Promise.resolve([]),
        needsRes ? getResidentResidenceIds(user.id) : Promise.resolve([]),
      ]);
      residentAptIds = aptIds;
      residentResIds = resIds;
    } else if (user.role === Role.TECHNICIAN) {
      if (requestedTypes.includes("announcement")) {
        techResIds = await mongoose
          .model("Incident")
          .find({ assignedTo: user.id })
          .distinct("residence");
      }
    }

    // Construit le filtre de pÃ©rimÃ¨tre pour un type donnÃ© (null = accÃ¨s interdit)
    const getScopeFilter = (
      type: SearchType
    ): Record<string, unknown> | null => {
      switch (user.role) {
        case Role.ADMIN:
          return {};

        case Role.SYNDIC:
          if (type === "charge" || type === "resident") {
            return { apartment: { $in: syndicAptIds } };
          }
          return { residence: { $in: syndicResIds } };

        case Role.RESIDENT:
          switch (type) {
            case "incident":
              return { reportedBy: user.id };
            case "charge":
              return { apartment: { $in: residentAptIds } };
            case "apartment":
              return { _id: { $in: residentAptIds } };
            case "announcement":
            case "document":
            case "meeting":
              return { residence: { $in: residentResIds } };
            case "resident":
              return null; // Interdit aux rÃ©sidents
          }
          break;

        case Role.TECHNICIAN:
          if (type === "incident") {
            return { assignedTo: user.id };
          }
          if (type === "announcement") {
            return { residence: { $in: techResIds } };
          }
          return null; // Autres types interdits au technicien
      }
      return null;
    };

    // Combine le filtre texte et le filtre de pÃ©rimÃ¨tre avec $and
    const buildQuery = (
      textFilter: Record<string, unknown>,
      scopeFilter: Record<string, unknown>
    ) => {
      if (Object.keys(scopeFilter).length === 0) {
        return textFilter;
      }
      return { $and: [textFilter, scopeFilter] };
    };

    // ExÃ©cute la recherche pour un type donnÃ© avec gestion d'erreur isolÃ©e
    const searchByType = async (type: SearchType): Promise<SearchResult[]> => {
      try {
        const scope = getScopeFilter(type);
        if (scope === null) return [];

        switch (type) {
          case "incident": {
            const textFilter = {
              $or: [{ title: regex }, { description: regex }],
            };
            const docs = (await mongoose
              .model("Incident")
              .find(buildQuery(textFilter, scope))
              .select("title category status")
              .limit(5)
              .lean()) as Array<{
              _id: unknown;
              title?: string;
              category?: string;
              status?: string;
            }>;

            return docs.map((d) => {
              const title = d.title || "";
              return {
                type: "incident",
                id: String(d._id),
                title,
                subtitle: d.category,
                badge: d.status,
                href: `/incidents?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`,
              };
            });
          }

          case "announcement": {
            const textFilter = {
              $or: [{ title: regex }, { content: regex }],
            };
            const docs = (await mongoose
              .model("Announcement")
              .find(buildQuery(textFilter, scope))
              .select("title content")
              .limit(5)
              .lean()) as Array<{
              _id: unknown;
              title?: string;
              content?: string;
            }>;

            return docs.map((d) => {
              const title = d.title || "";
              return {
                type: "announcement",
                id: String(d._id),
                title,
                subtitle: d.content ? d.content.slice(0, 80) : undefined,
                href: `/announcements?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`,
              };
            });
          }

          case "charge": {
            const textFilter = { period: regex };
            const docs = (await mongoose
              .model("Charge")
              .find(buildQuery(textFilter, scope))
              .select("period status apartment")
              .populate("apartment", "number")
              .limit(5)
              .lean()) as Array<{
              _id: unknown;
              period?: string;
              status?: string;
              apartment?: { number?: string } | null;
            }>;

            return docs.map((d) => {
              const period = d.period || "";
              const aptNumber = d.apartment?.number || "";
              return {
                type: "charge",
                id: String(d._id),
                title: `Charge ${period}`,
                subtitle: aptNumber ? `Appartement ${aptNumber}` : undefined,
                badge: d.status,
                href: `/charges?q=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`,
              };
            });
          }

          case "apartment": {
            const textFilter = { number: regex };
            const docs = (await mongoose
              .model("Apartment")
              .find(buildQuery(textFilter, scope))
              .select("number floor")
              .limit(5)
              .lean()) as Array<{
              _id: unknown;
              number?: string;
              floor?: number | string;
            }>;

            return docs.map((d) => {
              const number = d.number || "";
              return {
                type: "apartment",
                id: String(d._id),
                title: `Appartement ${number}`,
                subtitle:
                  d.floor !== undefined && d.floor !== null
                    ? `Ã‰tage ${d.floor}`
                    : undefined,
                href: `/apartments?q=${encodeURIComponent(number)}`,
              };
            });
          }

          case "resident": {
            const textFilter = {
              $or: [{ fullName: regex }, { phone: regex }, { email: regex }],
            };
            const docs = (await mongoose
              .model("Resident")
              .find(buildQuery(textFilter, scope))
              .select("fullName email phone")
              .limit(5)
              .lean()) as Array<{
              _id: unknown;
              fullName?: string;
              email?: string;
              phone?: string;
            }>;

            return docs.map((d) => {
              const fullName = d.fullName || "";
              return {
                type: "resident",
                id: String(d._id),
                title: fullName,
                subtitle: d.email || d.phone || undefined,
                href: `/residents?q=${encodeURIComponent(fullName)}`,
              };
            });
          }

          case "document": {
            const textFilter = { title: regex };
            const docs = (await mongoose
              .model("Document")
              .find(buildQuery(textFilter, scope))
              .select("title type")
              .limit(5)
              .lean()) as Array<{
              _id: unknown;
              title?: string;
              type?: string;
            }>;

            return docs.map((d) => {
              const title = d.title || "";
              return {
                type: "document",
                id: String(d._id),
                title,
                badge: d.type,
                href: `/documents?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`,
              };
            });
          }

          case "meeting": {
            const textFilter = { title: regex };
            const docs = (await mongoose
              .model("Meeting")
              .find(buildQuery(textFilter, scope))
              .select("title date")
              .limit(5)
              .lean()) as Array<{
              _id: unknown;
              title?: string;
              date?: Date | string;
            }>;

            return docs.map((d) => {
              const title = d.title || "";
              return {
                type: "meeting",
                id: String(d._id),
                title,
                subtitle: formatDateFR(d.date),
                href: `/meetings?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`,
              };
            });
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // Une erreur sur un type ne bloque pas les autres rÃ©sultats
        return [];
      }
    };

    // ExÃ©cution en parallÃ¨le et limitation Ã  25 rÃ©sultats au total
    const resultsPerType = await Promise.all(
      requestedTypes.map((type) => searchByType(type))
    );
    const data = resultsPerType.flat().slice(0, 25);

    return json({ data });
  },
  {
    roles: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC, Role.RESIDENT, Role.TECHNICIAN],
  }
);

import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { HttpError, isDuplicateKeyError, json, parseBody } from "@/lib/api";
import { User } from "@/models/User";
import { userCreateSchema } from "@/lib/validations/user";
import { hashPassword } from "@/lib/password";
import { withAuth, type AuthUser } from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

/**
 * GET /api/users
 * RÃ©servÃ© aux rÃ´les ADMIN et SYNDIC.
 * Supporte le filtrage par rÃ´le, la recherche textuelle (q) et la pagination { data, meta }.
 */
export const GET = withAuth(
  async (req: NextRequest) => {
    const { searchParams } = req.nextUrl;
    const roleParam = searchParams.get("role");
    const q = searchParams.get("q");
    const page = Math.max(1, parseInt(searchParams.get("page") || "1", 10));
    const limit = Math.min(200, Math.max(1, parseInt(searchParams.get("limit") || "20", 10)));

    const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    if (roleParam) {
      filter.role = roleParam;
    }
    if (q) {
      filter.$or = [
        { name: { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
        { email: { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
      ];
    }

    const [users, total] = await Promise.all([
      User.find(filter)
        .select("-passwordHash")
        .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
        .skip((page - 1) * limit)
        .limit(limit)
        .lean(),
      User.countDocuments(filter),
    ]);

    return json({
      data: users,
      meta: {
        page,
        limit,
        total,
      },
    });
  },
  { roles: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC] }
);


/**
 * POST /api/users
 * RÃ©servÃ© aux rÃ´les ADMIN et SYNDIC.
 * RÃ¨gle : Seul un ADMIN peut crÃ©er un utilisateur avec le rÃ´le ADMIN ou SYNDIC.
 */
export const POST = withAuth(
  async (req: NextRequest, currentUser: AuthUser) => {
    const data = await parseBody(req, userCreateSchema);

    // Seul un ADMIN a le droit de nommer un ADMIN ou un SYNDIC
    if (currentUser.role !== Role.ADMIN) {
      if (data.role === Role.ADMIN || data.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
        throw new HttpError(403, "Seul un administrateur peut crÃ©er un compte ADMIN ou SYNDIC");
      }
    }

    const normalizedEmail = data.email.toLowerCase().trim();
    const existing = await User.findOne({ email: normalizedEmail }).lean();
    if (existing) {
      throw new HttpError(409, "Un compte avec cette adresse email existe dÃ©jÃ ");
    }

    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(data.password);

    try {
      const created = await User.create({
        name: data.name,
        email: normalizedEmail,
        passwordHash,
        phone: data.phone,
        role: data.role,
        isActive: data.isActive ?? true,
      });

      return json(
        {
          _id: created._id.toString(),
          name: created.name,
          email: created.email,
          phone: created.phone,
          role: created.role,
          isActive: created.isActive,
          createdAt: created.createdAt,
        },
        201
      );
    } catch (err) {
      if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
        throw new HttpError(409, "Un compte avec cette adresse email existe dÃ©jÃ ");
      }
      throw err;
    }
  },
  { roles: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC] }
);

import { auth } from "@/auth";
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";
import { SwaggerViewer } from "@/components/docs/SwaggerViewer";

import { connection } from "next/server";

export const instant = false;

export default async function ApiDocsPage() {
  await connection();
  // En production, dÃ©sactiver si ENABLE_API_DOCS n'est pas "true"
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" && process.env.ENABLE_API_DOCS !== "true") {
    notFound();
  }

  const session = await auth();

  // Non connectÃ© : redirection vers login
  if (!session?.user?.id) {
    redirect("/login?callbackUrl=/api-docs");
  }

  // Seuls les rÃ´les ADMIN et SYNDIC sont autorisÃ©s Ã  consulter la documentation API
  if (session.user.role !== Role.ADMIN && session.user.role !== Role.SYNDIC) {
    redirect("/forbidden");
  }

  return <SwaggerViewer user={session.user} />;
}

import { ResourcePage } from "@/components/crud/ResourcePage";
import { chargeConfig } from "@/lib/resources/registry";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";

export default function ChargesPage() {
  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<Role | undefined>(undefined);
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [residences, setResidences] = useState<Array<{ label: string; value: string }>>([]);
  const [selectedResidence, setSelectedResidence] = useState("");
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState("");
  const [totalBudget, setTotalBudget] = useState<number | "">("");
  const [dueDate, setDueDate] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    setPeriod(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
    setDueDate(new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]);

    fetch("/api/auth/session")
      .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.user?.role) setUserRole(data.user.role as Role);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        getOfflineSession().then((offline) => {
          if (offline) setUserRole(offline.role);
        });
      });

    // Chargement des rÃ©sidences
    fetchWithOffline<any>("/api/residences")
      .then((res) => {
        const items = Array.isArray(res?.data) ? res.data : Array.isArray(res) ? res : [];
        const opts = items.map((r: any) => ({
          label: r.name,
          value: r._id,
        }));
        setResidences(opts);
        if (opts.length > 0) setSelectedResidence(opts[0].value);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const handleGenerate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedResidence || !totalBudget || Number(totalBudget) <= 0) {
      setErrorMessage("Veuillez sÃ©lectionner une copropriÃ©tÃ© et saisir un budget valide.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setErrorMessage(null);

      const res = await fetchWithOffline<any>("/api/charges/generate", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          residenceId: selectedResidence,
          period,
          totalBudget: Number(totalBudget),
          dueDate,
        }),
      });

      setSuccessMessage(
        res?.message || `Charges du mois ${period} gÃ©nÃ©rÃ©es avec succÃ¨s au prorata des tantiÃ¨mes !`
      );
      setIsModalOpen(false);
      setRefreshTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 5000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMessage(err.message || "Erreur lors de la gÃ©nÃ©ration des charges");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const isStaff = userRole === Role.ADMIN || userRole === Role.SYNDIC;

    <>
      <ResourcePage
        key={refreshTrigger}
        config={chargeConfig}
        headerExtra={() => (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {/* Message de succÃ¨s */}
            {successMessage && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-50/80 p-3.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 dark:text-emerald-300">
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                <span>{successMessage}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Bouton d'action GÃ©nÃ©rer rÃ©servÃ© aux ADMIN / SYNDIC */}
            {isStaff && (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-sky-500/20 bg-sky-50/50 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between dark:border-sky-500/20 dark:bg-sky-950/20">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-sky-950 dark:text-sky-200">
                    Appel de charges groupÃ©
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-sky-700/80 dark:text-sky-400">
                    GÃ©nÃ©rez en un clic les charges pour tous les lots d&apos;une copropriÃ©tÃ© au
                    prorata des tantiÃ¨mes.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="primary"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setErrorMessage(null);
                    setIsModalOpen(true);
                  }}
                  className="min-h-[44px] gap-2 whitespace-nowrap text-xs sm:min-h-[36px]"
                >
                  <Calculator className="h-4 w-4" />
                  GÃ©nÃ©rer les charges du mois
                </Button>

          </div>
        )}
      />

export default function DocumentsPage() {
  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<Role | undefined>(undefined);
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [residences, setResidences] = useState<Array<{ label: string; value: string }>>([]);
  const [selectedResidence, setSelectedResidence] = useState("");
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [docType, setDocType] = useState("OTHER");
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState(0);

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/session")
      .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.user?.role) setUserRole(data.user.role as Role);
      })

function getProp(obj: any, key: string): any {
  if (!obj) return undefined;
  if (typeof obj === "object") return obj[key];
  return undefined;
}

function formatFullDate(dateVal?: string | Date): string {
  if (!dateVal) return "â€”";
  try {
    const d = new Date(dateVal);
    const datePart = d.toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", {
      day: "numeric",
      month: "short",
      year: "numeric",
    });
    const timePart = d.toLocaleTimeString("fr-FR", {
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
    });
    return `${datePart} Ã  ${timePart}`;
  } catch {
    return "â€”";
  }
}

function formatRelativeTime(dateVal?: string | Date): string {
  if (!dateVal) return "";
  try {
    const diffMs = Date.now() - new Date(dateVal).getTime();
    if (diffMs < 0) return "Ã  l'instant";
    const minutes = Math.floor(diffMs / 60000);
    if (minutes < 1) return "Ã  l'instant";
    if (minutes < 60) return `il y a ${minutes} min`;
    const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
    if (hours < 24) return `il y a ${hours} h`;
    const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
    if (days < 30) return `il y a ${days} j`;
    const months = Math.floor(days / 30);
    if (months < 12) return `il y a ${months} mois`;
    const years = Math.floor(months / 12);
    return `il y a ${years} an${years > 1 ? "s" : ""}`;
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

function IncidentsContent() {

  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const submitLock = useRef(false);

    typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : String(Date.now())
  );

  const resetClientId = () => {
    clientIdRef.current =
      typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : String(Date.now());
  };

  const toggleExpandDescription = (id: string) => {
    setExpandedDescriptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const loadData = async () => {

      setLoading(true);

        fetchWithOffline<any>("/api/incidents"),

      setIncidents(incList);

      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[Incidents] Erreur chargement :", err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
    if (searchParams.get("action") === "new" || searchParams.get("new") === "1") {
      setIsModalOpen(true);
    }

    // RÃ©cupÃ©rer la session utilisateur courante
    fetch("/api/auth/session")
      .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
      .then((data) => {

  // Compression photo cÃ´tÃ© client via canvas (strictement <= 1 Mo)
  const handlePhotoUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const files = e.target.files;
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;

    const file = files[0];
    const reader = new FileReader();

    reader.onload = (event) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        const maxDim = 800;
        let width = img.width;
        let height = img.height;

        if (width > height && width > maxDim) {
          height = Math.round((height * maxDim) / width);
          width = maxDim;
        } else if (height > maxDim) {
          width = Math.round((width * maxDim) / height);
          height = maxDim;
        }

        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
        ctx?.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);

        // Compression adaptative assurant taille <= 1 Mo
        let quality = 0.75;
        let compressedDataUrl = canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", quality);
        while (compressedDataUrl.length > 1_000_000 && quality > 0.25) {
          quality -= 0.15;
          compressedDataUrl = canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", quality);
        }

        setPhotos((prev) => [...prev.slice(0, 2), compressedDataUrl]);
      };
      img.src = event.target?.result as string;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handleCreateIncident = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (submitLock.current) return;
    submitLock.current = true;

    if (!residenceId) {
      setErrorMessage("Veuillez sÃ©lectionner une rÃ©sidence.");
      submitLock.current = false;

  // Progression de statut stricte : REPORTED -> ASSIGNED -> IN_PROGRESS -> RESOLVED
  const handleAdvanceStatus = async (incidentId: string, nextStatus: IncidentStatus) => {

      });
      const data = await res.json();

      }
      setIncidents((prev) =>

      );

    } finally {

  };

    } finally {
      setIsActionLoading(null);
    }
  };

  const filteredIncidents = useMemo(() => {
    return incidents.filter((inc) => {
      const matchStatus =
        statusFilter === "ALL" || inc.status === statusFilter;
      const matchCat =
        categoryFilter === "ALL" || inc.category === categoryFilter;
      const matchSearch =
        !search ||
        inc.title?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        inc.description?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
      return matchStatus && matchCat && matchSearch;
    });
  }, [incidents, statusFilter, categoryFilter, search]);

  const canAssign =
    currentUser?.role === Role.ADMIN || currentUser?.role === Role.SYNDIC;

  const isFilterActive = statusFilter !== "ALL" || categoryFilter !== "ALL" || search.trim() !== "";

    <AppLayout>

        {/* En-tÃªte */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">

              Incidents & Pannes

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 sm:text-sm dark:text-slate-400">
              DÃ©clarez et suivez la rÃ©solution des pannes mÃªme sans connexion internet
            </p>
          </div>
          <Button

          </div>

            >
              <option value="ALL">Toutes catÃ©gories</option>
              <option value="PLUMBING">Plomberie</option>
              <option value="ELECTRICITY">Ã‰lectricitÃ©</option>
              <option value="ELEVATOR">Ascenseur</option>

                              />

                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Technicien assignÃ© */}
                    <div className="text-xs">
                      {techName ? (

                    {/* Mini barre de progression en 4 Ã©tapes */}
                    <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        {WORKFLOW_STEPS.map((step, idx) => {
                          const isPassed = idx <= currentStepIndex;
                          const isCurrent = idx === currentStepIndex;
                          return (
                            <span
                              key={step.key}
                              className={
                                isCurrent
                                  ? "text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400 font-bold"
                                  : isPassed

                        {WORKFLOW_STEPS.map((step, idx) => {

                          if (isCurrent) {

            typeof inc.assignedTo === "object" ? assignedToObj?._id : inc.assignedTo;
          const isAssignedTech =

            assignedToId === currentUser.id;

            inc.status === IncidentStatus.ASSIGNED && (isAssignedTech || canAssign);

"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { HandCoins, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { ResourcePage } from "@/components/crud/ResourcePage";
import { paymentConfig } from "@/lib/resources/registry";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/Input";
import { Select } from "@/components/ui/Select";
import { BottomSheet } from "@/components/ui/BottomSheet";
import { fetchWithOffline } from "@/lib/offline/apiClient";
import { Role, PaymentMethod } from "@/types/enums";
import { getOfflineSession } from "@/lib/offline/db";

export default function PaymentsPage() {
  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<Role | undefined>(undefined);
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [charges, setCharges] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedChargeId, setSelectedChargeId] = useState("");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState<number | "">("");
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<PaymentMethod>(PaymentMethod.BANK_TRANSFER);
  const [paymentDate, setPaymentDate] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    setPaymentDate(new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]);

    fetch("/api/auth/session")
      .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.user?.role) setUserRole(data.user.role as Role);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        getOfflineSession().then((offline) => {
          if (offline) setUserRole(offline.role);
        });
      });

    // Chargement des charges ouvertes pour sÃ©lection dans l'encaissement
    fetchWithOffline<any>("/api/charges")
      .then((res) => {
        const items = Array.isArray(res?.data) ? res.data : Array.isArray(res) ? res : [];
        setCharges(items);
        if (items.length > 0) {
          const first = items[0];
          setSelectedChargeId(first._id);
          const rem = Math.max(0, (first.amount || 0) - (first.paidAmount || 0));
          setAmount(rem > 0 ? rem : first.amount);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const selectedCharge = charges.find((c) => c._id === selectedChargeId);
  const remaining = selectedCharge
    ? Math.max(0, (selectedCharge.amount || 0) - (selectedCharge.paidAmount || 0))
    : 0;

  const handleChargeChange = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedChargeId(id);
    const c = charges.find((item) => item._id === id);
    if (c) {
      const rem = Math.max(0, (c.amount || 0) - (c.paidAmount || 0));
      setAmount(rem > 0 ? rem : c.amount);
    }
  };

  const handleCollectPayment = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedChargeId || !amount || Number(amount) <= 0) {
      setErrorMessage("Veuillez sÃ©lectionner une charge et saisir un montant valide.");
      return;
    }

    if (Number(amount) > remaining + 0.001) {
      setErrorMessage(
        `Le montant saisi (${Number(amount).toFixed(2)} â‚¬) dÃ©passe le reste dÃ» (${remaining.toFixed(2)} â‚¬).`
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setErrorMessage(null);

      const clientId =
        typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID
          ? crypto.randomUUID()
          : `pay-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`;

      await fetchWithOffline("/api/payments", {
        method: "POST",
        entity: "payment",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          charge: selectedChargeId,
          amount: Number(amount),
          method,
          paymentDate,
          clientId,
        }),
      });

      setSuccessMessage(
        "RÃ¨glement enregistrÃ© avec succÃ¨s ! ReÃ§u gÃ©nÃ©rÃ© et charge mise Ã  jour (fonctionne hors ligne)."
      );
      setIsModalOpen(false);
      setRefreshTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 5000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMessage(err.message || "Erreur lors de l'enregistrement du rÃ¨glement");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const isStaff = userRole === Role.ADMIN || userRole === Role.SYNDIC;

  return (
    <>
      <ResourcePage
        key={refreshTrigger}
        config={paymentConfig}
        headerExtra={() => (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {/* Message de succÃ¨s */}
            {successMessage && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-50/80 p-3.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 dark:text-emerald-300">
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                <span>{successMessage}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Ã‰cran d'action Encaisser utilisable hors ligne */}
            {isStaff && (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-50/40 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between dark:border-emerald-500/20 dark:bg-emerald-950/20">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-200">
                    Encaissement de rÃ¨glement (Hors ligne garanti)
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-emerald-800/80 dark:text-emerald-300/80">
                    Enregistrez un paiement par chÃ¨que, virement ou espÃ¨ces avec reÃ§u automatique et
                    contrÃ´le du reste Ã  payer.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="primary"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setErrorMessage(null);
                    setIsModalOpen(true);
                  }}
                  className="min-h-[44px] gap-2 whitespace-nowrap bg-emerald-600 text-xs hover:bg-emerald-500 active:bg-emerald-700 sm:min-h-[36px]"
                >
                  <HandCoins className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Encaisser un rÃ¨glement
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      />

      {/* Modal / BottomSheet d'encaissement */}
      <BottomSheet
        isOpen={isModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
        title="Encaisser un rÃ¨glement"
        description="Enregistre la transaction avec identifiant idempotent, date et calcul automatique du solde."
      >
        <form onSubmit={handleCollectPayment} className="space-y-4 pt-2 pb-4">
          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 p-3 text-xs font-semibold text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{errorMessage}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* SÃ©lection de la charge */}
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
              Charge Ã  rÃ©gler <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <select
              value={selectedChargeId}
              onChange={(e) => handleChargeChange(e.target.value)}
              className="min-h-[44px] w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-sky-500 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white"
              disabled={loading}
            >
              {charges.map((c) => {
                const aptNum =
                  typeof c.apartment === "object" ? c.apartment?.number : "N/A";
                const rem = Math.max(0, (c.amount || 0) - (c.paidAmount || 0));
                return (
                  <option key={c._id} value={c._id}>
                    Lot {aptNum} â€¢ {c.period} (AppelÃ© : {c.amount} â‚¬ - Reste : {rem.toFixed(2)} â‚¬)
                  </option>
                );
              })}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* DÃ©tails de la charge sÃ©lectionnÃ©e */}
          {selectedCharge && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3.5 text-xs dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/60">
              <div className="flex justify-between py-0.5 text-slate-500">
                <span>Montant total :</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                  {(selectedCharge.amount || 0).toFixed(2)} â‚¬
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between py-0.5 text-slate-500">
                <span>DÃ©jÃ  rÃ©glÃ© :</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-600">
                  {(selectedCharge.paidAmount || 0).toFixed(2)} â‚¬
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-1 font-bold text-slate-900 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-white">
                <span>Reste Ã  payer :</span>
                <span className="text-rose-600">{remaining.toFixed(2)} â‚¬</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <Input
            label="Montant du versement (â‚¬)"
            type="number"
            required
            value={amount}
            step="0.01"
            min="0.01"
            max={remaining}
            onChange={(e) => setAmount(parseFloat(e.target.value) || "")}
            disabled={loading}
            helperText={`Maximum autorisÃ© : ${remaining.toFixed(2)} â‚¬`}
          />

          <Select
            label="Mode de rÃ¨glement"
            required
            value={method}
            onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value as PaymentMethod)}
            disabled={loading}
            options={[
              { label: "Virement bancaire", value: "BANK_TRANSFER" },
              { label: "ChÃ¨que", value: "CHEQUE" },
              { label: "EspÃ¨ces (Cash)", value: "CASH" },
              { label: "Carte bancaire", value: "CARD" },
            ]}
          />

          <Input
            label="Date d'encaissement"
            type="date"
            required
            value={paymentDate}
            onChange={(e) => setPaymentDate(e.target.value)}
            disabled={loading}
          />

          <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 pt-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
              disabled={loading}
              className="min-h-[44px]"

              type="submit"
              variant="primary"
              isLoading={loading}

  model: Announcement,
  createSchema: announcementCreateSchema,
  updateSchema: announcementUpdateSchema,
  access: {
    read: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC, Role.RESIDENT, Role.TECHNICIAN],
    create: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
    update: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
    delete: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
  },
  searchFields: ["title", "content"],
  defaultSort: { isPinned: -1, publishedAt: -1 },
  populate: [
    { path: "author", select: "name" },
    { path: "residence", select: "name city" },
  ],

    if (user.role === Role.ADMIN) {
      return {};

    // 2. SYNDIC : rÃ©sidences gÃ©rÃ©es
    if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
      const residenceIds = await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id);
      return { residence: { $in: residenceIds } };
    }
    // 3. RESIDENT : rÃ©sidences oÃ¹ le rÃ©sident possÃ¨de ou occupe un appartement
    if (user.role === Role.RESIDENT) {

        "residence"

    return {};
  },
  injectOnCreate: async (user) => {
    // RÃ¨gle SyndicPro : author provient toujours de la session sÃ©curisÃ©e
    return {
      author: user.id,
    };
  },

      return { residence: { $in: syndicResidences } };

      throw new HttpError(404, "RÃ©sidence introuvable");

      number: body.number,
    }).lean();

      );

  },

      throw new HttpError(
        409,
        "Impossible de supprimer cet appartement car des charges y sont associÃ©es"
      );

  },
