=== FILE: C:\atelier_nextJs\syndicpro\lib\crud\createCrudRoutes.ts ===
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import type { Model } from "mongoose";

type MongoFilter<T = any> = Parameters<Model<T>["find"]>[0] | Record<string, any>;
import { z } from "zod";
import { HttpError, isValidObjectId, json, parseBody } from "@/lib/api";
import { withAuth, type AuthUser } from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

export interface CrudRouteConfig<T = any> {
  model: Model<T>;
  createSchema: z.ZodType<any>;
  updateSchema: z.ZodType<any>;
  access: {
    read?: Role[];
    create?: Role[];
    update?: Role[];
    delete?: Role[];
  };
  scope?: (user: AuthUser, req: NextRequest) => MongoFilter<T> | Promise<MongoFilter<T>>;
  injectOnCreate?: (
    user: AuthUser,
    body: any,
    req: NextRequest
  ) => Record<string, any> | Promise<Record<string, any>>;
  beforeCreate?: (user: AuthUser, body: any, req: NextRequest) => Promise<void> | void;
  beforeUpdate?: (id: string, body: any, user: AuthUser, req: NextRequest) => Promise<void> | void;
  beforeDelete?: (id: string, user: AuthUser, req: NextRequest) => Promise<void> | void;
  afterCreate?: (doc: any, user: AuthUser, req: NextRequest) => Promise<void> | void;
  afterUpdate?: (doc: any, user: AuthUser, req: NextRequest) => Promise<void> | void;
  afterDelete?: (deletedDoc: any, user: AuthUser, req: NextRequest) => Promise<void> | void;
  afterList?: (data: any[], req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser) => Promise<any> | any;
  searchFields?: string[];
  defaultSort?: Record<string, 1 | -1> | string;
  populate?: any;
  filterFields?: string[];
  dateField?: string;
  exportColumns?: { key: string; label: string }[];
  exportPopulate?: any;
}

const DATE_PARAM_REGEX = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

/**
 * RÃ©sout une valeur imbriquÃ©e via un chemin pointÃ© (ex: "apartment.number").
 */
function resolveDotPath(obj: any, path: string): any {
  if (!obj || !path) return undefined;
  return path.split(".").reduce((acc, part) => (acc != null ? acc[part] : undefined), obj);
}

/**
 * Formate une valeur brute en chaÃ®ne pour une cellule CSV.
 */
function formatCsvValue(val: any): string {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) return "";
  if (val instanceof Date) {
    return isNaN(val.getTime()) ? "" : val.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  }
  if (typeof val === "boolean") {
    return val ? "Oui" : "Non";
  }
  if (Array.isArray(val)) {
    return val.map((item) => formatCsvValue(item)).join(", ");
  }
  if (typeof val === "object") {
    // Support ObjectId ou objet ayant un toString dÃ©diÃ©
    if (typeof val.toHexString === "function") {
      return val.toHexString();
    }
    if (typeof val.toString === "function" && val.toString !== Object.prototype.toString) {
      return String(val.toString());
    }
    return JSON.stringify(val);
  }
  return String(val);
}

/**
 * ProtÃ¨ge contre l'injection de formules CSV et Ã©chappe les sÃ©parateurs/guillemets.
 */
function escapeCsvCell(raw: string): string {
  let cell = raw;
  if (/^[=+\-@]/.test(cell)) {
    cell = `'${cell}`;
  }
  if (/[;"\r\n]/.test(cell)) {
    return `"${cell.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
  }
  return cell;
}

/**
 * Extrait l'identifiant [id] de faÃ§on asynchrone pour compatibilitÃ© Next.js 15/16.
 */
async function extractId(context?: any): Promise<string | null> {
  if (!context?.params) return null;
  const p = await Promise.resolve(context.params);
  return p?.id || null;
}

/**
 * Fabrique de routes CRUD gÃ©nÃ©rique et rÃ©utilisable.
 * Produit { list, create, getOne, update, remove } sÃ©curisÃ©s et typÃ©s.
 */
export function createCrudRoutes<T = any>(config: CrudRouteConfig<T>) {
  const allRoles = [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC, Role.RESIDENT, Role.TECHNICIAN];
  const readRoles = config.access.read || allRoles;
  const createRoles = config.access.create || [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC];
  const updateRoles = config.access.update || [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC];
  const deleteRoles = config.access.delete || [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC];

  // Construit le filtre combinÃ© (scope + recherche ?q + filtres simples + plage de dates)
  async function buildFilter(req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser): Promise<MongoFilter<T>> {
    const { searchParams } = req.nextUrl;
    const filterConditions: MongoFilter<T>[] = [];

    // 1. Restriction de pÃ©rimÃ¨tre mÃ©tier (Scope)
    if (config.scope) {
      const scopedFilter = await config.scope(user, req);
      if (scopedFilter && Object.keys(scopedFilter).length > 0) {
        filterConditions.push(scopedFilter);
      }
    }

    // 2. Recherche textuelle multi-champs (?q=...)
    const q = searchParams.get("q")?.trim();
    if (q && config.searchFields && config.searchFields.length > 0) {
      const regex = new RegExp(q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&"), "i");
      filterConditions.push({
        $or: config.searchFields.map((field) => ({ [field]: regex })),
      } as MongoFilter<T>);
    }

    // 3. Plage de dates (?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD)
    if (config.dateField) {
      const fromParam = searchParams.get("from")?.trim();
      const toParam = searchParams.get("to")?.trim();
      const dateRange: Record<string, Date> = {};

      if (fromParam && DATE_PARAM_REGEX.test(fromParam)) {
        const fromDate = new Date(fromParam);
        if (!isNaN(fromDate.getTime())) {
          dateRange.$gte = fromDate;
        }
      }
      if (toParam && DATE_PARAM_REGEX.test(toParam)) {
        const toDate = new Date(`${toParam}T23:59:59.999Z`);
        if (!isNaN(toDate.getTime())) {
          dateRange.$lte = toDate;
        }
      }

      if (Object.keys(dateRange).length > 0) {
        filterConditions.push({ [config.dateField]: dateRange } as MongoFilter<T>);
      }
    }

    // 4. Filtres simples dÃ©clarÃ©s ou passÃ©s en query (?residence=...&status=...)
    searchParams.forEach((value, key) => {
      if (["page", "limit", "q", "sort", "from", "to"].includes(key)) return;
      if (config.filterFields && !config.filterFields.includes(key)) return;

      if (value === "true") {
        filterConditions.push({ [key]: true } as MongoFilter<T>);
      } else if (value === "false") {
        filterConditions.push({ [key]: false } as MongoFilter<T>);
      } else if (value !== "") {
        filterConditions.push({ [key]: value } as MongoFilter<T>);
      }
    });

    return filterConditions.length > 1
      ? { $and: filterConditions }
      : filterConditions[0] || {};
  }

  // Construit l'option de tri par dÃ©faut ou personnalisÃ©e (?sort=publishedAt:desc)
  function buildSort(req: NextRequest): any {
    const { searchParams } = req.nextUrl;
    let sortOption: any = config.defaultSort || { createdAt: -1 };
    const sortParam = searchParams.get("sort");
    if (sortParam) {
      const [field, direction] = sortParam.split(":");
      if (field) {
        sortOption = { [field]: direction === "asc" ? 1 : -1 };
      }
    }
    return sortOption;
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 1. LISTE (GET /api/<res>)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const list = withAuth(
    async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser) => {
      const { searchParams } = req.nextUrl;

      // Pagination avec limite max 50
      const rawPage = parseInt(searchParams.get("page") || "1", 10);
      const rawLimit = parseInt(searchParams.get("limit") || "20", 10);
      const page = isNaN(rawPage) || rawPage < 1 ? 1 : rawPage;
      const limit = isNaN(rawLimit) || rawLimit < 1 ? 20 : Math.min(rawLimit, 50);
      const skip = (page - 1) * limit;

      const finalFilter = await buildFilter(req, user);
      const sortOption = buildSort(req);

      // ExÃ©cution parallÃ¨le des requÃªtes donnÃ©es + total
      const query = config.model.find(finalFilter).sort(sortOption).skip(skip).limit(limit);

      if (config.populate) {
        query.populate(config.populate);
      }

      const [data, total] = await Promise.all([
        query.lean(),
        config.model.countDocuments(finalFilter),
      ]);

      if (config.afterList) {
        const customResult = await config.afterList(data, req, user);
        if (customResult !== undefined) {
          return customResult instanceof Response ? customResult : json(customResult);
        }
      }

      return json({
        data,
        meta: {
          page,
          limit,
          total,
        },
      });
    },
    { roles: readRoles }
  );

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 1b. EXPORT CSV (GET /api/<res>/export)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const exportCsv = withAuth(
    async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser) => {
      if (!config.exportColumns || config.exportColumns.length === 0) {
        throw new HttpError(400, "Export non disponible pour cette ressource");
      }

      const finalFilter = await buildFilter(req, user);
      const sortOption = buildSort(req);

      const query = config.model.find(finalFilter).sort(sortOption).limit(5000);
      const populateOption = config.exportPopulate ?? config.populate;
      if (populateOption) {
        query.populate(populateOption);
      }

      const rows = (await query.lean()) as any[];

      const headerLine = config.exportColumns
        .map((col) => escapeCsvCell(col.label))
        .join(";");

      const dataLines = rows.map((row) =>
        config.exportColumns!
          .map((col) => {
            const rawVal = resolveDotPath(row, col.key);
            return escapeCsvCell(formatCsvValue(rawVal));
          })
          .join(";")
      );

      const csvBody = "\uFEFF" + [headerLine, ...dataLines].join("\r\n") + "\r\n";

      return new Response(csvBody, {
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "text/csv; charset=utf-8",
          "Content-Disposition": `attachment; filename="export-${Date.now()}.csv"`,
          "Cache-Control": "no-store",
        },
      });
    },
    { roles: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC] }
  );

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 2. CRÃ‰ATION (POST /api/<res>)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const create = withAuth(
    async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser) => {
      const parsedBody = await parseBody(req, config.createSchema);

      // Idempotence PWA : si clientId est fourni et prÃ©sent dans le schÃ©ma
      const rawClientId = (parsedBody as any)?.clientId;
      if (rawClientId && config.model.schema.path("clientId")) {
        const existing = await config.model.findOne({ clientId: rawClientId }).lean();
        if (existing) {
          return json(existing, 200);
        }
      }

      // ForÃ§age de champs cÃ´tÃ© serveur (ex: author: session.user.id)
      const injected = config.injectOnCreate
        ? await config.injectOnCreate(user, parsedBody, req)
        : {};

      const docData = {
        ...parsedBody,
        ...injected,
      };

      if (config.beforeCreate) {
        await config.beforeCreate(user, docData, req);
      }

      const created = await config.model.create(docData);
      const resDoc = created.toObject ? created.toObject() : created;
      if (config.afterCreate) {
        await config.afterCreate(resDoc, user, req);
      }
      return json(resDoc, 201);
    },
    { roles: createRoles }
  );

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 3. OBTENIR UN Ã‰LÃ‰MENT (GET /api/<res>/[id])
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const getOne = withAuth(
    async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser, context?: any) => {
      const id = await extractId(context);
      if (!isValidObjectId(id)) {
        throw new HttpError(400, "Identifiant invalide");
      }

      const filterConditions: MongoFilter<T>[] = [{ _id: id } as MongoFilter<T>];

      if (config.scope) {
        const scopedFilter = await config.scope(user, req);
        if (scopedFilter && Object.keys(scopedFilter).length > 0) {
          filterConditions.push(scopedFilter);
        }
      }

      const finalFilter: MongoFilter<T> =
        filterConditions.length > 1
          ? { $and: filterConditions }
          : filterConditions[0];

      const query = config.model.findOne(finalFilter);
      if (config.populate) {
        query.populate(config.populate);
      }

      const item = await query.lean();
      if (!item) {
        throw new HttpError(404, "Ressource introuvable");
      }

      return json(item, 200);
    },
    { roles: readRoles }
  );

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 4. MISE Ã€ JOUR (PATCH /api/<res>/[id])
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const update = withAuth(
    async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser, context?: any) => {
      const id = await extractId(context);
      if (!isValidObjectId(id)) {
        throw new HttpError(400, "Identifiant invalide");
      }

      const parsedBody = await parseBody(req, config.updateSchema);
      if (config.beforeUpdate) {
        await config.beforeUpdate(id, parsedBody, user, req);
      }

      const filterConditions: MongoFilter<T>[] = [{ _id: id } as MongoFilter<T>];

      if (config.scope) {
        const scopedFilter = await config.scope(user, req);
        if (scopedFilter && Object.keys(scopedFilter).length > 0) {
          filterConditions.push(scopedFilter);
        }
      }

      const finalFilter: MongoFilter<T> =
        filterConditions.length > 1
          ? { $and: filterConditions }
          : filterConditions[0];

      const updated = await config.model
        .findOneAndUpdate(finalFilter, { $set: parsedBody }, { new: true, runValidators: true })
        .lean();

      if (!updated) {
        throw new HttpError(404, "Ressource introuvable ou modification non autorisÃ©e");
      }

      if (config.afterUpdate) {
        await config.afterUpdate(updated, user, req);
      }

      return json(updated, 200);
    },
    { roles: updateRoles }
  );

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 5. SUPPRESSION (DELETE /api/<res>/[id])
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const remove = withAuth(
    async (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser, context?: any) => {
      const id = await extractId(context);
      if (!isValidObjectId(id)) {
        throw new HttpError(400, "Identifiant invalide");
      }

      const filterConditions: MongoFilter<T>[] = [{ _id: id } as MongoFilter<T>];

      if (config.scope) {
        const scopedFilter = await config.scope(user, req);
        if (scopedFilter && Object.keys(scopedFilter).length > 0) {
          filterConditions.push(scopedFilter);
        }
      }

      const finalFilter: MongoFilter<T> =
        filterConditions.length > 1
          ? { $and: filterConditions }
          : filterConditions[0];

      if (config.beforeDelete) {
        await config.beforeDelete(id, user, req);
      }

      const deleted = await config.model.findOneAndDelete(finalFilter).lean();

      if (!deleted) {
        throw new HttpError(404, "Ressource introuvable ou suppression non autorisÃ©e");
      }

      if (config.afterDelete) {
        await config.afterDelete(deleted, user, req);
      }

      return json({ success: true, message: "Ressource supprimÃ©e avec succÃ¨s" }, 200);
    },
    { roles: deleteRoles }
  );

  return {
    list,
    create,
    getOne,
    update,
    remove,
    exportCsv,
  };
}

import {
  type EquipmentCreateInput,
  type EquipmentUpdateInput,
  equipmentCreateSchema,
  equipmentUpdateSchema,
} from "@/lib/validations/maintenance";
import { Equipment } from "@/models/Equipment";
import { MaintenanceTask } from "@/models/MaintenanceTask";
import { Residence } from "@/models/Residence";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

type EquipmentUpdatePayload = EquipmentUpdateInput & {
  nextMaintenanceDate?: Date | null;
};

export const equipmentCrudRoutes = createCrudRoutes({
  model: Equipment,
  createSchema: equipmentCreateSchema,
  updateSchema: equipmentUpdateSchema,
  access: {
    read: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC, Role.TECHNICIAN],
    create: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
    update: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
    delete: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
  },
  searchFields: ["name", "location", "serialNumber"],
  defaultSort: { name: 1 },
  populate: [{ path: "residence", select: "name city" }],
  filterFields: ["residence", "category", "status"],
  scope: (user) => equipmentScope(user),
  injectOnCreate: async (_user, body: EquipmentCreateInput) => {
    // RÃ¨gle 3 : prochaine date = derniÃ¨re intervention (ou date de crÃ©ation) + frÃ©quence
    const next = computeNextMaintenanceDate(
      body.lastMaintenanceDate ?? new Date(),
      body.maintenanceIntervalDays
    );
    return next ? { nextMaintenanceDate: next } : {};
  },
  beforeCreate: async (user, body: EquipmentCreateInput) => {
    const exists = await Residence.exists({ _id: body.residence });
    if (!exists) {
      throw new HttpError(404, "RÃ©sidence introuvable");
    }
    if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
      const ids = await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id);
      if (!ids.includes(String(body.residence))) {
        throw new HttpError(403, "Vous n'administrez pas cette rÃ©sidence");
      }
    }
  },
  beforeUpdate: async (id, body: EquipmentUpdatePayload) => {
    if (
      body.maintenanceIntervalDays === undefined &&
      body.lastMaintenanceDate === undefined
    ) {
      return;
    }
    const existant = await Equipment.findById(id).lean();
    if (!existant) return;

    const interval =
      body.maintenanceIntervalDays ?? existant.maintenanceIntervalDays;
    const base =
      body.lastMaintenanceDate ??
      existant.lastMaintenanceDate ??
      existant.createdAt;
    body.nextMaintenanceDate = computeNextMaintenanceDate(base, interval);
  },
  beforeDelete: async (id, user) => {
    const filtre = { _id: id, ...(await equipmentScope(user)) };
    const exists = await Equipment.exists(filtre);
    if (!exists) return;

    const hasTasks = await MaintenanceTask.exists({ equipment: id });
    if (hasTasks) {
      throw new HttpError(
        409,
        "Suppression impossible : cet Ã©quipement a des interventions. Passez-le plutÃ´t Â« hors service Â»."
      );
    }
  },
});

      if (!ids.includes(String(body.residence))) {
        throw new HttpError(403, "Vous n'administrez pas cette rÃ©sidence");
      }
    }

      .select("residence")

    if (!equipement) {

    if (String(equipement.residence) !== String(body.residence)) {

      );

      await assertAssignableTechnician(String(body.assignedTo));

      const inc = await Incident.findById(body.incident)
        .select("residence")
        .lean();
      if (!inc) {
        throw new HttpError(404, "Incident introuvable");
      }

      await assertAssignableTechnician(String(body.assignedTo));

      _id: id,
      ...(await maintenanceScope(user)),
    }).lean();

      await syncEquipmentStatus(deleted.equipment);

import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { createCrudRoutes } from "@/lib/crud/createCrudRoutes";
import { Payment } from "@/models/Payment";
import { Charge } from "@/models/Charge";
import { Apartment } from "@/models/Apartment";
import { getResidentApartmentIds, getSyndicResidenceIds } from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import { paymentCreateSchema, paymentUpdateSchema } from "@/lib/validations/payment";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";
import { HttpError } from "@/lib/api";

/**
 * Helper qui recalcule et persiste le montant payÃ© et le statut d'une charge
 * Ã  partir de la somme rÃ©elle de tous ses paiements.
 */
export async function recalculateCharge(chargeId: string | mongoose.Types.ObjectId): Promise<void> {
  const cId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(String(chargeId));
  const [agg] = await Payment.aggregate<{ total: number }>([
    { $match: { charge: cId } },
    { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: "$amount" } } },
  ]);
  const charge = await Charge.findById(cId);
  if (!charge) return;
  charge.paidAmount = Math.round((agg?.total ?? 0) * 100) / 100;
  charge.updateStatus();
  await charge.save();
}

export const paymentCrudRoutes = createCrudRoutes({
  model: Payment,
  createSchema: paymentCreateSchema,
  updateSchema: paymentUpdateSchema,
  access: {
    read: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC, Role.RESIDENT],
    create: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
    update: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
    delete: [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
  },
  searchFields: ["receiptNumber"],
  defaultSort: { paymentDate: -1, createdAt: -1 },
  populate: [
    {
      path: "charge",
      select: "period amount paidAmount status apartment",
      populate: { path: "apartment", select: "number floor residence", populate: { path: "residence", select: "name" } },
    },
    { path: "receivedBy", select: "name email" },
  ],
  filterFields: ["charge", "method"],
  dateField: "paymentDate",
  exportPopulate: [
    {
      path: "charge",
      select: "period apartment",
      populate: {
        path: "apartment",
        select: "number residence",
        populate: { path: "residence", select: "name" },
      },
    },
    { path: "receivedBy", select: "name" },
  ],
  exportColumns: [
    { key: "receiptNumber", label: "NÂ° reÃ§u" },
    { key: "paymentDate", label: "Date" },
    { key: "amount", label: "Montant" },
    { key: "method", label: "Mode" },
    { key: "charge.period", label: "PÃ©riode" },
    { key: "charge.apartment.number", label: "Appartement" },
    { key: "charge.apartment.residence.name", label: "RÃ©sidence" },
    { key: "receivedBy.name", label: "EncaissÃ© par" },
  ],

  scope: async (user) => {
    // 1. ADMIN : accÃ¨s global
    if (user.role === Role.ADMIN) {
      return {};
    }
    // 2. SYNDIC : paiements des rÃ©sidences administrÃ©es
    if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
      const residenceIds = await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id);
      const aptIds = await Apartment.find({ residence: { $in: residenceIds } }).distinct("_id");
      const chargeIds = await Charge.find({ apartment: { $in: aptIds } }).distinct("_id");
      return { charge: { $in: chargeIds } };
    }
    // 3. RESIDENT : paiements rattachÃ©s Ã  ses propres charges uniquement
    if (user.role === Role.RESIDENT) {
      const allowedAptIds = await getResidentApartmentIds(user.id);
      const chargeIds = await Charge.find({ apartment: { $in: allowedAptIds } }).distinct("_id");
      return { charge: { $in: chargeIds } };
    }
    return {};

  injectOnCreate: async (user, body) => {
    // RÃ¨gle de sÃ©curitÃ© stricte : receivedBy est TOUJOURS session.user.id
    // GÃ©nÃ¨re receiptNumber de faÃ§on unique (REC-AAAAMMJJ-xxxxxx alÃ©atoire)
    const d = new Date();
    const ymd = d.toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, "");
    const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8).toUpperCase();
    const receiptNumber =
      body.receiptNumber && body.receiptNumber.trim().length > 0
        ? body.receiptNumber
        : `REC-${ymd}-${rand}`;

    return {
      receivedBy: user.id,
      receiptNumber,
      syncedAt: new Date(),
    };
  },

    if (!charge) {
      throw new HttpError(404, "Charge introuvable");
    }

    if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
      const apt = await Apartment.findById(charge.apartment).lean();
      if (apt) {
        const residenceIds = await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id);
        if (!residenceIds.includes(String(apt.residence))) {
          throw new HttpError(403, "Vous n'administrez pas la rÃ©sidence de cette charge");
        }
      }
    }

    // RÃ¨gle mÃ©tier : refuse un montant supÃ©rieur au reste Ã  payer
    const remaining = Math.round((charge.amount - (charge.paidAmount || 0)) * 100) / 100;
    if (body.amount > remaining + 0.001) {
      throw new HttpError(
        400,
        `Le montant saisi (${body.amount.toFixed(2)} â‚¬) dÃ©passe le reste Ã  payer de cette charge (${remaining.toFixed(2)} â‚¬)`
      );
    }
  },

  afterUpdate: async (payment) => {
    if (payment?.charge) {
      await recalculateCharge(payment.charge);
    }
  },

  afterDelete: async (payment) => {
    if (payment?.charge) {
      await recalculateCharge(payment.charge);
    }
  },

=== FILE: C:\atelier_nextJs\syndicpro\lib\incidents\workflow.ts ===
import { IncidentStatus } from "@/types/enums";

/**
 * Workflow strict des incidents (partagÃ© client / serveur, sans dÃ©pendance serveur) :
 * REPORTED -> ASSIGNED -> IN_PROGRESS -> RESOLVED
 */
export const INCIDENT_TRANSITIONS: Record<IncidentStatus, IncidentStatus[]> = {
  [IncidentStatus.REPORTED]: [IncidentStatus.ASSIGNED],
  [IncidentStatus.ASSIGNED]: [IncidentStatus.IN_PROGRESS],
  [IncidentStatus.IN_PROGRESS]: [IncidentStatus.RESOLVED],
  [IncidentStatus.RESOLVED]: [],
};

/** Vrai si la transition from -> to est autorisÃ©e par le workflow. */
export function canTransition(from: IncidentStatus, to: IncidentStatus): boolean {
  return INCIDENT_TRANSITIONS[from]?.includes(to) ?? false;
}

/** Statut suivant dans le workflow (null si terminal). */
export function nextIncidentStatus(from: IncidentStatus): IncidentStatus | null {
  return INCIDENT_TRANSITIONS[from]?.[0] ?? null;
}

import type { HydratedDocument, Types } from "mongoose";
import { HttpError, isValidObjectId } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  type AuthUser,
  getResidentResidenceIds,
  getSyndicResidenceIds,
} from "@/lib/auth-guard";
import type { MaintenanceCompleteInput } from "@/lib/validations/maintenance";
import { Employee } from "@/models/Employee";
import { Equipment } from "@/models/Equipment";
import { Expense } from "@/models/Expense";
import { Incident } from "@/models/Incident";
import { type IMaintenanceTask, MaintenanceTask } from "@/models/MaintenanceTask";
import { User } from "@/models/User";

  EquipmentStatus,
  ExpenseCategory,
  MaintenanceStatus,
  MaintenanceType,
  Role,
} from "@/types/enums";

  canTransitionMaintenance,
  computeNextMaintenanceDate,
  OPEN_MAINTENANCE_STATUSES,
} from "./workflow";

/**
 * Renvoie la liste dÃ©doublonnÃ©e des rÃ©sidences auxquelles un technicien est rattachÃ©
 * (fiche employÃ© active, interventions de maintenance assignÃ©es ou incidents assignÃ©s).
 */
export async function getTechnicianResourceResidenceIds(
  userId: string
): Promise<string[]> {
  const [empRes, taskRes, incRes] = await Promise.all([
    Employee.find({ user: userId, isActive: true }).distinct("residence"),
    MaintenanceTask.find({ assignedTo: userId }).distinct("residence"),
    Incident.find({ assignedTo: userId }).distinct("residence"),
  ]);

  const ids = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of [...empRes, ...taskRes, ...incRes]) {
    if (id) ids.add(String(id));
  }
  return Array.from(ids);
}

/**
 * Filtre de pÃ©rimÃ¨tre des Ã©quipements selon le rÃ´le de l'utilisateur.
 */
export async function equipmentScope(
  user: AuthUser
): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  if (user.role === Role.ADMIN) return {};
  if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
    return { residence: { $in: await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id) } };
  }
  if (user.role === Role.TECHNICIAN) {
    return { residence: { $in: await getTechnicianResourceResidenceIds(user.id) } };
  }
  if (user.role === Role.RESIDENT) {
    return { residence: { $in: await getResidentResidenceIds(user.id) } };
  }
  return { _id: null };
}

/**
 * RÃ¨gle 4 : synchronise automatiquement le statut d'un Ã©quipement selon ses pannes correctives ouvertes.
 * Le statut OUT_OF_SERVICE n'est jamais modifiÃ© automatiquement.
 */
export async function syncEquipmentStatus(
  equipmentId: string | Types.ObjectId
): Promise<void> {
  const eq = await Equipment.findById(equipmentId).lean();
  if (!eq) return;

  const open = await MaintenanceTask.countDocuments({
    equipment: eq._id,
    type: MaintenanceType.CORRECTIVE,
    status: { $in: OPEN_MAINTENANCE_STATUSES },
  });

  let nextStatus: EquipmentStatus | null = null;
  if (
    open > 0 &&
    (eq.status === EquipmentStatus.OPERATIONAL ||
      eq.status === EquipmentStatus.UNDER_MAINTENANCE)
  ) {
    nextStatus = EquipmentStatus.BROKEN;
  } else if (open === 0 && eq.status === EquipmentStatus.BROKEN) {
    nextStatus = EquipmentStatus.OPERATIONAL;
  }

  if (nextStatus && nextStatus !== eq.status) {
    await Equipment.updateOne({ _id: eq._id }, { $set: { status: nextStatus } });
  }
}

];

/**
 * Filtre de pÃ©rimÃ¨tre des interventions de maintenance selon le rÃ´le de l'utilisateur.
 */
export async function maintenanceScope(
  user: AuthUser
): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  if (user.role === Role.ADMIN) return {};
  if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
    return { residence: { $in: await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id) } };
  }
  if (user.role === Role.TECHNICIAN) {
    return { assignedTo: user.id };
  }
  return { _id: null };
}

/**
 * VÃ©rifie que l'identifiant correspond bien Ã  un technicien actif.
 */
export async function assertAssignableTechnician(userId: string): Promise<void> {
  const u = await User.findById(userId).select("role isActive").lean();
  if (!u) {
    throw new HttpError(404, "Technicien introuvable");
  }
  if (u.role !== Role.TECHNICIAN || !u.isActive) {
    throw new HttpError(400, "L'utilisateur assignÃ© doit Ãªtre un technicien actif");
  }
}

/**
 * Charge une intervention et vÃ©rifie les droits d'accÃ¨s de l'acteur.
 */
export async function loadTaskForActor(
  id: string | null | undefined,
  user: AuthUser
): Promise<HydratedDocument<IMaintenanceTask>> {
  if (!isValidObjectId(id)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, "Identifiant invalide");
  }

  const task = await MaintenanceTask.findById(id);
  if (!task) {
    throw new HttpError(404, "Intervention introuvable");
  }

  if (
    user.role === Role.TECHNICIAN &&
    String(task.assignedTo) !== String(user.id)
  ) {
    throw new HttpError(403, "Cette intervention ne vous est pas assignÃ©e");
  }

  if (user.role === Role.SYNDIC) {
    const ids = await getSyndicResidenceIds(user.id);
    if (!ids.includes(String(task.residence))) {
      throw new HttpError(403, "Vous n'administrez pas cette rÃ©sidence");
    }
  }

  return task;
}

/**
 * Fait Ã©voluer le statut d'une intervention (dÃ©marrage ou annulation).
 */
export async function changeTaskStatus(
  task: HydratedDocument<IMaintenanceTask>,
  target: typeof MaintenanceStatus.IN_PROGRESS | typeof MaintenanceStatus.CANCELLED
): Promise<void> {
  if (!canTransitionMaintenance(task.status, target)) {
    throw new HttpError(
      409,
      `Transition invalide : de "${task.status}" vers "${target}".`
    );
  }

  task.status = target;
  await task.save();

  if (
    task.type === MaintenanceType.CORRECTIVE &&
    target === MaintenanceStatus.CANCELLED
  ) {
    await syncEquipmentStatus(task.equipment);
  }
}

/**
 * ClÃ´ture une intervention de maintenance (RÃ¨gles 3, 4 et 7).
 */
export async function completeTask(
  task: HydratedDocument<IMaintenanceTask>,
  data: MaintenanceCompleteInput
): Promise<void> {
  // 1. Idempotence hors ligne et garde statut
  if (task.status === MaintenanceStatus.DONE) return;
  if (task.status === MaintenanceStatus.CANCELLED) {
    throw new HttpError(409, "Intervention annulÃ©e : clÃ´ture impossible");
  }

  // 2. Consolidation des champs de clÃ´ture
  const originalStatus = task.status;
  const completedAt = data.completedAt ?? new Date();
  const cost = data.cost ?? task.cost;
  const notes = data.notes ?? task.notes;
  const checklist = data.checklist
    ? data.checklist.map((i) => ({ label: i.label, done: i.done ?? false }))
    : task.checklist;

  // 3. Prise atomique anti double clÃ´ture
  const claimed = await MaintenanceTask.findOneAndUpdate(
    { _id: task._id, status: { $in: OPEN_MAINTENANCE_STATUSES } },
    {
      $set: {
        status: MaintenanceStatus.DONE,
        completedAt,
        cost,
        notes,
        checklist,
      },
    },
    { new: true, runValidators: true }
  );

  if (!claimed) {
    const reloaded = await MaintenanceTask.findById(task._id).lean();
    if (reloaded?.status === MaintenanceStatus.DONE) return;
    throw new HttpError(409, "ClÃ´ture impossible");
  }

  const eq = await Equipment.findById(task.equipment).lean();

  // 4. RÃ¨gle 7 : crÃ©ation automatique d'une dÃ©pense si cost > 0
  if (cost > 0 && !claimed.expense) {
    let createdExpenseId: Types.ObjectId | null = null;
    try {
      const description = eq?.name
        ? `Maintenance : ${task.title} (${eq.name})`
        : `Maintenance : ${task.title}`;
      const expense = await Expense.create({
        residence: task.residence,
        category: ExpenseCategory.MAINTENANCE,
        description,
        amount: cost,
        expenseDate: completedAt,
      });
      createdExpenseId = expense._id;
      await MaintenanceTask.updateOne(
        { _id: task._id },
        { $set: { expense: expense._id } }
      );
    } catch (err) {
      if (createdExpenseId) {
        await Expense.deleteOne({ _id: createdExpenseId }).catch(() => {});
      }
      await MaintenanceTask.updateOne(
        { _id: task._id },
        {
          $set: { status: originalStatus },
          $unset: { completedAt: 1 },
        }
      ).catch(() => {});
      throw err;
    }
  }

  // 5. RÃ¨gle 3 : mise Ã  jour des dates d'entretien de l'Ã©quipement
  if (
    eq &&
    (!eq.lastMaintenanceDate ||
      completedAt.getTime() >= new Date(eq.lastMaintenanceDate).getTime())
  ) {
    await Equipment.updateOne(
      { _id: eq._id },
      {
        $set: {
          lastMaintenanceDate: completedAt,
          nextMaintenanceDate: computeNextMaintenanceDate(
            completedAt,
            eq.maintenanceIntervalDays
          ),
        },
      }
    );
  }

  // 6. RÃ¨gle 4 : synchronisation du statut de l'Ã©quipement
  await syncEquipmentStatus(task.equipment);
}

import { MaintenanceStatus } from "@/types/enums";

/**
 * Transitions autorisÃ©es pour le statut d'une intervention de maintenance.
 * Le passage direct de PLANNED Ã  DONE est autorisÃ© pour la clÃ´ture hors ligne.
 */
export const MAINTENANCE_TRANSITIONS: Record<MaintenanceStatus, MaintenanceStatus[]> = {
  [MaintenanceStatus.PLANNED]: [
    MaintenanceStatus.IN_PROGRESS,
    MaintenanceStatus.DONE,
    MaintenanceStatus.CANCELLED,
  ],
  [MaintenanceStatus.IN_PROGRESS]: [
    MaintenanceStatus.DONE,
    MaintenanceStatus.CANCELLED,
  ],
  [MaintenanceStatus.DONE]: [],
  [MaintenanceStatus.CANCELLED]: [],
};

export function canTransitionMaintenance(
  from: MaintenanceStatus,
  to: MaintenanceStatus
): boolean {
  return MAINTENANCE_TRANSITIONS[from]?.includes(to) ?? false;
}

export const OPEN_MAINTENANCE_STATUSES: MaintenanceStatus[] = [
  MaintenanceStatus.PLANNED,
  MaintenanceStatus.IN_PROGRESS,
];

/**
 * Calcule la prochaine date d'entretien pÃ©riodique.
 * Renvoie null si la date de base est absente/invalide ou si l'intervalle est absent ou <= 0.
 */
export function computeNextMaintenanceDate(
  base: Date | string | null | undefined,
  intervalDays: number | null | undefined
): Date | null {
  if (!base || !intervalDays || intervalDays <= 0) return null;
  const d = new Date(base);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + intervalDays);
  return d;
}

import { getOfflineDB } from "./db";
import type {
  ResidenceDTO,
  ApartmentDTO,
  ChargeDTO,
  IncidentDTO,
  AnnouncementDTO,
  DocumentDTO,
  PaymentDTO,
} from "@/types/api";

interface FetchOptions extends RequestInit {
  entity?: "residence" | "apartment" | "charge" | "incident" | "announcement" | "payment" | "document";
  offlineFallbackData?: unknown;
}

/**
 * Client HTTP : Fonctionnement 100% standard en ligne, avec repli automatique
 * uniquement si la connexion internet est coupÃ©e.
 */
export async function fetchWithOffline<T>(url: string, options: FetchOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
  const method = (options.method || "GET").toUpperCase();
  const db = getOfflineDB();

  const isActuallyOnline = typeof navigator !== "undefined" ? navigator.onLine : true;

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 1. REQUÃŠTES EN LECTURE (GET)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  if (method === "GET") {
    // Si on a du rÃ©seau : appel direct au serveur comme un site normal
    if (isActuallyOnline) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(url, options);
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = (await res.json()) as T;
          // Mise en cache asynchrone en arriÃ¨re-plan sans bloquer
          cacheGetResult(url, data).catch(() => {});
          return data;
        }
        // Si le serveur a rÃ©pondu (ex: 400 ou 500), on remonte l'erreur sans basculer sur le offline
        const errorText = await res.text();
        throw new Error(`Erreur serveur (${res.status}): ${errorText}`);
      } catch (networkError: unknown) {
        // Seulement si c'est une VRAIE erreur de coupure rÃ©seau (ex: Failed to fetch)
        if (
          !navigator.onLine ||
          (networkError instanceof TypeError && networkError.message.includes("fetch"))
        ) {
          console.warn("[ApiClient] Connexion rÃ©seau perdue, passage automatique au cache local.");
        } else {
          throw networkError;
        }
      }
    }

    // RÃ©seau coupÃ© : repli transparent sur le cache local Dexie
    const cached = await readFromLocalCache<T>(url);
    if (cached !== null) {
      return cached;
    }

    if (options.offlineFallbackData !== undefined) {
      return options.offlineFallbackData as T;
    }

    throw new Error("Vous Ãªtes hors ligne et cette ressource n'a pas encore Ã©tÃ© consultÃ©e.");
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 2. REQUÃŠTES EN SUPPRESSION (DELETE)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const entityType = options.entity || inferEntityFromUrl(url);
  const targetId = url.split("?")[0].split("/").filter(Boolean).pop();

  if (method === "DELETE") {
    if (isActuallyOnline) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(url, options);
        if (res.ok) {
          const responseData = (await res.json().catch(() => ({ success: true }))) as T;
          if (targetId) {
            removeLocalCacheItem(entityType, targetId).catch(() => {});
          }
          return responseData;
        }
        const errorJson = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(errorJson.error || `Erreur serveur (${res.status})`);
      } catch (networkError: unknown) {
        if (!navigator.onLine || (networkError instanceof TypeError && networkError.message.includes("fetch"))) {
          console.warn("[ApiClient] Coupure rÃ©seau pendant le DELETE, bascule automatique sur l'Outbox.");
        } else {
          throw networkError;
        }
      }
    }

    // RÃ©seau coupÃ© pour DELETE : enregistrement outbox et retrait du cache local
    const clientId =
      typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID
        ? crypto.randomUUID()
        : `offline-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`;

    const { getOfflineSession } = await import("./db");
    const offlineUser = await getOfflineSession();

    await db.outbox.put({
      id: clientId,
      userId: offlineUser?.id,
      entity: (entityType as any) || "incident",
      method: "DELETE",
      url,
      payload: {},
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: "pending",
      retries: 0,
    });

    if (targetId) {
      await removeLocalCacheItem(entityType, targetId);
    }

    return { success: true } as unknown as T;
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // 3. REQUÃŠTES EN Ã‰CRITURE (POST / PATCH / PUT)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  const rawBody = options.body ? JSON.parse(options.body as string) : {};

  // Si on est en ligne : envoi direct normal au serveur
  if (isActuallyOnline) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        ...options,
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          ...(options.headers || {}),
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(rawBody),
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        const responseData = (await res.json()) as T;
        updateLocalCacheAfterMutation(entityType, responseData, false, method).catch(() => {});
        return responseData;
      }

      // Si le serveur a rejetÃ© la requÃªte mÃ©tier (ex: validation 400), on retourne l'erreur
      const errorJson = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(errorJson.error || `Erreur serveur (${res.status})`);
    } catch (networkError: unknown) {
      if (
        !navigator.onLine ||
        (networkError instanceof TypeError && networkError.message.includes("fetch"))
      ) {
        console.warn("[ApiClient] Coupure rÃ©seau pendant la mutation, bascule automatique sur l'Outbox.");
      } else {
        throw networkError;
      }
    }
  }

  // RÃ©seau coupÃ© : enregistrement automatique dans la file Outbox et rÃ©ponse optimiste
  const { getOfflineSession } = await import("./db");
  const offlineUser = await getOfflineSession();

  // PATCH hors ligne : met Ã  jour l'Ã©lÃ©ment existant dans Dexie sans crÃ©er de faux Ã©lÃ©ment
  if (method === "PATCH") {
    const clientId =
      rawBody.clientId ||
      (typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID
        ? crypto.randomUUID()
        : `offline-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`);

    await db.outbox.put({
      id: clientId,
      userId: offlineUser?.id,
      entity: (entityType as any) || "incident",
      method: "PATCH",
      url,
      payload: rawBody,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: "pending",
      retries: 0,
    });

    const updated = await patchLocalCacheItem(entityType, targetId || "", rawBody);
    return updated as unknown as T;
  }

  // POST hors ligne : crÃ©ation optimiste
  const clientId =
    rawBody.clientId ||
    (typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID
      ? crypto.randomUUID()
      : `offline-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`);

  const payload = {
    ...rawBody,
    clientId,
  };

  await db.outbox.put({
    id: clientId,
    userId: offlineUser?.id,
    entity: (entityType as any) || "incident",
    method: "POST",
    url,
    payload,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    status: "pending",
    retries: 0,
  });

  const optimisticResult = createOptimisticResponse(entityType, payload, clientId);
  await updateLocalCacheAfterMutation(entityType, optimisticResult, true, "POST");

  return optimisticResult as unknown as T;
}

function inferEntityFromUrl(url: string): string {
  if (url.includes("/api/incidents")) return "incident";
  if (url.includes("/api/payments")) return "payment";
  if (url.includes("/api/charges")) return "charge";
  if (url.includes("/api/announcements")) return "announcement";
  if (url.includes("/api/residences")) return "residence";
  if (url.includes("/api/apartments")) return "apartment";
  if (url.includes("/api/documents")) return "document";
  return "unknown";
}

async function cacheGetResult(url: string, rawData: unknown) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || !rawData) return;
  const db = getOfflineDB();

  // Les rÃ©ponses API sont { data: [...], meta } ou tableau
  const data =
    typeof rawData === "object" && rawData !== null && "data" in rawData
      ? (rawData as any).data
      : rawData;

  try {
    if (url.includes("/api/residences")) {
      const list = Array.isArray(data) ? (data as ResidenceDTO[]) : [data as ResidenceDTO];
      await db.residences.bulkPut(list);
    } else if (url.includes("/api/apartments")) {
      const list = Array.isArray(data) ? (data as ApartmentDTO[]) : [data as ApartmentDTO];
      await db.apartments.bulkPut(list);
    } else if (url.includes("/api/charges")) {
      const list = Array.isArray(data) ? (data as ChargeDTO[]) : [data as ChargeDTO];
      await db.charges.bulkPut(list);
    } else if (url.includes("/api/incidents")) {
      const list = Array.isArray(data) ? (data as IncidentDTO[]) : [data as IncidentDTO];
      await db.incidents.bulkPut(list);
    } else if (url.includes("/api/announcements")) {
      const list = Array.isArray(data) ? (data as AnnouncementDTO[]) : [data as AnnouncementDTO];
      await db.announcements.bulkPut(list);
    } else if (url.includes("/api/documents")) {
      const list = Array.isArray(data) ? (data as DocumentDTO[]) : [data as DocumentDTO];
      await db.documents.bulkPut(list);
    } else if (url.includes("/api/dashboard")) {
      await db.dashboard.put({
        id: "current",
        data: rawData,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      });
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignorer silencieusement
  }
}

async function readFromLocalCache<T>(url: string): Promise<T | null> {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  const db = getOfflineDB();

  try {
    if (url.includes("/api/dashboard")) {
      const cached = await db.dashboard.get("current");
      return (cached?.data || null) as unknown as T;
    }

    // Gestion ressource unique /api/<entity>/[id]
    const pathNoQuery = url.split("?")[0];
    const matchId = pathNoQuery.match(/\/api\/([a-z]+)\/([a-f0-9]{24}|[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{10,})$/i);
    if (matchId) {
      const entity = matchId[1];
      const id = matchId[2];
      let item = null;
      if (entity === "residences") item = await db.residences.get(id);
      else if (entity === "apartments") item = await db.apartments.get(id);
      else if (entity === "charges") item = await db.charges.get(id);
      else if (entity === "incidents") item = await db.incidents.get(id);
      else if (entity === "announcements") item = await db.announcements.get(id);
      else if (entity === "documents") item = await db.documents.get(id);
      if (item) return item as unknown as T;
    }

    let items: any[] = [];
    if (url.includes("/api/residences")) {
      items = await db.residences.toArray();
    } else if (url.includes("/api/apartments")) {
      items = await db.apartments.toArray();
    } else if (url.includes("/api/charges")) {
      items = await db.charges.toArray();
    } else if (url.includes("/api/incidents")) {
      items = await db.incidents.reverse().sortBy("reportedAt");
    } else if (url.includes("/api/announcements")) {
      items = await db.announcements.reverse().sortBy("publishedAt");
    } else if (url.includes("/api/documents")) {
      items = await db.documents.reverse().sortBy("uploadedAt");
    }

    if (items.length > 0) {
      return {
        data: items,
        meta: {
          page: 1,
          limit: items.length,
          total: items.length,
        },
      } as unknown as T;
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignorer silencieusement
  }
  return null;
}

function createOptimisticResponse(entity: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>, clientId: string) {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  if (entity === "incident") {
    return {
      _id: clientId,
      clientId,
      residence: payload.residence,
      apartment: payload.apartment,
      title: payload.title,
      description: payload.description,
      category: payload.category,
      photos: payload.photos || [],
      priority: payload.priority || "MEDIUM",
      status: "REPORTED",
      reportedAt: now,
      createdOffline: true,
      pending: true,
    } as IncidentDTO;
  }

  if (entity === "payment") {
    return {
      _id: clientId,
      clientId,
      charge: payload.charge,
      amount: payload.amount,
      paymentDate: now,
      method: payload.method,
      receiptNumber: `OFFLINE-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`,
      createdOffline: true,
    } as PaymentDTO;
  }

  return {
    _id: clientId,
    ...payload,
    createdOffline: true,
  };
}

async function removeLocalCacheItem(entity: string, id: string) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || !id) return;
  const db = getOfflineDB();
  try {
    if (entity === "incident") await db.incidents.delete(id);
    else if (entity === "charge") await db.charges.delete(id);
    else if (entity === "residence") await db.residences.delete(id);
    else if (entity === "apartment") await db.apartments.delete(id);
    else if (entity === "announcement") await db.announcements.delete(id);
    else if (entity === "document") await db.documents.delete(id);
  } catch {
    // Ignorer
  }
}

async function patchLocalCacheItem(entity: string, id: string, patch: Record<string, unknown>) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || !id) return { _id: id, ...patch };
  const db = getOfflineDB();
  try {
    if (entity === "incident") {
      const existing = await db.incidents.get(id);
      if (existing) {
        const merged = { ...existing, ...patch, pending: true };
        await db.incidents.put(merged);
        return merged;
      }
    } else if (entity === "charge") {
      const existing = await db.charges.get(id);
      if (existing) {
        const merged = { ...existing, ...patch };
        await db.charges.put(merged);
        return merged;
      }
    } else if (entity === "residence") {
      const existing = await db.residences.get(id);
      if (existing) {
        const merged = { ...existing, ...patch };
        await db.residences.put(merged);
        return merged;
      }
    } else if (entity === "apartment") {
      const existing = await db.apartments.get(id);
      if (existing) {
        const merged = { ...existing, ...patch };
        await db.apartments.put(merged);
        return merged;
      }
    } else if (entity === "announcement") {
      const existing = await db.announcements.get(id);
      if (existing) {
        const merged = { ...existing, ...patch };
        await db.announcements.put(merged);
        return merged;
      }
    } else if (entity === "document") {
      const existing = await db.documents.get(id);
      if (existing) {
        const merged = { ...existing, ...patch };
        await db.documents.put(merged);
        return merged;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignorer
  }
  return { _id: id, ...patch };
}

async function updateLocalCacheAfterMutation(
  entity: string,
  data: unknown,
  isOptimistic = false,
  _method = "POST"
) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || !data) return;
  const db = getOfflineDB();

  try {
    if (entity === "incident") {
      const incident = data as IncidentDTO;
      await db.incidents.put({
        ...incident,
        pending: isOptimistic,
      });
    } else if (entity === "payment") {
      const payment = data as PaymentDTO;
      if (isOptimistic) {
        // En mode optimiste hors ligne : mise Ã  jour temporaire
        const charge = await db.charges.get(payment.charge);
        if (charge) {
          const updatedPaid = (charge.paidAmount || 0) + payment.amount;
          await db.charges.update(charge._id, {
            paidAmount: updatedPaid,
            remaining: Math.max(0, charge.amount - updatedPaid),
            status: updatedPaid >= charge.amount ? "PAID" : "PARTIAL",
          });
        }
      } else {
        // En ligne / synchro : recalcule Ã  partir des donnÃ©es serveur au lieu d'ajouter le montant
        try {
          const chargeRes = await fetch(`/api/charges/${payment.charge}`);
          if (chargeRes.ok) {
            const chargeData = await chargeRes.json();
            const chargeToStore =
              chargeData && typeof chargeData === "object" && "data" in chargeData
                ? chargeData.data
                : chargeData;
            if (chargeToStore && chargeToStore._id) {
              await db.charges.put(chargeToStore);
            }
          }
        } catch {
          // Si Ã©chec rÃ©seau, on ne sur-ajoute pas
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignorer
  }
}

import Dexie, { type Table } from "dexie";
import type {
  ResidenceDTO,
  ApartmentDTO,
  ChargeDTO,
  AnnouncementDTO,
  IncidentDTO,
  DocumentDTO,
} from "@/types/api";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

export interface OfflineSessionProfile {
  id: string; // userId
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: Role;
  expiresAt: number; // timestamp ms
}

export interface OutboxItem {
  id: string; // uuid = clientId
  userId?: string; // id de l'utilisateur ayant Ã©mis la requÃªte
  entity: "incident" | "payment" | "charge" | "announcement" | "residence" | "apartment" | "document";
  method: "POST" | "PATCH" | "DELETE";
  url: string;
  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  createdAt: string;
  status: "pending" | "syncing" | "failed";
  retries: number;
  error?: string;
}

export class SyndicProDatabase extends Dexie {
  residences!: Table<ResidenceDTO, string>;
  apartments!: Table<ApartmentDTO, string>;
  charges!: Table<ChargeDTO, string>;
  announcements!: Table<AnnouncementDTO, string>;
  incidents!: Table<IncidentDTO, string>;
  documents!: Table<DocumentDTO, string>;
  outbox!: Table<OutboxItem, string>;
  session!: Table<OfflineSessionProfile, string>;
  dashboard!: Table<{ id: string; data: any; updatedAt: string }, string>;

  constructor() {
    super("SyndicProDB");
    this.version(1).stores({
      residences: "_id",
      apartments: "_id, residence",
      charges: "_id, apartment, period, status",
      announcements: "_id, residence, isPinned, publishedAt",
      incidents: "_id, residence, status, clientId, reportedAt",
      documents: "_id, residence, type",
      outbox: "id, status, createdAt, entity",
    });
    this.version(2).stores({
      outbox: "id, status, createdAt, entity, userId",
      session: "id",
    });
    this.version(3).stores({
      dashboard: "id",
    });
  }
}


// Instance singleton utilisable cÃ´tÃ© client uniquement
let dbInstance: SyndicProDatabase | null = null;

export function getOfflineDB(): SyndicProDatabase {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") {
    // Si appelÃ© cÃ´tÃ© serveur (SSR), on retourne un proxy vide sÃ©curisÃ©
    return new Proxy({} as SyndicProDatabase, {
      get() {
        return undefined;
      },
    });
  }
  if (!dbInstance) {
    dbInstance = new SyndicProDatabase();
  }
  return dbInstance;
}

/**
 * Enregistre le profil utilisateur dans Dexie Ã  la connexion rÃ©ussie.
 */
export async function saveOfflineSession(profile: OfflineSessionProfile): Promise<void> {
  const db = getOfflineDB();
  if (db.session) {
    await db.session.put(profile);
  }
}

/**
 * RÃ©cupÃ¨re le profil utilisateur actif en cache Dexie (utile en mode hors-ligne).
 */
export async function getOfflineSession(): Promise<OfflineSessionProfile | undefined> {
  const db = getOfflineDB();
  if (!db.session) return undefined;
  const sessions = await db.session.toArray();
  const session = sessions[0];
  if (!session) return undefined;
  // VÃ©rifie si la session offline a expirÃ©
  if (session.expiresAt && session.expiresAt < Date.now()) {
    await db.session.clear();
    return undefined;
  }
  return session;
}

/**
 * Supprime la session offline lors de la dÃ©connexion.
 */
export async function clearOfflineSession(): Promise<void> {
  const db = getOfflineDB();
  if (db.session) {
    await db.session.clear();
  }
}

/**
 * Vide TOUTES les tables Dexie (donnÃ©es, session, dashboard, et optionnellement outbox).
 */
export async function clearAllOfflineData(clearOutbox = true): Promise<void> {
  const db = getOfflineDB();
  const tasks: Promise<void>[] = [];
  if (db.residences) tasks.push(db.residences.clear());
  if (db.apartments) tasks.push(db.apartments.clear());
  if (db.charges) tasks.push(db.charges.clear());
  if (db.announcements) tasks.push(db.announcements.clear());
  if (db.incidents) tasks.push(db.incidents.clear());
  if (db.documents) tasks.push(db.documents.clear());
  if (db.session) tasks.push(db.session.clear());
  if (db.dashboard) tasks.push(db.dashboard.clear());
  if (clearOutbox && db.outbox) tasks.push(db.outbox.clear());
  await Promise.all(tasks);
}

import { getOfflineDB } from "./db";
import type { IncidentDTO, PaymentDTO } from "@/types/api";

type SyncListener = (syncing: boolean, pendingCount: number) => void;
const listeners: Set<SyncListener> = new Set();

export function onSyncChange(listener: SyncListener) {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

function notifySync(syncing: boolean, count: number) {
  listeners.forEach((l) => l(syncing, count));
}

type SessionExpiredListener = (message: string) => void;
const sessionExpiredListeners: Set<SessionExpiredListener> = new Set();

export function onSessionExpired(listener: SessionExpiredListener) {
  sessionExpiredListeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    sessionExpiredListeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

export function notifySessionExpired(message = "Session expirÃ©e, reconnectez-vous pour synchroniser") {
  sessionExpiredListeners.forEach((l) => l(message));
  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("syndicpro:session-expired", { detail: { message } }));
  }
}

let isSyncInProgress = false;

/**
 * Rejoue toutes les requÃªtes en attente dans la file Outbox Dexie.
 * DÃ©clenchÃ© lors du retour de connexion, au chargement de l'app ou manuellement.
 */
export async function syncOutbox(): Promise<{ processed: number; errors: number }> {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return { processed: 0, errors: 0 };
  if (!navigator.onLine) return { processed: 0, errors: 0 };
  if (isSyncInProgress) return { processed: 0, errors: 0 };

  const db = getOfflineDB();
  const { getOfflineSession } = await import("./db");
  const currentSession = await getOfflineSession();

  const pendingItems = await db.outbox
    .where("status")
    .anyOf(["pending", "failed"])
    .sortBy("createdAt");

  if (pendingItems.length === 0) {
    notifySync(false, 0);
    return { processed: 0, errors: 0 };
  }

  isSyncInProgress = true;
  notifySync(true, pendingItems.length);

  let processed = 0;
  let errors = 0;

  for (const item of pendingItems) {
    // Ignore et ne rejoue jamais les Ã©lÃ©ments dont userId diffÃ¨re de la session courante
    if (item.userId && (!currentSession?.id || item.userId !== currentSession.id)) {
      continue;
    }

    try {
      await db.outbox.update(item.id, { status: "syncing" });

      const isDelete = item.method === "DELETE";
      const response = await fetch(item.url, {
        method: item.method,
        headers: isDelete ? undefined : { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: isDelete ? undefined : JSON.stringify(item.payload),
      });

      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

        // Mise Ã  jour du cache local suite au succÃ¨s serveur
        if (item.entity === "incident") {
          const serverIncident = data as IncidentDTO;
          await db.incidents.put({
            ...serverIncident,
            pending: false,
          });
        } else if (item.entity === "payment") {
          const payment = data as PaymentDTO;
          // Ã‰vite le double comptage : recalcule Ã  partir des donnÃ©es serveur au lieu d'ajouter le montant
          try {
            const chargeRes = await fetch(`/api/charges/${payment.charge}`);
            if (chargeRes.ok) {
              const chargeData = await chargeRes.json();
              const chargeToStore =
                chargeData && typeof chargeData === "object" && "data" in chargeData
                  ? chargeData.data
                  : chargeData;
              if (chargeToStore && chargeToStore._id) {
                await db.charges.put(chargeToStore);
              }
            }
          } catch {
            // Ignorer si Ã©chec rÃ©seau temporaire
          }
        }

        // Supprime l'Ã©lÃ©ment de la file d'attente
        await db.outbox.delete(item.id);
        processed++;
      } else if (response.status === 401) {
        // Point critique H4 : JWT expirÃ© lors de la synchro
        // On remet l'Ã©lÃ©ment en 'pending' (PAS 'failed') pour qu'aucune donnÃ©e ne soit perdue
        await db.outbox.update(item.id, { status: "pending" });
        notifySessionExpired("Session expirÃ©e, reconnectez-vous pour synchroniser");
        
        // Redirection vers login avec conservation de la page courante
        const currentPath = window.location.pathname + window.location.search;
        window.location.href = `/login?callbackUrl=${encodeURIComponent(currentPath)}`;
        break; // ArrÃªte la boucle de synchro
      } else {
        const errText = await response.text();
        await db.outbox.update(item.id, {
          status: "failed",
          retries: item.retries + 1,
          error: `Erreur serveur (${response.status}): ${errText.slice(0, 100)}`,
        });
        errors++;
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const errorMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Erreur rÃ©seau";
      await db.outbox.update(item.id, {
        status: "failed",
        retries: item.retries + 1,
        error: errorMsg,
      });
      errors++;
      if (!navigator.onLine) break;
    }
  }

  isSyncInProgress = false;
  const remaining = await db.outbox.where("status").anyOf(["pending", "failed"]).count();
  notifySync(false, remaining);

  return { processed, errors };
}

/**
 * Initialise les Ã©couteurs de reprise de connectivitÃ© pour dÃ©clencher
 * automatiquement la synchronisation.
 */
export function initAutoSync() {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

  window.addEventListener("online", () => {
    console.log("[Sync] RÃ©seau rÃ©tabli, lancement de syncOutbox()...");
    syncOutbox();
  });

  if (navigator.onLine) {
    syncOutbox();
  }
}

import { registerAuthPaths } from "./auth";
import { registerUsersPaths } from "./users";
import { registerResidencesPaths } from "./residences";
import { registerApartmentsPaths } from "./apartments";

import { registerChargesPaths } from "./charges";
import { registerPaymentsPaths } from "./payments";
import { registerExpensesPaths } from "./expenses";
import { registerIncidentsPaths } from "./incidents";
import { registerAnnouncementsPaths } from "./announcements";
import { registerMeetingsPaths } from "./meetings";
import { registerResolutionsPaths } from "./resolutions";
import { registerVotesPaths } from "./votes";
import { registerDocumentsPaths } from "./documents";
import { registerDashboardPaths } from "./dashboard";

let pathsRegistered = false;

export function registerAllPaths() {
  if (pathsRegistered) return;
  registerAuthPaths();
  registerUsersPaths();
  registerResidencesPaths();
  registerApartmentsPaths();
  registerResidentsPaths();
  registerChargesPaths();
  registerPaymentsPaths();
  registerExpensesPaths();
  registerIncidentsPaths();
  registerAnnouncementsPaths();
  registerMeetingsPaths();
  registerResolutionsPaths();
  registerVotesPaths();
  registerDocumentsPaths();
  registerDashboardPaths();
  pathsRegistered = true;
}

import { OpenApiGeneratorV3 } from "@asteasolutions/zod-to-openapi";
import { registry } from "./registry";
import { registerAllPaths } from "./paths";

let cachedDocument: ReturnType<OpenApiGeneratorV3["generateDocument"]> | null = null;

/**
 * GÃ©nÃ¨re la spÃ©cification OpenAPI 3.0 complÃ¨te pour l'API SyndicPro.
 * Les schÃ©mas Zod et les routes sont enregistrÃ©s puis compilÃ©s en spÃ©cification standard.

export function getOpenApiDocument() {

=== FILE: C:\atelier_nextJs\syndicpro\lib\openapi\init.ts ===
import { extendZodWithOpenApi } from "@asteasolutions/zod-to-openapi";
import { z } from "zod";

// Ã‰tend Zod avec les mÃ©thodes OpenAPI (.openapi())
extendZodWithOpenApi(z);

const openapiFn = (z as any).ZodType?.prototype?.openapi;

/**
 * Garantit que le schÃ©ma possÃ¨de la mÃ©thode .openapi() mÃªme si le schÃ©ma a Ã©tÃ© instanciÃ©
 * avant l'appel Ã  extendZodWithOpenApi (par exemple suite au hoisting d'imports par Turbopack).
 */
export function ensureOpenApi<T>(schema: T): T {
  if (schema && typeof (schema as any).openapi !== "function" && openapiFn) {
    const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(schema);
    if (proto && !proto.openapi) {
      proto.openapi = openapiFn;
    }
    (schema as any).openapi = openapiFn;
  }
  return schema;
}

  EQUIPMENT_CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.value === v)?.label ?? v;

const daysUntil = (d?: string | Date | null) =>
  d ? Math.ceil((new Date(d).getTime() - Date.now()) / 86400000) : null;

        if (days !== null && days < 0) {
          return <span className="text-rose-600">{formatted} (expirÃ©e)</span>;
        }
        if (days !== null && days >= 0 && days <= 30) {
          return <span className="text-amber-600">{formatted} (bientÃ´t)</span>;
        }
        return formatted;
      },
    },
  ],

      UNDER_MAINTENANCE: { label: "En maintenance", color: "warning" },
      OUT_OF_SERVICE: { label: "Hors service", color: "default" },
    },
  },
  canCreate: (role) => role === Role.ADMIN || role === Role.SYNDIC,
  canEdit: (_item, role) => role === Role.ADMIN || role === Role.SYNDIC,
  canDelete: (_item, role) => role === Role.ADMIN || role === Role.SYNDIC,

=== FILE: C:\atelier_nextJs\syndicpro\lib\resources\types.ts ===
import React from "react";

export type FieldType =
  | "text"
  | "number"
  | "date"
  | "select"
  | "textarea"
  | "relation"
  | "boolean";

export interface FieldOption {
  label: string;
  value: string | number | boolean;
}

export interface RelationConfig {
  resource: string; // Ex: "residences", "apartments", "users"
  apiPath?: string; // Ex: "/api/residences" (par dÃ©faut `/api/${resource}`)
  labelField: string; // Champ affichÃ©, ex: "name" ou "title"
  valueField?: string; // Champ clÃ©, ex: "_id" (par dÃ©faut "_id")
}

export interface ResourceField {
  name: string;
  label: string;
  type: FieldType;
  required?: boolean;
  defaultValue?: any;
  placeholder?: string;
  options?: FieldOption[];
  relation?: RelationConfig;
  readOnly?: boolean;
  helpText?: string;
  min?: number;
  max?: number;
}

export interface ListColumn<T = any> {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  type?: "text" | "number" | "date" | "badge" | "boolean" | "relation";
  render?: (value: any, item: T) => React.ReactNode;
}

export interface BadgeFieldConfig {
  name: string;
  variantMap?: Record<
    string,
    {
      label: string;
      color?: "default" | "success" | "warning" | "danger" | "info";
      className?: string;
    }
  >;
}

export interface FilterDef {
  key: string; // nom du paramÃ¨tre de requÃªte (ex "status")
  label: string; // libellÃ© franÃ§ais
  type: "select" | "boolean" | "month" | "dateRange";
  options?: { value: string; label: string }[]; // pour "select"
}
// "boolean" : valeurs "true" / "false". "month" : valeur "YYYY-MM".
// "dateRange" : utilise les deux clÃ©s fixes "from" et "to" (YYYY-MM-DD) ; key sert d'identifiant.

export interface SortOption {
  value: string;
  label: string;
} // value = "champ:asc" ou "champ:desc"

export interface RowLink {
  label: string;
  href: (item: any) => string;
  roles?: string[]; // rÃ´les autorisÃ©s ; absent = tous
}

export interface ResourceConfig<T = any> {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  plural: string;
  apiPath: string;
  entityOfflineKey?: "announcement" | "incident" | "payment" | "charge" | "residence" | "apartment";

  fields: ResourceField[];
  listColumns: ListColumn<T>[];
  cardTitle: (item: T) => string;
  cardSubtitle: (item: T) => string;
  badgeField?: BadgeFieldConfig;
  searchPlaceholder?: string;
  defaultSort?: string;
  canCreate?: (userRole?: string) => boolean;
  canEdit?: (item: T, userRole?: string) => boolean;
  canDelete?: (item: T, userRole?: string) => boolean;
  filterDefs?: FilterDef[];
  sortOptions?: SortOption[];
  exportPath?: string; // ex "/api/payments/export"
  rowLinks?: RowLink[];
}

    .string()
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .email("Adresse email invalide"),
  password: z
    .string()
    .min(8, "Le mot de passe doit comporter au moins 8 caractÃ¨res"),
});

/**
 * SchÃ©ma de validation pour l'inscription publique d'un rÃ©sident.
 * Exige au moins 8 caractÃ¨res, 1 lettre et 1 chiffre.
 */
export const registerSchema = z
  .object({
    name: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .min(2, "Le nom doit comporter au moins 2 caractÃ¨res"),
    email: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase()
      .email("Adresse email invalide"),
    password: z
      .string()
      .min(8, "Le mot de passe doit comporter au moins 8 caractÃ¨res")
      .regex(/[a-zA-Z]/, "Le mot de passe doit contenir au moins une lettre")
      .regex(/[0-9]/, "Le mot de passe doit contenir au moins un chiffre"),
    confirmPassword: z.string(),
    phone: z.string().trim().optional(),
  })

  });

  residence: objectId,
  employee: objectId,
  startAt: date,
  endAt: date,
  mission: z.string().trim().max(120).optional(),

  notes: z.string().trim().max(500).optional(),
});

  !v.startAt ||

  path: ["endAt"],
};

  .omit({ residence: true, employee: true })
  .partial()
  .refine(shiftTimeRule, shiftTimeError);

});

  !v.startDate || !v.endDate || v.endDate >= v.startDate;

  message: "La fin du congÃ© prÃ©cÃ¨de son dÃ©but",
  path: ["endDate"],
};

export const leaveCreateSchema = leaveBase.refine(leaveRule, leaveError);

import { connection, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { z, ZodError } from "zod";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db";
import "@/models";

/** Forme standard d'une erreur renvoyÃ©e par l'API. */
export interface ApiErrorBody {
  error: string;
  details?: unknown;
}

/** Erreur mÃ©tier avec code HTTP explicite (ex : 404 ressource introuvable). */
export class HttpError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public status: number,
    message: string,
    public details?: unknown
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export function json<T>(data: T, status = 200) {
  return NextResponse.json<T>(data, { status });
}

/** Lit et valide le corps JSON d'une requÃªte avec un schÃ©ma Zod. */
export async function parseBody<S extends z.ZodType>(req: Request, schema: S): Promise<z.infer<S>> {
  let raw: unknown;
  try {
    raw = await req.json();
  } catch {
    throw new HttpError(400, "Corps JSON invalide");
  }

  // Les formulaires envoient "" pour un champ facultatif laissÃ© vide : on le traite
  // comme un champ absent pour que la validation Zod ne le rejette pas.
  if (raw && typeof raw === "object" && !Array.isArray(raw)) {
    raw = Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(raw as Record<string, unknown>).filter(([, value]) => value !== "")
    );
  }
  return schema.parse(raw);
}

export function isValidObjectId(id: string | null | undefined): id is string {
  return !!id && /^[a-f\d]{24}$/i.test(id);
}

/** Erreur MongoDB "duplicate key" (index unique violÃ©). */
export function isDuplicateKeyError(err: unknown): err is { code: 11000; keyPattern?: Record<string, number> } {
  return typeof err === "object" && err !== null && (err as { code?: number }).code === 11000;
}

function toErrorResponse(err: unknown) {
  if (err instanceof HttpError) {
    return json<ApiErrorBody>({ error: err.message, details: err.details }, err.status);
  }
  if (err instanceof ZodError) {
    return json<ApiErrorBody>({ error: "DonnÃ©es invalides", details: z.flattenError(err) }, 400);
  }
  if (err instanceof mongoose.Error.ValidationError || err instanceof mongoose.Error.CastError) {
    return json<ApiErrorBody>({ error: "DonnÃ©es invalides", details: err.message }, 400);
  }
  if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
    return json<ApiErrorBody>({ error: "Ressource dÃ©jÃ  existante", details: err.keyPattern }, 409);
  }
  console.error("[API]", err);
  return json<ApiErrorBody>({ error: "Erreur interne du serveur" }, 500);
}

/**
 * Enveloppe commune des handlers :
 *  1. `connection()` : avec cacheComponents, indique Ã  Next que la rÃ©ponse dÃ©pend
 *     de la requÃªte -> pas de prÃ©rendu au build (sinon il tenterait d'interroger MongoDB).
 *     AppelÃ© hors du try pour ne pas intercepter le signal interne de Next.
 *  2. connexion MongoDB (mise en cache par lib/db.ts)
 *  3. conversion des erreurs en rÃ©ponses 400 / 404 / 409 / 500
 */
export async function handle(fn: () => Promise<Response>): Promise<Response> {
  await connection();
  try {
    await connectDB();
    return await fn();
  } catch (err) {
    return toErrorResponse(err);
  }
}

import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { auth } from "@/auth";
import { HttpError, handle } from "@/lib/api";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";
import { Apartment } from "@/models/Apartment";
import { Resident } from "@/models/Resident";
import { Residence } from "@/models/Residence";
import { Incident } from "@/models/Incident";

export interface AuthUser {
  id: string;
  name?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
  role: Role;
}

/**
 * RÃ©cupÃ¨re l'utilisateur connectÃ© via la session JWT Auth.js.
 * LÃ¨ve une exception HttpError(401) si l'utilisateur n'est pas authentifiÃ©.
 */
export async function requireAuth(): Promise<AuthUser> {
  const session = await auth();
  if (!session?.user?.id) {
    throw new HttpError(401, "Authentification requise");
  }
  return {
    id: session.user.id,
    name: session.user.name,
    email: session.user.email,
    role: session.user.role as Role,
  };
}

/**
 * VÃ©rifie que le rÃ´le de l'utilisateur fait partie des rÃ´les autorisÃ©s.
 * LÃ¨ve une exception HttpError(403) si le rÃ´le est insuffisant.
 */
export function requireRole(user: AuthUser, ...roles: Role[]): void {
  if (!roles.includes(user.role)) {
    throw new HttpError(403, "AccÃ¨s interdit : rÃ´le insuffisant");
  }
}

/**
 * Wrapper de route API avec authentification et contrÃ´le de rÃ´le automatique.
 */
export function withAuth(
  handler: (req: NextRequest, user: AuthUser, context?: any) => Promise<Response>,
  options?: { roles?: Role[] }
) {
  return async (req: NextRequest, context?: any): Promise<Response> => {
    return handle(async () => {
      const user = await requireAuth();
      if (options?.roles && options.roles.length > 0) {
        requireRole(user, ...options.roles);
      }
      return handler(req, user, context);
    });
  };
}

/**
 * RÃ©cupÃ¨re les identifiants d'appartements associÃ©s Ã  un rÃ©sident
 * (soit en tant que propriÃ©taire, soit en tant que rÃ©sident enregistrÃ©).
 */
export async function getResidentApartmentIds(userId: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const [owned, residentIn] = await Promise.all([
    Apartment.find({ owner: userId }).distinct("_id"),
    Resident.find({ user: userId, isActive: true }).distinct("apartment"),
  ]);
  const allIds = new Set<string>([
    ...owned.map((id) => id.toString()),
    ...residentIn.map((id) => id.toString()),
  ]);
  return Array.from(allIds);
}

export async function getSyndicResidenceIds(userId: string): Promise<string[]> {

  return residences.map((id) => id.toString());
}

/**

 */
export async function getResidentResidenceIds(userId: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const aptIds = await getResidentApartmentIds(userId);
  const residences = await Apartment.find({ _id: { $in: aptIds } }).distinct("residence");
  return residences.map((id) => id.toString());
}

 */

  return residences.map((id) => id.toString());

import mongoose from "mongoose";

const MONGODB_URI = process.env.MONGODB_URI;

if (!MONGODB_URI) {
  throw new Error("MONGODB_URI manquant dans .env.local");
}

type MongooseCache = {
  conn: typeof mongoose | null;
  promise: Promise<typeof mongoose> | null;
};

const globalWithMongoose = global as typeof globalThis & {
  mongooseCache?: MongooseCache;
};

const cached: MongooseCache = (globalWithMongoose.mongooseCache ??= {
  conn: null,
  promise: null,
});

export async function connectDB() {
  if (cached.conn) return cached.conn;

  cached.promise ??= mongoose.connect(MONGODB_URI!, {
    bufferCommands: false,
  });

  cached.conn = await cached.promise;
  return cached.conn;
}

const SALT_ROUNDS = 10;

/**
 * Hache un mot de passe en clair avec bcryptjs (10 tours de sel).

/**
 * VÃ©rifie un mot de passe en clair contre un hash bcrypt.
 */
export async function verifyPassword(password: string, hash: string): Promise<boolean> {
