  "/api-docs": [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
  "/api/openapi": [Role.ADMIN, Role.SYNDIC],
};

/**
 * Types d'actions et ressources pour la matrice de contrÃ´le d'accÃ¨s.
 */
export type Action = "create" | "read" | "update" | "delete";
export type Resource =
  | "residence"
  | "apartment"
  | "resident"
  | "expense"
  | "user"
  | "charge"
  | "payment"
  | "incident"
  | "announcement"
  | "meeting"
  | "resolution"
  | "document"
  | "vote";


/**
 * VÃ©rifie si un rÃ´le donnÃ© a la permission d'effectuer une action sur une ressource.
 */
export function can(role: Role, action: Action, resource: Resource): boolean {
  // L'ADMIN a un accÃ¨s complet sans restriction
  if (role === Role.ADMIN) return true;

  switch (resource) {
    case "residence":
    case "apartment":
    case "resident":
    case "expense":
      // Le SYNDIC a tous les droits sur les rÃ©sidences qu'il gÃ¨re
      return role === Role.SYNDIC;

    case "user":
      // ADMIN gÃ¨re tous les utilisateurs, SYNDIC peut voir et crÃ©er des techniciens ou rÃ©sidents
      if (role === Role.SYNDIC) {
        return action === "read" || action === "create" || action === "update";
      }
      return false;

    case "charge":
    case "payment":
      if (role === Role.SYNDIC) return true;
      if (role === Role.RESIDENT) {
        // Le rÃ©sident peut uniquement consulter ses charges et effectuer un paiement
        return action === "read" || action === "create";
      }
      return false;

    case "incident":
      if (role === Role.SYNDIC) return true;
      if (role === Role.RESIDENT) {
        // Le rÃ©sident peut dÃ©clarer et lire ses incidents
        return action === "create" || action === "read";
      }
      if (role === Role.TECHNICIAN) {
        // Le technicien lit et met Ã  jour le statut des incidents qui lui sont assignÃ©s
        return action === "read" || action === "update";
      }
      return false;

    case "announcement":
      if (role === Role.SYNDIC) return true;
      // RÃ©sident et technicien sont en lecture seule pour les annonces
      return action === "read";

    case "meeting":
    case "resolution":
    case "vote":
      if (role === Role.SYNDIC) return true;
      if (role === Role.RESIDENT) {
        return action === "read" || (resource === "vote" && action === "create");
      }
      return false;

    case "document":
      if (role === Role.SYNDIC) return true;
      if (role === Role.RESIDENT) {
        return action === "read";
      }
      return false;


    default:
      return false;
  }
}

/**
 * VÃ©rifie si un chemin d'URL est autorisÃ© pour un rÃ´le donnÃ©.
 */
export function isRouteAllowed(pathname: string, role: Role): boolean {
  // Recherche de la correspondance du prÃ©fixe le plus prÃ©cis
  const matchingPrefix = Object.keys(ROUTE_ACCESS)
    .filter((prefix) => pathname === prefix || pathname.startsWith(`${prefix}/`))
    .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length)[0];

  if (!matchingPrefix) {
    // Si la route n'est pas restreinte dans la table, accÃ¨s accordÃ© par dÃ©faut
    return true;
  }

  return ROUTE_ACCESS[matchingPrefix].includes(role);
}
=== FILE: C:\atelier_nextJs\syndicpro\lib\rate-limit.ts ===
/**
 * Limiteur de tentatives de connexion en mÃ©moire vive (In-Memory).
 * Note importante d'architecture : Cette limitation rÃ©side dans la mÃ©moire du processus Node.js.
 * Elle est adaptÃ©e pour une instance unique ou le dÃ©veloppement, mais n'est pas partagÃ©e
 * entre plusieurs instances scalÃ©es horizontalement (pour un dÃ©ploiement multi-instances,
 * un magasin distribuÃ© tel que Redis ou MongoDB TTL index serait prÃ©conisÃ©).
 */

interface AttemptRecord {
  count: number;
  blockedUntil: number | null;
  firstAttempt: number;
}

const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5;
const BLOCK_DURATION_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes
const WINDOW_DURATION_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes

const attemptsMap = new Map<string, AttemptRecord>();

export function checkRateLimit(key: string): { allowed: boolean; retryAfterSeconds?: number } {
  const now = Date.now();
  const record = attemptsMap.get(key);

  if (!record) {
    return { allowed: true };
  }

  // Si le compte/IP est temporairement bloquÃ©
  if (record.blockedUntil && record.blockedUntil > now) {
    const retryAfterSeconds = Math.ceil((record.blockedUntil - now) / 1000);
    return { allowed: false, retryAfterSeconds };
  }

  // Si la fenÃªtre de temps est expirÃ©e, on rÃ©initialise
  if (now - record.firstAttempt > WINDOW_DURATION_MS) {
    attemptsMap.delete(key);
    return { allowed: true };
  }

  return { allowed: true };
}

export function recordFailedAttempt(key: string): void {
  const now = Date.now();
  const record = attemptsMap.get(key);

  if (!record || now - record.firstAttempt > WINDOW_DURATION_MS) {
    attemptsMap.set(key, {
      count: 1,
      blockedUntil: null,
      firstAttempt: now,
    });
    return;
  }

  record.count += 1;
  if (record.count >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
    record.blockedUntil = now + BLOCK_DURATION_MS;
  }
}

export function resetRateLimit(key: string): void {
  attemptsMap.delete(key);
}

import mongoose, { Schema, model, type Model, type Types } from "mongoose";

export interface IApartment {
  residence: Types.ObjectId;
  number: string;
  floor: number;
  surface: number;
  /** TantiÃ¨mes (quote-part des parties communes) */
  shares: number;
  isOccupied: boolean;
  owner?: Types.ObjectId;
}

interface ApartmentStatics {
  /**
   * Charge mensuelle d'un lot = totalBudget * tantiÃ¨mes du lot / somme des tantiÃ¨mes de la rÃ©sidence.
   * Statique async car il faut interroger tous les lots de la rÃ©sidence.
   */
  calculateMonthlyCharge(apartmentId: Types.ObjectId | string, totalBudget: number): Promise<number>;
  /** Somme des tantiÃ¨mes d'une rÃ©sidence. */
  totalShares(residenceId: Types.ObjectId | string): Promise<number>;
}

export type ApartmentModel = Model<IApartment> & ApartmentStatics;

const ApartmentSchema = new Schema<IApartment, ApartmentModel, {}, {}, {}, ApartmentStatics>(
  {
    residence: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Residence", required: true },
    number: { type: String, required: true, trim: true },
    floor: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0 },
    surface: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0 },
    shares: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0 },
    isOccupied: { type: Boolean, default: false },
    owner: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "User" },
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

// Un numÃ©ro de lot est unique au sein d'une rÃ©sidence
ApartmentSchema.index({ residence: 1, number: 1 }, { unique: true });

ApartmentSchema.static("totalShares", async function (residenceId: Types.ObjectId | string) {
  const [res] = await Apartment.aggregate<{ total: number }>([
    { $match: { residence: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(String(residenceId)) } },
    { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: "$shares" } } },
  ]);
  return res?.total ?? 0;
});

ApartmentSchema.static(
  "calculateMonthlyCharge",
  async function (apartmentId: Types.ObjectId | string, totalBudget: number) {
    const apt = await Apartment.findById(apartmentId).lean();
    if (!apt) throw new Error("Appartement introuvable");
    const total = await Apartment.totalShares(apt.residence);
    if (total === 0) return 0;
    // Arrondi au centime
    return Math.round(((totalBudget * apt.shares) / total) * 100) / 100;
  }
);

export const Apartment =
  (mongoose.models.Apartment as ApartmentModel) ||
  model<IApartment, ApartmentModel>("Apartment", ApartmentSchema);

import mongoose, { Schema, model, type Model, type Types, type HydratedDocument } from "mongoose";
import { ChargeStatus, values } from "@/types/enums";
import "./Apartment";

export interface ICharge {
  apartment: Types.ObjectId;
  /** PÃ©riode au format "YYYY-MM" */
  period: string;
  amount: number;
  paidAmount: number;
  dueDate: Date;
  status: ChargeStatus;
}

export interface IChargeMethods {
  getRemainingAmount(): number;
  isOverdue(now?: Date): boolean;
  /** Recalcule le statut Ã  partir de paidAmount / amount / dueDate (sans sauvegarder). */
  updateStatus(now?: Date): ChargeStatus;
}

export type ChargeModel = Model<ICharge, {}, IChargeMethods>;
export type ChargeDocument = HydratedDocument<ICharge, IChargeMethods>;

/**
 * RÃ¨gle de statut, extraite en fonction pure pour pouvoir Ãªtre rÃ©utilisÃ©e
 * (mÃ©thode d'instance, seed, UI).
 */
export function computeChargeStatus(
  c: Pick<ICharge, "amount" | "paidAmount" | "dueDate">,
  now: Date = new Date()
): ChargeStatus {
  if (c.paidAmount >= c.amount) return ChargeStatus.PAID;
  if (c.paidAmount > 0) return ChargeStatus.PARTIAL;
  if (new Date(c.dueDate).getTime() < now.getTime()) return ChargeStatus.OVERDUE;
  return ChargeStatus.PENDING;
}

const ChargeSchema = new Schema<ICharge, ChargeModel, IChargeMethods>(
  {
    apartment: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Apartment", required: true },
    period: { type: String, required: true, match: /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/ },
    amount: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0 },
    paidAmount: { type: Number, default: 0, min: 0 },
    dueDate: { type: Date, required: true },
    status: { type: String, enum: values(ChargeStatus), default: ChargeStatus.PENDING },
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

// Une seule charge par lot et par mois
ChargeSchema.index({ apartment: 1, period: 1 }, { unique: true });
ChargeSchema.index({ status: 1 });

ChargeSchema.method("getRemainingAmount", function () {
  return Math.max(0, this.amount - this.paidAmount);
});

ChargeSchema.method("isOverdue", function (now: Date = new Date()) {
  return this.dueDate.getTime() < now.getTime() && this.paidAmount < this.amount;
});

ChargeSchema.method("updateStatus", function (now: Date = new Date()) {
  this.status = computeChargeStatus(this, now);
  return this.status;
});

// Le statut est toujours recalculÃ© avant sauvegarde : il ne peut pas diverger des montants
ChargeSchema.pre("save", function () {
  this.status = computeChargeStatus(this);
});

export const Charge =
  (mongoose.models.Charge as ChargeModel) || model<ICharge, ChargeModel>("Charge", ChargeSchema);

import mongoose, { Schema, model, type Model, type Types } from "mongoose";
import { MeetingStatus, values } from "@/types/enums";
import { Resolution } from "./Resolution";
import { Vote } from "./Vote";
import { Apartment } from "./Apartment";

export interface IMeeting {
  residence: Types.ObjectId;
  title: string;
  date: Date;
  location?: string;
  agenda?: string;
  status: MeetingStatus;
}

export interface IMeetingMethods {
  /**
   * Quorum atteint si la somme des tantiÃ¨mes des lots ayant votÃ© Ã  au moins
   * une rÃ©solution de l'AG dÃ©passe 50 % des tantiÃ¨mes de la rÃ©sidence.
   * HypothÃ¨se : "prÃ©sent" = a votÃ© (pas de feuille de prÃ©sence dans le modÃ¨le).
   */
  isQuorumReached(): Promise<boolean>;
}

export type MeetingModel = Model<IMeeting, {}, IMeetingMethods>;

const MeetingSchema = new Schema<IMeeting, MeetingModel, IMeetingMethods>(
  {
    residence: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Residence", required: true },
    title: { type: String, required: true, trim: true },
    date: { type: Date, required: true },
    location: { type: String },
    agenda: { type: String },
    status: { type: String, enum: values(MeetingStatus), default: MeetingStatus.PLANNED },
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

MeetingSchema.index({ residence: 1, date: -1 });

MeetingSchema.method("isQuorumReached", async function () {
  const resolutionIds = await Resolution.find({ meeting: this._id }).distinct("_id");
  const apartmentIds = await Vote.find({ resolution: { $in: resolutionIds } }).distinct("apartment");

  const [present] = await Apartment.aggregate<{ total: number }>([
    { $match: { _id: { $in: apartmentIds } } },
    { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: "$shares" } } },
  ]);
  const total = await Apartment.totalShares(this.residence);
  if (total === 0) return false;
  return (present?.total ?? 0) / total > 0.5;
});

export const Meeting =
  (mongoose.models.Meeting as MeetingModel) ||
  model<IMeeting, MeetingModel>("Meeting", MeetingSchema);

import mongoose, { Schema, model, type Model, type Types } from "mongoose";
import { PaymentMethod, values } from "@/types/enums";
import { Charge } from "./Charge";

export interface IPayment {
  charge: Types.ObjectId;
  receivedBy?: Types.ObjectId;
  amount: number;
  paymentDate: Date;
  method: PaymentMethod;
  receiptNumber: string;
  /**
   * Identifiant gÃ©nÃ©rÃ© cÃ´tÃ© client (uuid) : permet de rejouer une requÃªte
   * depuis la file offline sans crÃ©er de doublon (idempotence).
   */
  clientId?: string;
  /** Date de rÃ©ception par le serveur (utile pour tracer les synchros offline). */
  syncedAt?: Date;
}

export type PaymentModel = Model<IPayment>;

const PaymentSchema = new Schema<IPayment, PaymentModel>(
  {
    charge: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Charge", required: true },
    receivedBy: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "User" },
    amount: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0.01 },
    paymentDate: { type: Date, default: () => new Date() },
    method: { type: String, enum: values(PaymentMethod), required: true },
    receiptNumber: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
    // sparse : l'unicitÃ© ne s'applique qu'aux documents qui ont un clientId
    clientId: { type: String, unique: true, sparse: true },
    syncedAt: { type: Date },
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

PaymentSchema.index({ charge: 1 });

/** GÃ©nÃ¨re un numÃ©ro de reÃ§u lisible si absent : REC-AAAAMMJJ-XXXXXX */
PaymentSchema.pre("validate", function () {
  if (!this.receiptNumber) {
    const d = new Date();
    const ymd = d.toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, "");
    const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8).toUpperCase();
    this.receiptNumber = `REC-${ymd}-${rand}`;
  }
});

/**
 * Hook post-save : met Ã  jour la charge liÃ©e.
 * On RECALCULE paidAmount comme la somme de tous les paiements de la charge
 * (au lieu d'incrÃ©menter) : le rÃ©sultat reste juste mÃªme si le hook est rejouÃ©.
 */
PaymentSchema.post("save", async function (doc) {
  const [agg] = await Payment.aggregate<{ total: number }>([
    { $match: { charge: doc.charge } },
    { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: "$amount" } } },
  ]);
  const charge = await Charge.findById(doc.charge);
  if (!charge) return;
  charge.paidAmount = Math.round((agg?.total ?? 0) * 100) / 100;
  charge.updateStatus();
  await charge.save();
});

  (mongoose.models.Payment as PaymentModel) ||
  model<IPayment, PaymentModel>("Payment", PaymentSchema);
=== FILE: C:\atelier_nextJs\syndicpro\models\Residence.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, model, type Model, type Types } from "mongoose";
import { IncidentStatus } from "@/types/enums";
import { Apartment } from "./Apartment";
import { Charge } from "./Charge";
import { Payment } from "./Payment";
import { Expense } from "./Expense";
import { Incident, type IncidentDocument } from "./Incident";

export interface IResidence {
  name: string;
  address: string;
  city: string;
  floors: number;
  totalApartments: number;
  syndic?: Types.ObjectId;
}

type Id = Types.ObjectId | string;

export interface ResidenceStatics {
  /** % du montant appelÃ© effectivement encaissÃ© sur une pÃ©riode "YYYY-MM". */
  getCollectionRate(residenceId: Id, period: string): Promise<number>;
  /** TrÃ©sorerie = somme des paiements - somme des dÃ©penses. */
  getBalance(residenceId: Id): Promise<{ payments: number; expenses: number; balance: number }>;
  /** Incidents non rÃ©solus, du plus rÃ©cent au plus ancien. */
  getOpenIncidents(residenceId: Id): Promise<IncidentDocument[]>;
}

export type ResidenceModel = Model<IResidence> & ResidenceStatics;

const ResidenceSchema = new Schema<IResidence, ResidenceModel, {}, {}, {}, ResidenceStatics>(
  {
    name: { type: String, required: true, trim: true },
    address: { type: String, required: true, trim: true },
    city: { type: String, required: true, trim: true },
    floors: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0 },
    totalApartments: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0 },
    syndic: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "User" },
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

const toId = (id: Id) => new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(String(id));

/** Liste des _id d'appartements d'une rÃ©sidence (charges/paiements y sont rattachÃ©s indirectement). */
async function apartmentIds(residenceId: Id) {
  return Apartment.find({ residence: toId(residenceId) }).distinct("_id");
}

ResidenceSchema.static("getCollectionRate", async function (residenceId: Id, period: string) {
  const ids = await apartmentIds(residenceId);
  const [agg] = await Charge.aggregate<{ amount: number; paid: number }>([
    { $match: { apartment: { $in: ids }, period } },
    { $group: { _id: null, amount: { $sum: "$amount" }, paid: { $sum: "$paidAmount" } } },
  ]);
  if (!agg || agg.amount === 0) return 0;
  return Math.round((Math.min(agg.paid, agg.amount) / agg.amount) * 1000) / 10;
});

ResidenceSchema.static("getBalance", async function (residenceId: Id) {
  const ids = await apartmentIds(residenceId);
  const chargeIds = await Charge.find({ apartment: { $in: ids } }).distinct("_id");
  const [[p], [e]] = await Promise.all([
    Payment.aggregate<{ total: number }>([
      { $match: { charge: { $in: chargeIds } } },
      { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: "$amount" } } },
    ]),
    Expense.aggregate<{ total: number }>([
      { $match: { residence: toId(residenceId) } },
      { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: "$amount" } } },
    ]),
  ]);
  const payments = p?.total ?? 0;
  const expenses = e?.total ?? 0;
  return { payments, expenses, balance: Math.round((payments - expenses) * 100) / 100 };
});

ResidenceSchema.static("getOpenIncidents", async function (residenceId: Id) {
  return Incident.find({
    residence: toId(residenceId),
    status: { $ne: IncidentStatus.RESOLVED },
  }).sort({ reportedAt: -1 });
});

export const Residence =
  (mongoose.models.Residence as ResidenceModel) ||
  model<IResidence, ResidenceModel>("Residence", ResidenceSchema);

import { VoteChoice } from "@/types/enums";
import { Vote } from "./Vote";

export interface IResolution {
  meeting: Types.ObjectId;

  description?: string;
  /** MajoritÃ© requise en % des voix exprimÃ©es (FOR + AGAINST) */
  requiredMajority: number;

export interface IResolutionMethods {
  /** Somme des poids (tantiÃ¨mes) par choix. */
  countVotes(): Promise<VoteTally>;
  /** FOR / (FOR + AGAINST) * 100 >= requiredMajority. Les abstentions ne comptent pas. */
  isAdopted(): Promise<boolean>;

    title: { type: String, required: true, trim: true },

    requiredMajority: { type: Number, default: 50, min: 0, max: 100 },
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

ResolutionSchema.index({ meeting: 1 });

ResolutionSchema.method("countVotes", async function () {
  const rows = await Vote.aggregate<{ _id: VoteChoice; total: number }>([
    { $match: { resolution: this._id } },
    { $group: { _id: "$choice", total: { $sum: "$weight" } } },
  ]);
  const tally: VoteTally = { FOR: 0, AGAINST: 0, ABSTAIN: 0 };
  for (const r of rows) tally[r._id] = r.total;
  return tally;
});

ResolutionSchema.method("isAdopted", async function () {
  const t = await this.countVotes();
  const expressed = t.FOR + t.AGAINST;
  if (expressed === 0) return false;
  return (t.FOR / expressed) * 100 >= this.requiredMajority;
});

export const Resolution =
  (mongoose.models.Resolution as ResolutionModel) ||
  model<IResolution, ResolutionModel>("Resolution", ResolutionSchema);

  createdAt?: Date;

  {
    residence: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Residence", required: true },
    employee: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Employee", required: true },
    startAt: { type: Date, required: true },
    endAt: {
      type: Date,
      required: true,
      validate: {
        validator: function (this: unknown, value: Date): boolean {
          const doc = this as { startAt?: Date } | null;
          // Sur une requÃªte de mise Ã  jour, "this" n'est pas un document : on laisse passer
          // (la validation Zod de l'API contrÃ´le ce cas).
          if (!doc || !doc.startAt || !(value instanceof Date)) return true;
          return value.getTime() > new Date(doc.startAt).getTime();
        },
        message: "L'heure de fin doit suivre l'heure de dÃ©but",
      },
    },
    mission: { type: String, trim: true },
    status: {
      type: String,
      enum: values(ShiftStatus),
      default: ShiftStatus.PLANNED,
    },
    notes: { type: String },
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

export const Shift =
  (mongoose.models.Shift as ShiftModel) ||

/**
 * Ã‰numÃ©rations mÃ©tier de SyndicPro.
 * Pattern "as const" : un seul objet sert de source de vÃ©ritÃ© pour
 * Mongoose (enum: Object.values(X)), Zod (z.enum(values(X))) et l'UI (libellÃ©s).
 */

/** Renvoie les valeurs d'un enum sous forme de tuple non vide (requis par z.enum). */
export function values<T extends Record<string, string>>(e: T) {
  return Object.values(e) as [T[keyof T], ...T[keyof T][]];
}

"use client";

import React, { useEffect } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";

export interface ConfirmDialogProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  title?: string;
  message?: string;
  confirmLabel?: string;
  cancelLabel?: string;
  isDestructive?: boolean;
  loading?: boolean;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export function ConfirmDialog({
  isOpen,
  title = "Confirmer l'action",
  message = "ÃŠtes-vous sÃ»r de vouloir continuer ? Cette action est irrÃ©versible.",
  confirmLabel = "Confirmer",
  cancelLabel = "Annuler",
  isDestructive = true,
  loading = false,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
}: ConfirmDialogProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onCancel();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onCancel]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      {/* ArriÃ¨re-plan floutÃ© */}
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-950/60 backdrop-blur-sm transition-opacity"
        onClick={onCancel}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      {/* Conteneur modal tactile */}
      <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl transition-all dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
          <div
            className={`flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl ${
              isDestructive
                ? "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 dark:bg-rose-500/20 dark:text-rose-400"
                : "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-400"
            }`}
          >
            <AlertTriangle className="h-6 w-6 stroke-[2]" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{title}</h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{message}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            onClick={onCancel}
            disabled={loading}
            className="min-h-[44px] w-full sm:w-auto"
          >
            {cancelLabel}
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant={isDestructive ? "danger" : "primary"}
            onClick={onConfirm}
            isLoading={loading}
            className="min-h-[44px] w-full sm:w-auto"
          >
            {confirmLabel}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Search, Plus, X, SlidersHorizontal, ArrowUpDown } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";

type FilterDef = {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  type: "select" | "boolean" | "month" | "dateRange";
  options?: { value: string; label: string }[];
};

type SortOption = {
  value: string;
  label: string;
};

export interface FilterBarProps {
  search: string;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  searchPlaceholder?: string;
  canCreate?: boolean;
  createLabel?: string;
  onCreateClick?: () => void;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  defs?: FilterDef[];
  values?: Record<string, string>;
  onValuesChange?: (v: Record<string, string>) => void;
  sortOptions?: SortOption[];
  sort?: string;
  onSortChange?: (s: string) => void;
}

// Met Ã  jour une clÃ© dans un objet de valeurs en supprimant les clÃ©s vides
function updateValueMap(
  current: Record<string, string>,
  key: string,
  val: string
): Record<string, string> {
  const next = { ...current };
  if (!val || val.trim() === "") {
    delete next[key];
  } else {
    next[key] = val;
  }
  return next;
}

export function FilterBar({
  search,
  onSearchChange,
  searchPlaceholder = "Rechercher...",
  canCreate = false,
  createLabel = "Nouveau",
  onCreateClick,
  children,
  defs,
  values = {},
  onValuesChange,
  sortOptions,
  sort = "",
  onSortChange,
}: FilterBarProps) {
  const [isMobileSheetOpen, setIsMobileSheetOpen] = useState(false);
  const [draftValues, setDraftValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [draftSort, setDraftSort] = useState<string>("");

  const hasDefs = Array.isArray(defs) && defs.length > 0;
  const hasSortOptions = Array.isArray(sortOptions) && sortOptions.length > 0;

  // Construction des puces (chips) de filtres actifs
  const activeChips: { key: string; label: string; displayValue: string }[] = [];
  if (hasDefs && defs) {
    for (const def of defs) {
      if (def.type === "dateRange") {
        if (values.from) {
          activeChips.push({
            key: "from",
            label: `${def.label} (du)`,
            displayValue: values.from,
          });
        }
        if (values.to) {
          activeChips.push({
            key: "to",
            label: `${def.label} (au)`,
            displayValue: values.to,
          });
        }
      } else {
        const rawVal = values[def.key];
        if (rawVal !== undefined && rawVal !== "") {
          let displayValue = rawVal;
          if (def.type === "boolean") {
            displayValue = rawVal === "true" ? "Oui" : "Non";
          } else if (def.type === "select" && def.options) {
            const found = def.options.find((o) => o.value === rawVal);
            if (found) displayValue = found.label;
          }
          activeChips.push({
            key: def.key,
            label: def.label,
            displayValue,
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const activeCount = activeChips.length;

  // Ouverture du panneau mobile avec copie de l'Ã©tat courant
  const openMobileSheet = () => {
    setDraftValues({ ...values });
    setDraftSort(sort || "");
    setIsMobileSheetOpen(true);
  };

  // Application des filtres du panneau mobile
  const applyMobileSheet = () => {
    onValuesChange?.(draftValues);
    if (hasSortOptions && onSortChange) {
      onSortChange(draftSort);
    }
    setIsMobileSheetOpen(false);
  };

  // RÃ©initialisation dans le panneau mobile
  const resetMobileSheet = () => {
    setDraftValues({});
    if (hasSortOptions && sortOptions?.[0]) {
      setDraftSort(sortOptions[0].value);
    } else {
      setDraftSort("");
    }
  };

  // Suppression d'un filtre actif via sa puce
  const handleRemoveChip = (chipKey: string) => {
    if (!onValuesChange) return;
    onValuesChange(updateValueMap(values, chipKey, ""));
  };

  // Effacement de tous les filtres actifs
  const handleClearAll = () => {
    onValuesChange?.({});
  };

  const controlClassName =
    "min-h-[44px] rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-base text-slate-900 focus:border-sky-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sky-500/20 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/60 dark:text-white";

  // Rendu d'un contrÃ´le de filtre unitaire (utilisÃ© en desktop et en mobile)
  const renderFilterControl = (
    def: FilterDef,
    currentValues: Record<string, string>,
    onChangeMap: (next: Record<string, string>) => void,
    fullWidth = false
  ) => {
    const widthClass = fullWidth ? "w-full" : "w-auto";

    if (def.type === "select") {
      return (
        <div key={def.key} className={`flex flex-col gap-1 ${widthClass}`}>
          <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            {def.label}
          </label>
          <select
            aria-label={def.label}
            value={currentValues[def.key] || ""}
            onChange={(e) =>
              onChangeMap(updateValueMap(currentValues, def.key, e.target.value))
            }
            className={`${controlClassName} ${widthClass}`}
          >
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            {def.options?.map((opt) => (
              <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                {opt.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      );
    }

    if (def.type === "boolean") {
      return (
        <div key={def.key} className={`flex flex-col gap-1 ${widthClass}`}>
          <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            {def.label}
          </label>
          <select
            aria-label={def.label}
            value={currentValues[def.key] || ""}
            onChange={(e) =>
              onChangeMap(updateValueMap(currentValues, def.key, e.target.value))
            }
            className={`${controlClassName} ${widthClass}`}
          >
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option value="true">Oui</option>
            <option value="false">Non</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      );
    }

    if (def.type === "month") {
      return (
        <div key={def.key} className={`flex flex-col gap-1 ${widthClass}`}>
          <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            {def.label}
          </label>
          <input
            type="month"
            aria-label={def.label}
            value={currentValues[def.key] || ""}
            onChange={(e) =>
              onChangeMap(updateValueMap(currentValues, def.key, e.target.value))
            }
            className={`${controlClassName} ${widthClass}`}
          />
        </div>
      );
    }

    if (def.type === "dateRange") {
      return (
        <div
          key={def.key}
          className={`flex flex-col gap-1 ${fullWidth ? "w-full" : "sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:gap-2"}`}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              {def.label} (du)
            </label>
            <input
              type="date"
              aria-label={`${def.label} du`}
              value={currentValues.from || ""}
              onChange={(e) =>
                onChangeMap(updateValueMap(currentValues, "from", e.target.value))
              }
              className={`${controlClassName} ${widthClass}`}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              {def.label} (au)
            </label>
            <input
              type="date"
              aria-label={`${def.label} au`}
              value={currentValues.to || ""}
              onChange={(e) =>
                onChangeMap(updateValueMap(currentValues, "to", e.target.value))
              }
              className={`${controlClassName} ${widthClass}`}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return null;
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        {/* Zone de recherche tactile */}
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3.5 text-slate-400">
            <Search className="h-4 w-4" />
          </div>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => onSearchChange(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={searchPlaceholder}
            className="min-h-[44px] w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white py-2.5 pr-10 pl-10 text-base text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-sky-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sky-500/20 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/60 dark:text-white"
          />
          {search && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSearchChange("")}
              className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 flex min-w-[44px] items-center justify-center pr-3 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-200"
              aria-label="Effacer la recherche"
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Filtres additionnels Ã©ventuels, bouton Filtres mobile & Bouton CrÃ©er */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {(hasDefs || hasSortOptions) && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={openMobileSheet}
              className="inline-flex min-h-[44px] items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-base font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 md:hidden dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/60 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
            >
              <SlidersHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>Filtres</span>
              {activeCount > 0 && (
                <span className="inline-flex h-5 min-w-[20px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-sky-600 px-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
                  {activeCount}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          )}

          {children}

          {canCreate && onCreateClick && (
            <Button
              type="button"
              onClick={onCreateClick}
              size="md"
              className="min-h-[44px] flex-1 gap-2 whitespace-nowrap sm:flex-initial"
            >
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4 stroke-[2.5]" />
              <span>{createLabel}</span>
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Barre de filtres et de tri en ligne sur Desktop (md+) */}
      {(hasDefs || hasSortOptions) && (
        <div className="hidden flex-wrap items-end gap-3 md:flex">
          {hasDefs &&
            defs?.map((def) =>
              renderFilterControl(def, values, (next) => onValuesChange?.(next), false)
            )}

          {hasSortOptions && (
            <div className="ml-auto flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                <ArrowUpDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>Trier par</span>
              </label>
              <select
                aria-label="Trier par"
                value={sort}
                onChange={(e) => onSortChange?.(e.target.value)}
                className={controlClassName}
              >
                {sortOptions?.map((opt) => (
                  <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                    {opt.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Puces (chips) des filtres actifs */}
      {activeChips.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
          {activeChips.map((chip) => (
            <span
              key={chip.key}
              className="inline-flex min-h-[36px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 py-1 pr-1.5 pl-3 text-xs font-medium text-sky-900 dark:border-sky-800/60 dark:bg-sky-950/50 dark:text-sky-200"
            >
              <span>
                {chip.label} : <strong>{chip.displayValue}</strong>
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleRemoveChip(chip.key)}
                aria-label={`Retirer le filtre ${chip.label}`}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[28px] min-w-[28px] items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sky-700 hover:bg-sky-200/60 dark:text-sky-300 dark:hover:bg-sky-900"
              >
                <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </button>
            </span>
          ))}

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleClearAll}
            className="inline-flex min-h-[36px] items-center rounded-lg px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 underline-offset-2 hover:underline dark:text-slate-400"
          >
            Tout effacer
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Panneau infÃ©rieur mobile local (sous md) */}
      {isMobileSheetOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col justify-end bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs md:hidden dark:bg-slate-950/70"
          onClick={() => setIsMobileSheetOpen(false)}
        >
          <div
            className="max-h-[85vh] w-full overflow-y-auto rounded-t-3xl border-t border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 pb-[max(1.25rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] shadow-2xl dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                Filtres et tri
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsMobileSheetOpen(false)}
                aria-label="Fermer les filtres"
                className="inline-flex min-h-[44px] min-w-[44px] items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
              >
                <X className="h-5 w-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

                defs?.map((def) =>
                  renderFilterControl(def, draftValues, setDraftValues, true)
                )}

              {hasSortOptions && (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Trier par
                  </label>

                      </option>

                  </select>
                </div>

              <button
                type="button"

                RÃ©initialiser
              </button>

                type="button"
                onClick={applyMobileSheet}
                className="min-h-[44px] flex-1 rounded-xl bg-sky-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-base font-medium text-white hover:bg-sky-700"
              >
                Appliquer
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );

"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { BottomSheet } from "@/components/ui/BottomSheet";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/Input";
import { Select } from "@/components/ui/Select";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { fetchWithOffline } from "@/lib/offline/apiClient";
import type { ResourceField, RelationConfig } from "@/lib/resources/types";

export interface ResourceFormProps<T = any> {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  fields: ResourceField[];
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  initialData?: Partial<T> | null;
  loading?: boolean;
  onSubmit: (values: Record<string, any>) => Promise<void>;
}

export function ResourceForm<T = any>({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  fields,
  title,
  description,
  initialData,
  loading = false,
  onSubmit,
}: ResourceFormProps<T>) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, any>>({});
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [relationOptions, setRelationOptions] = useState<
    Record<string, Array<{ label: string; value: string }>>
  >({});
  const [loadingRelations, setLoadingRelations] = useState(false);

  // Initialisation des valeurs du formulaire
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const initial: Record<string, any> = {};
    fields.forEach((f) => {
      if (initialData && initialData[f.name as keyof T] !== undefined) {
        let val: any = initialData[f.name as keyof T];
        // Si le champ est un objet relation peuplÃ© (ex: author: { _id, name }), on prend l'_id
        if (val && typeof val === "object" && "_id" in val) {
          val = val._id;
        }
        if (f.type === "date" && val) {
          try {
            val = new Date(val).toISOString().split("T")[0];
          } catch {
            // garder la valeur
          }
        }
        initial[f.name] = val;
      } else if (f.defaultValue !== undefined) {
        initial[f.name] = f.defaultValue;
      } else if (f.type === "date") {
        initial[f.name] = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
      } else if (f.type === "boolean") {
        initial[f.name] = false;
      } else {
        initial[f.name] = "";
      }

    });

    setValues(initial);
    setErrors({});
  }, [isOpen, fields, initialData]);

  // Chargement asynchrone des options pour les champs de type "relation"
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const relationFields = fields.filter((f) => f.type === "relation" && f.relation);
    if (relationFields.length === 0) return;

    let isMounted = true;
    async function loadRelations() {
      setLoadingRelations(true);
      const optionsMap: Record<string, Array<{ label: string; value: string }>> = {};

      await Promise.all(
        relationFields.map(async (field) => {
          const rel = field.relation as RelationConfig;
          const endpoint = rel.apiPath || `/api/${rel.resource}`;
          try {
            const res = await fetchWithOffline<any>(endpoint);
            const items = Array.isArray(res?.data) ? res.data : Array.isArray(res) ? res : [];
            const valueKey = rel.valueField || "_id";

            optionsMap[field.name] = items.map((item: any) => ({
              label: String(item[rel.labelField] || item.name || item.title || item._id),
              value: String(item[valueKey] || item._id),
            }));
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn(`[ResourceForm] Erreur chargement relation ${field.name}:`, err);
            optionsMap[field.name] = [];
          }
        })
      );

      if (isMounted) {
        setRelationOptions(optionsMap);
        setLoadingRelations(false);
      }
    }

    loadRelations();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [isOpen, fields]);

  const handleChange = (name: string, val: any) => {
    setValues((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: val }));
    if (errors[name]) {
      setErrors((prev) => {
        const next = { ...prev };
        delete next[name];
        return next;
      });
    }
  };

  const validate = (): boolean => {
    const newErrors: Record<string, string> = {};

    fields.forEach((f) => {
      const val = values[f.name];
      if (f.required) {
        if (val === undefined || val === null || val === "") {
          newErrors[f.name] = `Le champ "${f.label}" est obligatoire`;
        }
      }
      if (f.type === "number" && val !== "" && val !== undefined) {
        if (isNaN(Number(val))) {
          newErrors[f.name] = `Veuillez saisir un nombre valide`;
        }
      }
    });

    setErrors(newErrors);
    return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!validate()) return;

    // Nettoyage et typage avant soumission
    const cleaned: Record<string, any> = {};
    fields.forEach((f) => {
      const val = values[f.name];
      if (f.type === "number") {
        cleaned[f.name] = val === "" || val === undefined ? undefined : Number(val);
      } else if (f.type === "boolean") {
        cleaned[f.name] = Boolean(val);
      } else if (f.type === "date") {
        if (!val) {
          cleaned[f.name] = f.required ? "" : undefined;
        } else {
          cleaned[f.name] = new Date(val).toISOString();
        }
      } else if (f.type === "relation" || f.type === "select") {
        cleaned[f.name] = val === "" ? undefined : val;
      } else if (f.type === "text" || f.type === "textarea") {
        cleaned[f.name] = val === "" && !f.required ? undefined : val;
      } else {
        cleaned[f.name] = val;
      }
    });

    await onSubmit(cleaned);
  };

  return (
    <BottomSheet isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} title={title} description={description}>
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4 pt-2 pb-4">
        {fields.map((field) => {
          const errorMsg = errors[field.name];
          const val = values[field.name] ?? "";

          // 1. SELECT CLASSIQUE
          if (field.type === "select") {
            const rawOpts = (field.options || []).map((o) => ({
              label: o.label,
              value: String(o.value),
            }));
            const opts =
              !field.required && field.defaultValue === undefined
                ? [{ value: "", label: "â€” Aucun â€”" }, ...rawOpts]
                : rawOpts;
            return (
              <Select
                key={field.name}
                id={`field-${field.name}`}
                label={field.label}
                required={field.required}
                options={opts}
                value={String(val)}
                onChange={(e) => handleChange(field.name, e.target.value)}
                error={errorMsg}
                disabled={loading || field.readOnly}
              />
            );
          }

          // 2. RELATION DYNAMIQUE
          if (field.type === "relation") {
            const rawOpts = relationOptions[field.name] || [];
            const opts = [
              { value: "", label: field.required ? "â€” SÃ©lectionner â€”" : "â€” Aucun â€”" },
              ...rawOpts,
            ];
            return (
              <div key={field.name} className="space-y-1">
                <Select
                  id={`field-${field.name}`}
                  label={field.label}
                  required={field.required}
                  options={opts}
                  value={String(val)}
                  onChange={(e) => handleChange(field.name, e.target.value)}
                  error={errorMsg}
                  disabled={loading || loadingRelations || field.readOnly}
                />
                {loadingRelations && (
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Chargement des options...</p>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          }

          // 3. TEXTAREA
          if (field.type === "textarea") {
            return (
              <div key={field.name} className="space-y-1.5">
                <label
                  htmlFor={`field-${field.name}`}
                  className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
                >
                  {field.label} {field.required && <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  id={`field-${field.name}`}
                  rows={4}
                  value={String(val)}
                  onChange={(e) => handleChange(field.name, e.target.value)}
                  placeholder={field.placeholder}
                  disabled={loading || field.readOnly}
                  className={`w-full rounded-2xl border bg-white p-3 text-base text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 sm:text-sm dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white ${
                    errorMsg
                      ? "border-rose-500 focus:ring-rose-500/20"
                      : "border-slate-200 focus:border-sky-500 focus:ring-sky-500/20 dark:border-slate-800"
                  }`}
                />
                {errorMsg && <p className="text-xs text-rose-500">{errorMsg}</p>}
                {field.helpText && !errorMsg && (
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{field.helpText}</p>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          }

          // 4. BOOLEAN (SWITCH / CHECKBOX)
          if (field.type === "boolean") {
            return (
              <div
                key={field.name}
                className="flex min-h-[44px] items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 p-3.5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/40"
              >
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor={`field-${field.name}`}
                    className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white"
                  >
                    {field.label}
                  </label>
                  {field.helpText && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{field.helpText}</p>
                  )}
                </div>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  id={`field-${field.name}`}
                  checked={Boolean(val)}
                  onChange={(e) => handleChange(field.name, e.target.checked)}
                  disabled={loading || field.readOnly}
                  className="h-5 w-5 rounded border-slate-300 text-sky-600 focus:ring-sky-500 dark:border-slate-700"
                />
              </div>
            );
          }

          // 5. INPUT STANDARD (text, number, date)
          return (
            <Input
              key={field.name}
              id={`field-${field.name}`}
              label={field.label}
              type={field.type}
              required={field.required}
              placeholder={field.placeholder}
              value={val}
              onChange={(e) => handleChange(field.name, e.target.value)}
              error={errorMsg}
              disabled={loading || field.readOnly}
              helperText={field.helpText}
            />
          );
        })}

        {/* Boutons d'action tactiles */}
        <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 pt-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            onClick={onClose}
            disabled={loading}
            className="min-h-[44px] w-full sm:w-auto"
          >
            Annuler
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="submit"
            variant="primary"
            isLoading={loading}
            className="min-h-[44px] w-full sm:w-auto"
          >
            Enregistrer
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}

export function ResourceList<T extends { _id?: string; id?: string }>({
  config,

  // Rendu de la valeur d'une cellule pour la vue tableau
  const renderCellValue = (column: ListColumn<T>, item: T) => {
    const rawVal = item[column.key as keyof T];

    if (column.render) {
      return column.render(rawVal, item);
    }

    if (rawVal === undefined || rawVal === null) {
      return <span className="text-slate-400">â€”</span>;
    }

    if (column.type === "date") {
      try {
        return new Date(rawVal as string).toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", {
          day: "2-digit",
          month: "2-digit",
          year: "numeric",
        });
      } catch {
        return String(rawVal);
      }
    }

    if (column.type === "boolean") {
      return rawVal ? (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-500/10 dark:text-emerald-400">
          Oui
        </span>

        <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400">
          Non
        </span>
      );
    }

    if (column.type === "relation" && typeof rawVal === "object") {
      return (rawVal as any).name || (rawVal as any).title || String((rawVal as any)._id || "");
    }

                  <Skeleton className="h-4 w-1/4" />

            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );

              </Button>

          }
        />
      </div>
    );

"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, Suspense } from "react";
import { useRouter, usePathname, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { RefreshCw, AlertCircle, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { AppLayout } from "@/components/layout/AppLayout";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { FilterBar } from "@/components/crud/FilterBar";
import { ResourceList } from "@/components/crud/ResourceList";
import { ResourceForm } from "@/components/crud/ResourceForm";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/crud/ConfirmDialog";
import { useResource } from "@/hooks/useResource";
import type { ResourceConfig } from "@/lib/resources/types";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";
import { getOfflineSession } from "@/lib/offline/db";

export interface ResourcePageProps<T = any> {
  config: ResourceConfig<T>;
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  headerExtra?: (items: T[]) => React.ReactNode;
}

function ResourcePageInner<T extends { _id?: string; id?: string }>({
  config,
  title,
  description,
  headerExtra,
}: ResourcePageProps<T>) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  const {
    items,
    meta,
    loading,
    error,
    search,
    filters,
    setPage,
    setSearch,
    setFilters,
    refresh,
    create,
    update,
    remove,
  } = useResource(config);

  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<Role | undefined>(undefined);
  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState<boolean>(() =>
    typeof navigator !== "undefined" ? navigator.onLine : true
  );
  const [isFormOpen, setIsFormOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [isConfirmOpen, setIsConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deletingItem, setDeletingItem] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

  const isUrlInitializedRef = useRef(false);

  // Suivi de l'Ã©tat de connexion pour le bouton d'export CSV
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOnline = () => setIsOnline(true);
    const handleOffline = () => setIsOnline(false);
    window.addEventListener("online", handleOnline);
    window.addEventListener("offline", handleOffline);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("online", handleOnline);
      window.removeEventListener("offline", handleOffline);
    };
  }, []);

  // Lecture initiale unique de la query string au montage
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isUrlInitializedRef.current) return;
    isUrlInitializedRef.current = true;

    const initialQ = searchParams.get("q");
    if (initialQ) {
      setSearch(initialQ);
    }

    const initialFilters: Record<string, string> = {};
    searchParams.forEach((value, key) => {
      if (["q", "page", "limit"].includes(key)) return;
      if (value !== "") {
        initialFilters[key] = value;
      }
    });

    if (Object.keys(initialFilters).length > 0) {
      setFilters(initialFilters);
    }
  }, [searchParams, setSearch, setFilters]);

  // Synchronisation de l'URL Ã  chaque changement de recherche ou de filtres (sans page ni limit)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isUrlInitializedRef.current) return;

    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (search && search.trim() !== "") {
      params.set("q", search.trim());
    }
    if (filters) {
      Object.entries(filters).forEach(([key, val]) => {
        if (["page", "limit"].includes(key)) return;
        if (val !== undefined && val !== null && String(val) !== "") {
          params.set(key, String(val));
        }
      });
    }

    const queryString = params.toString();
    const targetUrl = queryString ? `${pathname}?${queryString}` : pathname;
    router.replace(targetUrl, { scroll: false });
  }, [search, filters, pathname, router]);

  // RÃ©cupÃ©ration de la session utilisateur pour Ã©valuer les permissions UI
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/session")
      .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.user?.role) {
          setUserRole(data.user.role as Role);
        } else {
          getOfflineSession().then((offline) => {
            if (offline) setUserRole(offline.role);
          });
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        getOfflineSession().then((offline) => {
          if (offline) setUserRole(offline.role);
        });
      });
  }, []);

  const pageTitle = title || config.plural;
  const pageDescription =
    description || `Gestion et consultation des ${config.plural.toLowerCase()}`;

  const allowCreate = !config.canCreate || config.canCreate(userRole);

  // Extraction des filtres sans la clÃ© "sort" pour FilterBar
  const filterValuesWithoutSort: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (filters) {
    Object.entries(filters).forEach(([k, v]) => {
      if (k !== "sort" && v !== undefined && v !== null && String(v) !== "") {
        filterValuesWithoutSort[k] = String(v);
      }
    });
  }

  // Construction de la query string courante pour l'export CSV
  const exportQueryParams = new URLSearchParams();
  if (search && search.trim() !== "") {
    exportQueryParams.set("q", search.trim());
  }
  if (filters) {
    Object.entries(filters).forEach(([k, v]) => {
      if (["page", "limit"].includes(k)) return;
      if (v !== undefined && v !== null && String(v) !== "") {
        exportQueryParams.set(k, String(v));
      }
    });
  }
  const exportQuery = exportQueryParams.toString();

  const canExportCsv =
    Boolean(config.exportPath) &&
    (userRole === Role.ADMIN || userRole === Role.SYNDIC) &&
    isOnline;

  const filteredRowLinks = config.rowLinks?.filter(
    (l) => !l.roles || (userRole ? l.roles.includes(userRole) : false)
  );

  const handleOpenCreate = () => {
    setEditingItem(null);
    setIsFormOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEdit = (item: T) => {
    setEditingItem(item);
    setIsFormOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenDelete = (item: T) => {
    setDeletingItem(item);
    setIsConfirmOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSubmitForm = async (values: Record<string, any>) => {
    try {
      setIsSubmitting(true);
      if (editingItem) {
        const id = editingItem._id || editingItem.id || "";
        await update(id, values as Partial<T>);
      } else {
        await create(values as Partial<T>);
      }
      setIsFormOpen(false);
      setEditingItem(null);
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err?.details?.fieldErrors && typeof err.details.fieldErrors === "object") {
        const fieldErrors = err.details.fieldErrors as Record<string, string[]>;
        const fieldMap = new Map((config.fields || []).map((f) => [f.name, f.label]));
        const lines = Object.entries(fieldErrors).map(([name, messages]) => {
          const label = fieldMap.get(name) || name;
          const msg = Array.isArray(messages) && messages.length > 0 ? messages[0] : "";
          return `- ${label} : ${msg}`;
        });
        alert(`DonnÃ©es invalides :\n${lines.join("\n")}`);
      } else {
        alert(err.message || "Une erreur est survenue lors de l'enregistrement");
      }
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmDelete = async () => {
    if (!deletingItem) return;
    try {
      setIsDeleting(true);
      const id = deletingItem._id || deletingItem.id || "";
      await remove(id);
      setIsConfirmOpen(false);
      setDeletingItem(null);
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(err.message || "Erreur lors de la suppression");
    } finally {
      setIsDeleting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AppLayout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        {/* En-tÃªte mobile-first */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-3xl dark:text-white">
              {pageTitle}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 sm:text-sm dark:text-slate-400">
              {pageDescription}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start sm:self-center">
            {canExportCsv && (
              <a
                href={`${config.exportPath}?${exportQuery}`}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[44px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 sm:min-h-[36px] dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
              >
                <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>Exporter CSV</span>
              </a>
            )}

            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={refresh}
              disabled={loading}
              className="min-h-[44px] gap-1.5 sm:min-h-[36px]"
            >
              <RefreshCw className={`h-4 w-4 ${loading ? "animate-spin" : ""}`} />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Actualiser</span>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Message d'erreur Ã©ventuel */}
        {error && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-50/50 p-4 text-xs font-medium text-rose-700 dark:border-rose-500/30 dark:bg-rose-950/20 dark:text-rose-300">
            <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-rose-500" />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Indicateurs ou bandeaux personnalisÃ©s */}
        {headerExtra && headerExtra(items)}

        {/* Barre de filtrage et d'action */}
        <FilterBar
          search={search}
          onSearchChange={setSearch}
          searchPlaceholder={
            config.searchPlaceholder || `Rechercher ${config.plural.toLowerCase()}...`
          }
          canCreate={allowCreate}
          createLabel={`Nouveau ${config.label.toLowerCase()}`}
          onCreateClick={handleOpenCreate}
          defs={config.filterDefs}
          values={filterValuesWithoutSort}
          onValuesChange={(v) =>
            setFilters({ ...v, ...(filters.sort ? { sort: filters.sort } : {}) })
          }
          sortOptions={config.sortOptions}
          sort={filters.sort}
          onSortChange={(s) => setFilters({ ...filters, sort: s })}
        />

        {/* Liste des Ã©lÃ©ments (Cartes sur mobile, Tableau sur desktop) */}
        <ResourceList
          config={config}
          items={items}
          loading={loading}
          meta={meta}
          onPageChange={setPage}
          onEdit={handleOpenEdit}
          onDelete={handleOpenDelete}
          onCreateClick={handleOpenCreate}
          userRole={userRole}
          rowLinks={filteredRowLinks}
        />

        {/* Formulaire Bottom Sheet / Modal */}
        <ResourceForm
          isOpen={isFormOpen}
          onClose={() => {
            setIsFormOpen(false);
            setEditingItem(null);
          }}
          fields={config.fields}
          title={
            editingItem
              ? `Modifier ${config.label.toLowerCase()}`
              : `Nouveau ${config.label.toLowerCase()}`
          }
          description={`Remplissez les informations ci-dessous pour ${
            editingItem ? "mettre Ã  jour" : "crÃ©er"
          } ${config.label.toLowerCase()}.`}
          initialData={editingItem}
          loading={isSubmitting}
          onSubmit={handleSubmitForm}
        />

        {/* BoÃ®te de dialogue de confirmation de suppression */}
        <ConfirmDialog
          isOpen={isConfirmOpen}
          title={`Supprimer ${config.label.toLowerCase()}`}
          message={`ÃŠtes-vous sÃ»r de vouloir supprimer cet Ã©lÃ©ment ? Cette action est irrÃ©versible.`}
          confirmLabel="Supprimer"
          cancelLabel="Annuler"
          isDestructive={true}
          loading={isDeleting}
          onConfirm={handleConfirmDelete}
          onCancel={() => {
            setIsConfirmOpen(false);
            setDeletingItem(null);
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </AppLayout>
  );
}

export function ResourcePage<T extends { _id?: string; id?: string }>(
  props: ResourcePageProps<T>
) {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <AppLayout>
          <div className="space-y-4 py-4">
            <div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800" />
            <div className="h-11 w-full animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800" />
            <div className="h-64 w-full animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800" />
          </div>
        </AppLayout>
      }
    >
      <ResourcePageInner {...props} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Building2, Home, Info, ShieldCheck, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";

interface SwaggerViewerProps {
  user?: {
    name?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    role?: Role;
  };
}

export function SwaggerViewer({ user }: SwaggerViewerProps) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    // 1. Injection de la feuille de style Swagger UI
    const linkId = "swagger-ui-styles";
    if (!document.getElementById(linkId)) {
      const link = document.createElement("link");
      link.id = linkId;
      link.rel = "stylesheet";
      link.href = "https://unpkg.com/swagger-ui-dist@5/swagger-ui.css";
      document.head.appendChild(link);
    }

    // 2. Chargement asynchrone des scripts du bundle Swagger UI
    const loadScript = (src: string): Promise<void> => {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        if (document.querySelector(`script[src="${src}"]`)) {
          resolve();
          return;
        }
        const script = document.createElement("script");
        script.src = src;
        script.async = true;
        script.onload = () => resolve();
        script.onerror = () => reject(new Error(`Ã‰chec du chargement du script ${src}`));
        document.body.appendChild(script);
      });
    };

    Promise.all([
      loadScript("https://unpkg.com/swagger-ui-dist@5/swagger-ui-bundle.js"),
      loadScript("https://unpkg.com/swagger-ui-dist@5/swagger-ui-standalone-preset.js"),
    ])
      .then(() => {
        setLoading(false);
        const win = window as any;
        if (win.SwaggerUIBundle) {
          win.SwaggerUIBundle({
            url: "/api/openapi",
            dom_id: "#swagger-ui",
            deepLinking: true,
            presets: [win.SwaggerUIBundle.presets.apis, win.SwaggerUIStandalonePreset],
            layout: "BaseLayout",
            // Point clÃ© B : Envoi automatique des cookies de session pour le "Try it out"
            requestInterceptor: (req: any) => {
              req.credentials = "include";
              return req;
            },
          });
        }
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.error("[SwaggerUI] Erreur de chargement :", err);
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900">
      {/* Barre supÃ©rieure d'information et navigation */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white/95 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur-md dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950/95">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-600 to-sky-400 text-white shadow-md shadow-sky-500/20">
              <Building2 className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-lg font-extrabold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                  Syndic<span className="text-sky-500">Pro</span>
                </span>
                <span className="rounded bg-sky-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-sky-700 dark:bg-sky-950 dark:text-sky-300">
                  OpenAPI 3.0
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Documentation Swagger UI</p>
            </div>
          </div>

            {user && (

            >
              <Home className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Retour Ã  l'application
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Note d'information sur la session */}
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 pt-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-sky-200 bg-sky-50/80 p-4 text-xs text-sky-900 sm:text-sm dark:border-sky-900/50 dark:bg-sky-950/40 dark:text-sky-200">

"use client";

import React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Receipt,
  AlertTriangle,
  Megaphone,
  MoreHorizontal,
  Building2,
  WifiOff,
} from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { useOnlineStatus } from "@/hooks/useOnlineStatus";
import { UserMenu } from "@/components/layout/UserMenu";
import { isRouteAllowed } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { Role } from "@/types/enums";
import { getOfflineSession, saveOfflineSession } from "@/lib/offline/db";

interface NavItem {
  label: string;
  href: string;
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}

const ALL_NAV_ITEMS: NavItem[] = [
  { label: "Accueil", href: "/dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { label: "Charges", href: "/charges", icon: Receipt },
  { label: "Incidents", href: "/incidents", icon: AlertTriangle },
  { label: "Annonces", href: "/announcements", icon: Megaphone },
  { label: "Plus", href: "/more", icon: MoreHorizontal },
];

export interface AppLayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  user?: {
    id?: string;
    name?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    role?: Role;
  };
}

export function AppLayout({ children, user: serverUser }: AppLayoutProps) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { isOnline, pendingCount } = useOnlineStatus();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = React.useState(false);
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = React.useState(serverUser);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
    if (!serverUser) {
      // Tente d'abord de rÃ©cupÃ©rer la session courante via l'API Auth.js
      fetch("/api/auth/session")
        .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
        .then((data) => {
          if (data?.user?.id) {
            const u = {
              id: data.user.id,
              name: data.user.name,
              email: data.user.email,
              role: data.user.role as Role,
            };
            setCurrentUser(u);
            saveOfflineSession({
              id: u.id,
              name: u.name || "Utilisateur",
              email: u.email || "",
              role: u.role || Role.RESIDENT,
              expiresAt: Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1000,
            }).catch(() => {});
          } else {
            // Repli sur la session en cache local Dexie (mode hors ligne)
            getOfflineSession().then((offline) => {
              if (offline) {
                setCurrentUser({
                  id: offline.id,
                  name: offline.name,
                  email: offline.email,
                  role: offline.role,
                });
              }
            });
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {
          getOfflineSession().then((offline) => {
            if (offline) {
              setCurrentUser({
                id: offline.id,
                name: offline.name,
                email: offline.email,
                role: offline.role,
              });
            }
          });
        });
    } else {
      setCurrentUser(serverUser);
    }
  }, [serverUser]);

  const showOffline = mounted && !isOnline;
  const userRole = currentUser?.role || Role.RESIDENT;

  // Filtrage strict des Ã©lÃ©ments de navigation selon la matrice de permissions
  const visibleNavItems = ALL_NAV_ITEMS.filter((item) =>
    isRouteAllowed(item.href, userRole)
  );

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950">
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* 1. SIDEBAR DESKTOP & TABLETTE (>= md)                        */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <aside className="hidden md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:flex md:w-64 md:flex-col md:border-r md:border-slate-200 md:bg-white md:dark:border-slate-800 md:dark:bg-slate-900/80 md:backdrop-blur-md">
        {/* Logo SyndicPro */}
        <div className="flex h-16 items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 px-6 dark:border-slate-800">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-600 to-sky-400 text-white shadow-md shadow-sky-500/20">
            <Building2 className="h-5 w-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              Syndic<span className="text-sky-500">Pro</span>
            </span>
            <span className="block text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
              PWA CopropriÃ©tÃ©
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Liens de navigation desktop filtrÃ©s par rÃ´le */}
        <nav className="flex-1 space-y-1.5 px-4 py-6">
          {visibleNavItems.map((item) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            const isActive =
              pathname === item.href ||
              (item.href !== "/dashboard" && pathname?.startsWith(item.href));

            return (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                className={clsx(
                  "flex items-center gap-3.5 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold transition-all",
                  isActive
                    ? "bg-sky-500 text-white shadow-md shadow-sky-500/20"
                    : "text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/60 dark:hover:text-slate-200"

  [Role.ADMIN]: { label: "Admin", variant: "danger" },
  [Role.SYNDIC]: { label: "Syndic", variant: "info" },
  [Role.TECHNICIAN]: { label: "Technicien", variant: "warning" },

export function UserMenu({ initialUser, compact = false }: UserMenuProps) {

    if (initialUser?.id && initialUser.role) {
      return {
        id: initialUser.id,
        name: initialUser.name || "Utilisateur",
        email: initialUser.email || "",
        role: initialUser.role,
        expiresAt: Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1000,
      };
    }
    return null;
  });

  const [isLoggingOut, setIsLoggingOut] = React.useState(false);

  // Synchronisation du profil dans le cache Dexie pour la persistance hors-ligne
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (initialUser?.id && initialUser.role) {
      const profile: OfflineSessionProfile = {
        id: initialUser.id,
        name: initialUser.name || "Utilisateur",
        email: initialUser.email || "",
        role: initialUser.role,
        expiresAt: Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1000,
      };
      setUser(profile);
      saveOfflineSession(profile).catch(() => {});
    } else {
      // Si non fourni par le serveur (ex: chargement hors-ligne), lecture depuis Dexie
      getOfflineSession().then((cached) => {
        if (cached) setUser(cached);
      });
    }
  }, [initialUser]);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    if (isLoggingOut) return;

    try {
      // Exigence H5 : Avertissement si des modifications hors-ligne sont en attente
      const db = getOfflineDB();
      if (db.outbox) {
        const pendingCount = await db.outbox.where("status").anyOf(["pending", "failed"]).count();

            `${pendingCount} modification(s) non synchronisÃ©e(s) seront dÃ©finitivement perdues. ÃŠtes-vous sÃ»r de vouloir vous dÃ©connecter ?`

      await clearAllOfflineData(true);

      if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && "serviceWorker" in navigator && navigator.serviceWorker.controller) {
        navigator.serviceWorker.controller.postMessage({ type: "LOGOUT" });
      }

      console.error("[UserMenu] Erreur lors de la dÃ©connexion :", err);
      // Redirection de secours
      window.location.href = "/login";
    } finally {
      setIsLoggingOut(false);
    }
  };

  if (!user) {
    return null;

  if (compact) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-sky-100 text-sky-700 font-bold text-xs dark:bg-sky-950 dark:text-sky-300">
