import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Download, Share2, PlusSquare, X } from "lucide-react";

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: "accepted" | "dismissed" }>;
}

export function InstallPrompt() {
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const [isIOS, setIsIOS] = useState(false);
  const [isStandalone, setIsStandalone] = useState(false);
  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

    // DÃ©tection si l'application s'exÃ©cute dÃ©jÃ  en mode PWA installÃ©e
    const standaloneMedia = window.matchMedia("(display-mode: standalone)");
    const isStandaloneMode =
      standaloneMedia.matches ||
      (window.navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;

    setIsStandalone(isStandaloneMode);

    // DÃ©tection iOS (Safari)
    const userAgent = window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
    const isAppleDevice = /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(userAgent);
    setIsIOS(isAppleDevice);

    // Ã‰coute de l'Ã©vÃ©nement standard beforeinstallprompt (Chromium / Android)
    const handleBeforeInstallPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    window.addEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", handleBeforeInstallPrompt);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleInstallClick = async () => {
    if (!deferredPrompt) return;
    await deferredPrompt.prompt();
    const choice = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
    if (choice.outcome === "accepted") {
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
    }
  };

  // Ne pas afficher si dÃ©jÃ  installÃ© ou si l'utilisateur l'a fermÃ©
  if (isStandalone || dismissed) {
    return null;
  }

  // Si on a le prompt Chromium
  if (deferredPrompt) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed bottom-20 left-4 right-4 z-40 mx-auto max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-sky-500/30 bg-slate-900/95 p-4 text-white shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md sm:bottom-6 sm:left-6 sm:right-auto">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400">
              <Download className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Installer SyndicPro</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
                AccÃ¨s rapide hors ligne et expÃ©rience plein Ã©cran.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setDismissed(true)}
            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            aria-label="Fermer"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={handleInstallClick}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-sky-500 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow hover:bg-sky-400 active:scale-95"
          >
            <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Installer l&apos;application
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Si sur iOS Safari
  if (isIOS) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed bottom-20 left-4 right-4 z-40 mx-auto max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-sky-500/30 bg-slate-900/95 p-4 text-white shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md sm:bottom-6 sm:left-6 sm:right-auto">

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";

/**
 * Enregistrement du Service Worker PWA.
 * - En dÃ©veloppement (npm run dev) : DÃ©senregistre tout Service Worker pour garantir
 *   que le site fonctionne normalement sans interfÃ©rence de cache.
 * - En production (npm start) : Active le Service Worker pour le prÃ©-cache et le mode hors ligne.
 */
export function ServiceWorkerRegister() {
  const [waitingWorker, setWaitingWorker] = useState<ServiceWorker | null>(null);
  const [showUpdate, setShowUpdate] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || !("serviceWorker" in navigator)) {
      return;
    }

    const isProd = process.env.NODE_ENV === "production";
    const forceEnable = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SW === "true";

    // En DEV : on s'assure qu'aucun ancien service worker ne tourne pour naviguer normalement
    if (!isProd && !forceEnable) {
      navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations().then((registrations) => {
        for (const reg of registrations) {
          reg.unregister();
          console.log("[SW] Service Worker dÃ©senregistrÃ© en mode dev pour fonctionnement normal");
        }
      });
      return;
    }

    // En PRODUCTION : enregistrement standard
    navigator.serviceWorker
      .register("/sw.js", { scope: "/" })
      .then((registration) => {
        if (registration.waiting) {
          setWaitingWorker(registration.waiting);
          setShowUpdate(true);
        }

        registration.addEventListener("updatefound", () => {
          const installingWorker = registration.installing;
          if (!installingWorker) return;

          installingWorker.addEventListener("statechange", () => {
            if (
              installingWorker.state === "installed" &&
              navigator.serviceWorker.controller
            ) {
              setWaitingWorker(installingWorker);
              setShowUpdate(true);
            }
          });
        });
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.warn("[SW] Enregistrement ignorÃ© :", err);
      });

    let refreshing = false;
    navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener("controllerchange", () => {
      if (!refreshing) {
        refreshing = true;
        window.location.reload();
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const handleUpdate = () => {
    if (waitingWorker) {
      waitingWorker.postMessage({ type: "SKIP_WAITING" });
    }
    setShowUpdate(false);
  };

  if (!showUpdate) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-20 left-4 right-4 z-50 mx-auto max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-sky-500/20 bg-slate-900/95 p-4 text-white shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md sm:bottom-6 sm:right-6 sm:left-auto">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="text-sm">
          <p className="font-semibold text-sky-400">Mise Ã  jour prÃªte</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
            Une nouvelle version de l&apos;application est disponible.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={handleUpdate}
          className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-sky-500 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow hover:bg-sky-400 active:scale-95"
        >
          <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          Recharger
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// Lecture sÃ©curisÃ©e des recherches rÃ©centes depuis localStorage
function loadRecentSearches(): string[] {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(RECENT_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
      return parsed.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string").slice(0, 5);
    }
    return [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

// Sauvegarde sÃ©curisÃ©e dans localStorage (5 max)
function saveRecentSearch(term: string): string[] {
  const cleaned = term.trim();
  if (cleaned.length < 2) return loadRecentSearches();
  try {
    const current = loadRecentSearches();
    const updated = [cleaned, ...current.filter((q) => q.toLowerCase() !== cleaned.toLowerCase())].slice(0, 5);
    window.localStorage.setItem(RECENT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
    return updated;
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

// Recherche locale dans IndexedDB via Dexie en mode hors ligne
async function searchOfflineDexie(q: string): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
  const needle = q.toLowerCase();
  const db = getOfflineDB();
  const results: SearchResult[] = [];

  try {
    const [incidents, announcements, charges, documents] = await Promise.all([
      db.incidents
        .filter(
          (i) =>
            Boolean(i.title?.toLowerCase().includes(needle)) ||
            Boolean(i.description?.toLowerCase().includes(needle))
        )
        .limit(5)
        .toArray()
        .catch(() => []),
      db.announcements
        .filter(
          (a) =>
            Boolean(a.title?.toLowerCase().includes(needle)) ||
            Boolean(a.content?.toLowerCase().includes(needle))
        )
        .limit(5)
        .toArray()
        .catch(() => []),
      db.charges
        .filter((c) => Boolean(c.period?.toLowerCase().includes(needle)))
        .limit(5)
        .toArray()
        .catch(() => []),
      db.documents
        .filter((d) => Boolean(d.title?.toLowerCase().includes(needle)))
        .limit(5)
        .toArray()
        .catch(() => []),
    ]);

    for (const inc of incidents) {
      const title = inc.title || "";
      results.push({
        type: "incident",
        id: String(inc._id),
        title,
        subtitle: inc.description ? inc.description.slice(0, 80) : undefined,
        badge: inc.status,
        href: `/incidents?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`,
      });
    }

    for (const ann of announcements) {
      const title = ann.title || "";
      results.push({
        type: "announcement",
        id: String(ann._id),
        title,
        subtitle: ann.content ? ann.content.slice(0, 80) : undefined,
        href: `/announcements?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`,
      });
    }

    for (const ch of charges) {
      const period = ch.period || "";
      results.push({
        type: "charge",
        id: String(ch._id),
        title: `Charge ${period}`,
        badge: ch.status,
        href: `/charges?q=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`,
      });
    }

    for (const doc of documents) {
      const title = doc.title || "";
      results.push({
        type: "document",
        id: String(doc._id),
        title,
        badge: doc.type,
        href: `/documents?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`,
      });
    }
  } catch {
    // Retourne les rÃ©sultats partiels en cas d'erreur Dexie
  }

  return results;
}

export function GlobalSearch({ variant }: { variant: "icon" | "field" }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [results, setResults] = useState<SearchResult[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isOfflineResult, setIsOfflineResult] = useState(false);
  const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] = useState<number>(-1);
  const [recentSearches, setRecentSearches] = useState<string[]>(() => loadRecentSearches());

  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const debounceTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  // Nettoyage des timers et requÃªtes en cours
  const cleanupPendingSearch = useCallback(() => {
    if (debounceTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(debounceTimerRef.current);
      debounceTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
      abortControllerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  // Ouverture de la modale de recherche
  const openModal = useCallback(() => {
    setRecentSearches(loadRecentSearches());
    setIsOpen(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      inputRef.current?.focus();
    }, 10);
  }, []);

  // Fermeture et rÃ©initialisation de la modale
  const closeModal = useCallback(() => {
    cleanupPendingSearch();
    setIsOpen(false);
    setQuery("");
    setResults([]);
    setIsLoading(false);
    setError(null);
    setIsOfflineResult(false);
    setSelectedIndex(-1);
  }, [cleanupPendingSearch]);

  // ExÃ©cution effective de la recherche (API ou repli Dexie hors ligne)
  const executeSearch = useCallback(async (searchTerm: string) => {
    const trimmed = searchTerm.trim();
    if (trimmed.length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      setIsLoading(false);
      setError(null);
      setIsOfflineResult(false);
      setSelectedIndex(-1);
      return;
    }

    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    // Mode hors ligne direct
    if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator.onLine === false) {
      try {
        const localData = await searchOfflineDexie(trimmed);
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setResults(localData);
          setIsOfflineResult(true);
          setError(null);
          setIsLoading(false);
          setSelectedIndex(localData.length > 0 ? 0 : -1);
        }
      } catch {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setError("Impossible de charger les rÃ©sultats hors ligne.");
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}`, {
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error("Erreur rÃ©seau");
      }

      const payload = (await response.json()) as { data?: SearchResult[] };
      if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
        const list = Array.isArray(payload.data) ? payload.data : [];
        setResults(list);
        setIsOfflineResult(false);
        setError(null);
        setIsLoading(false);
        setSelectedIndex(list.length > 0 ? 0 : -1);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if ((err as Error)?.name === "AbortError") {
        return;
      }
      // En cas d'Ã©chec de fetch, tentative sur la base locale Dexie
      try {
        const localData = await searchOfflineDexie(trimmed);
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setResults(localData);
          setIsOfflineResult(true);
          setError(null);
          setIsLoading(false);
          setSelectedIndex(localData.length > 0 ? 0 : -1);
        }
      } catch {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setError("Une erreur rÃ©seau est survenue lors de la recherche.");
          setResults([]);
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }
  }, []);

  // DÃ©clenchement de la recherche depuis onChange avec debounce de 300 ms
  const handleInputChange = (value: string) => {
    setQuery(value);
    cleanupPendingSearch();

    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (trimmed.length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      setIsLoading(false);
      setError(null);
      setIsOfflineResult(false);
      setSelectedIndex(-1);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    debounceTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      void executeSearch(trimmed);
    }, 300);
  };

  // SÃ©lection d'un rÃ©sultat et navigation
  const handleSelectResult = useCallback(
    (item: SearchResult) => {
      const updated = saveRecentSearch(query.trim() || item.title);
      setRecentSearches(updated);
      closeModal();
      router.push(item.href);
    },
    [query, closeModal, router]
  );

  // Liste ordonnÃ©e Ã  plat pour la navigation clavier
  const orderedResults: SearchResult[] = [];
  for (const type of GROUP_ORDER) {
    for (const r of results) {
      if (r.type === type) {
        orderedResults.push(r);
      }
    }
  }

  // Raccourcis clavier globaux (Ctrl+K / Cmd+K et Ã‰chap)
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleGlobalKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (isOpen) {
          closeModal();
        } else {
          openModal();
        }
      } else if (e.key === "Escape" && isOpen) {
        e.preventDefault();
        closeModal();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleGlobalKeyDown);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleGlobalKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, openModal, closeModal]);

  // Nettoyage au dÃ©montage du composant
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      cleanupPendingSearch();
    };
  }, [cleanupPendingSearch]);

  // Navigation clavier dans le champ de recherche (FlÃ¨ches Haut/Bas et EntrÃ©e)
  const handleKeyDownInModal = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown") {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (orderedResults.length > 0) {
        setSelectedIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % orderedResults.length);
      }
    } else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (orderedResults.length > 0) {
        setSelectedIndex((prev) => (prev <= 0 ? orderedResults.length - 1 : prev - 1));
      }
    } else if (e.key === "Enter") {
      if (selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex < orderedResults.length) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleSelectResult(orderedResults[selectedIndex]);
      }
    }
  };

  // Effacer l'historique des recherches rÃ©centes
  const handleClearRecent = () => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.removeItem(RECENT_STORAGE_KEY);
    } catch {
      // Ignore les erreurs d'accÃ¨s au stockage
    }
    setRecentSearches([]);
  };

  const trimmedQuery = query.trim();

  return (
    <>
      {variant === "icon" ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={openModal}
          aria-label="Rechercher"
          className="inline-flex min-h-[44px] min-w-[44px] items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:text-white"
        >
          <Search className="h-5 w-5" />
        </button>
      ) : (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={openModal}
          className="flex min-h-[44px] w-full max-w-xs items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-500 transition-colors hover:border-sky-300 hover:bg-white dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800/70 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:border-sky-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
        >
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 truncate">
            <Search className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" />
            <span>Rechercherâ€¦</span>
          </span>
          <kbd className="hidden rounded border border-slate-200 bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 sm:inline-block dark:border-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
            Ctrl K
          </kbd>
        </button>
      )}

      {isOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col bg-white pt-[env(safe-area-inset-top)] md:items-center md:justify-start md:bg-slate-900/50 md:pt-20 md:backdrop-blur-xs dark:bg-slate-900 dark:md:bg-slate-950/70"
          onClick={closeModal}
        >
          <div
            className="flex h-full w-full flex-col overflow-hidden bg-white md:h-auto md:max-h-[80vh] md:max-w-2xl md:rounded-2xl md:border md:border-slate-200 md:shadow-2xl dark:bg-slate-900 dark:md:border-slate-800"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            {/* Barre de saisie supÃ©rieure */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 dark:border-slate-800">
              <Search className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400" />
              <input
                ref={inputRef}
                type="search"
                autoFocus
                value={query}
                onChange={(e) => handleInputChange(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={handleKeyDownInModal}
                placeholder="Rechercher un incident, rÃ©sident, documentâ€¦"
                className="min-h-[44px] flex-1 bg-transparent text-base text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500"
              />
              {query.length > 0 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    handleInputChange("");
                    inputRef.current?.focus();
                  }}
                  aria-label="Effacer la saisie"
                  className="inline-flex min-h-[44px] min-w-[44px] items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-200"
                >
                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              )}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={closeModal}
                aria-label="Fermer la recherche"
                className="inline-flex min-h-[44px] items-center justify-center rounded-lg px-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
              >
                Fermer

import React from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import {
  chargeStatusLabel,
  incidentStatusLabel,
  priorityLabel,
  type ChargeStatus,
  type IncidentStatus,
  type Priority,
} from "@/types/enums";

export interface BadgeProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> {
  variant?: "default" | "success" | "warning" | "danger" | "info" | "neutral";
  chargeStatus?: ChargeStatus;
  incidentStatus?: IncidentStatus;
  priority?: Priority;
}

export function Badge({
  className,
  variant,
  chargeStatus,
  incidentStatus,
  priority,
  children,
  ...props
}: BadgeProps) {
  let computedVariant = variant || "default";
  let content = children;

  if (chargeStatus) {
    content = chargeStatusLabel[chargeStatus];
    switch (chargeStatus) {
      case "PAID":
        computedVariant = "success";
        break;
      case "PARTIAL":
        computedVariant = "warning";
        break;
      case "OVERDUE":
        computedVariant = "danger";
        break;
      case "PENDING":
        computedVariant = "info";
        break;
    }
  } else if (incidentStatus) {
    content = incidentStatusLabel[incidentStatus];
    switch (incidentStatus) {
      case "RESOLVED":
        computedVariant = "success";
        break;
      case "IN_PROGRESS":
        computedVariant = "info";
        break;
      case "ASSIGNED":
        computedVariant = "warning";
        break;
      case "REPORTED":
        computedVariant = "danger";
        break;
    }
  } else if (priority) {
    content = priorityLabel[priority];
    switch (priority) {
      case "URGENT":
        computedVariant = "danger";
        break;
      case "HIGH":
        computedVariant = "warning";
        break;
      case "MEDIUM":
        computedVariant = "info";
        break;
      case "LOW":
        computedVariant = "neutral";
        break;
    }
  }

  const variantStyles = {
    default: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300",
    success:
      "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-500/20 dark:bg-emerald-400/10 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-400/20",
    warning:
      "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 border border-amber-500/20 dark:bg-amber-400/10 dark:text-amber-400 dark:border-amber-400/20",
    danger:
      "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-700 border border-rose-500/20 dark:bg-rose-400/10 dark:text-rose-400 dark:border-rose-400/20",
    info:
      "bg-sky-500/10 text-sky-700 border border-sky-500/20 dark:bg-sky-400/10 dark:text-sky-400 dark:border-sky-400/20",
    neutral: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400",
  };

  return (
    <span
      className={clsx(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide select-none",
        variantStyles[computedVariant],
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current opacity-70" />
      {content}
    </span>

export interface BottomSheetProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function BottomSheet({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  description,
  children,
  className,
}: BottomSheetProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };

    // Bloque le dÃ©filement arriÃ¨re-plan
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);

    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center sm:items-center sm:p-4">
      {/* Backdrop sombre avec flou */}
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-950/60 backdrop-blur-sm transition-opacity"
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      {/* Conteneur : Bottom Sheet sur mobile (< sm/md), Modal centrÃ© sur desktop */}
      <div
        className={clsx(
          "relative z-10 flex max-h-[90vh] w-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-t-3xl bg-white shadow-2xl transition-all dark:bg-slate-900 sm:max-w-lg sm:rounded-3xl sm:border sm:border-slate-800",
          className
        )}
        style={{
          paddingBottom: "max(1rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 1rem))",
        }}
      >
        {/* PoignÃ©e de glissement sur mobile */}
        <div className="mx-auto mt-3 h-1.5 w-12 rounded-full bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-700 sm:hidden" />

        {/* En-tÃªte */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between px-6 pt-4 pb-3">
          <div>
            {title && (
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                {title}
              </h2>
            )}
            {description && (
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                {description}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-200"
            aria-label="Fermer"

            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>

        {/* Contenu avec dÃ©filement interne fluide */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-2">{children}</div>

import React from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";

export interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "outline" | "danger" | "ghost";
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  isLoading?: boolean;
}

export const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  (
    {
      className,
      variant = "primary",
      size = "md",
      isLoading = false,
      disabled,
      children,
      ...props
    },
    ref
  ) => {
    const baseStyles =
      "inline-flex items-center justify-center font-medium transition-all focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none active:scale-[0.98] select-none";

    const variants = {
      primary:
        "bg-sky-600 text-white hover:bg-sky-500 active:bg-sky-700 shadow-sm focus:ring-sky-500",
      secondary:
        "bg-slate-200 text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 focus:ring-slate-400",
      outline:
        "border border-slate-300 bg-transparent text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/60 focus:ring-slate-400",
      danger:
        "bg-rose-600 text-white hover:bg-rose-500 active:bg-rose-700 shadow-sm focus:ring-rose-500",
      ghost:
        "bg-transparent text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 focus:ring-slate-400",
    };

    // Hauteur minimale garantie >= 44px sur mobile pour confort tactile
    const sizes = {
      sm: "min-h-[40px] px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-xl gap-1.5",
      md: "min-h-[44px] px-4 py-2 text-sm rounded-xl gap-2",
      lg: "min-h-[48px] px-5 py-2.5 text-base rounded-2xl gap-2.5 font-semibold",
    };

    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        disabled={disabled || isLoading}
        className={clsx(baseStyles, variants[variant], sizes[size], className)}
        {...props}
      >
        {isLoading && (
          <svg
            className="animate-spin h-4 w-4 shrink-0"
            xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
            fill="none"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          >
            <circle
              className="opacity-25"
              cx="12"
              cy="12"
              r="10"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="4"
            />
            <path
              className="opacity-75"
              fill="currentColor"
              d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8v8H4z"
            />
          </svg>
        )}
        {children}
      </button>
    );
  }
);

import React from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";

export interface SelectProps extends React.SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
  options?: { value: string | number; label: string }[];
}

export const Select = React.forwardRef<HTMLSelectElement, SelectProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, id, options, children, ...props }, ref) => {
    const selectId = id || (label ? label.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-") : undefined);

    return (
      <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label
            htmlFor={selectId}
            className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
          >
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <select
          id={selectId}
          ref={ref}
          className={clsx(
            "min-h-[44px] w-full rounded-xl border bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-base text-slate-900 shadow-sm transition-all focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sky-500 disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-60 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white dark:disabled:bg-slate-800",
            error
              ? "border-rose-500 focus:border-rose-500 focus:ring-rose-400"
              : "border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 focus:border-sky-500",
            className
          )}
          {...props}
        >
          {options
            ? options.map((opt) => (
                <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                  {opt.label}
                </option>
              ))
            : children}
        </select>
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-xs text-rose-500">{error}</p>
        ) : helperText ? (
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{helperText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);

=== FILE: C:\atelier_nextJs\syndicpro\hooks\useOnlineStatus.ts ===
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { getOfflineDB } from "@/lib/offline/db";
import { syncOutbox, onSyncChange, initAutoSync } from "@/lib/offline/sync";

export function useOnlineStatus() {
  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(
    typeof navigator !== "undefined" ? navigator.onLine : true
  );
  const [pendingCount, setPendingCount] = useState(0);
  const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing] = useState(false);

  const refreshPendingCount = useCallback(async () => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
    try {
      const db = getOfflineDB();
      const count = await db.outbox.where("status").anyOf(["pending", "failed"]).count();
      setPendingCount(count);
    } catch {
      // Ignorer si IndexedDB n'est pas encore initialisÃ©
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

    setIsOnline(navigator.onLine);
    refreshPendingCount();
    initAutoSync();

    const handleOnline = () => {
      setIsOnline(true);
      refreshPendingCount();
    };

    const handleOffline = () => {
      setIsOnline(false);
      refreshPendingCount();
    };

    window.addEventListener("online", handleOnline);
    window.addEventListener("offline", handleOffline);

    const unsubscribe = onSyncChange((syncing, count) => {
      setIsSyncing(syncing);
      setPendingCount(count);
    });

    // Intervalle de vÃ©rification du compteur outbox
    const interval = setInterval(refreshPendingCount, 5000);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("online", handleOnline);
      window.removeEventListener("offline", handleOffline);
      unsubscribe();
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [refreshPendingCount]);

  const syncNow = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsSyncing(true);
    try {
      await syncOutbox();
    } finally {
      await refreshPendingCount();
      setIsSyncing(false);
    }
  }, [refreshPendingCount]);

  return { isOnline, pendingCount, isSyncing, syncNow, refreshPendingCount };
}
=== FILE: C:\atelier_nextJs\syndicpro\hooks\useResource.ts ===
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from "react";
import { fetchWithOffline } from "@/lib/offline/apiClient";
import type { ResourceConfig } from "@/lib/resources/types";

export interface PaginationMeta {
  page: number;
  limit: number;
  total: number;
}

export interface UseResourceReturn<T> {
  items: T[];
  meta: PaginationMeta;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  page: number;
  search: string;
  filters: Record<string, any>;
  setPage: (page: number) => void;
  setSearch: (search: string) => void;
  setFilters: (filters: Record<string, any>) => void;
  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
  getOne: (id: string) => Promise<T>;
  create: (data: Partial<T>) => Promise<T>;
  update: (id: string, data: Partial<T>) => Promise<T>;
  remove: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
}

export function useResource<T extends { _id?: string; id?: string }>(
  config: ResourceConfig<T>
): UseResourceReturn<T> {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<T[]>([]);
  const [meta, setMeta] = useState<PaginationMeta>({ page: 1, limit: 20, total: 0 });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<Record<string, any>>({});

  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      mountedRef.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const fetchList = useCallback(
    async (currentPage = page, currentSearch = search, currentFilters = filters) => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError(null);

        const params = new URLSearchParams();
        params.set("page", currentPage.toString());
        params.set("limit", "20");

        if (currentSearch.trim()) {
          params.set("q", currentSearch.trim());
        }

        if (config.defaultSort) {
          params.set("sort", config.defaultSort);
        }

        Object.entries(currentFilters).forEach(([key, val]) => {
          if (val !== undefined && val !== null && val !== "") {
            params.set(key, String(val));
          }
        });

        const url = `${config.apiPath}?${params.toString()}`;

        const res = await fetchWithOffline<any>(url, {
          entity: config.entityOfflineKey,
        });

        if (!mountedRef.current) return;

        // DÃ©tection format API standard { data, meta } ou fallback Dexie (tableau direct)
        if (res && Array.isArray(res.data)) {
          setItems(res.data);
          setMeta(res.meta || { page: currentPage, limit: 20, total: res.data.length });
        } else if (Array.isArray(res)) {
          setItems(res);
          setMeta({ page: currentPage, limit: 20, total: res.length });
        } else {
          setItems([]);
          setMeta({ page: currentPage, limit: 20, total: 0 });
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (!mountedRef.current) return;
        setError(err.message || "Erreur de chargement des donnÃ©es");
        setItems([]);
      } finally {
        if (mountedRef.current) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    },
    [config.apiPath, config.entityOfflineKey, config.defaultSort, page, search, filters]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchList(page, search, filters);
  }, [fetchList, page, search, filters]);

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    await fetchList(page, search, filters);
  }, [fetchList, page, search, filters]);

  const getOne = useCallback(
    async (id: string): Promise<T> => {
      return await fetchWithOffline<T>(`${config.apiPath}/${id}`);
    },
    [config.apiPath]
  );

  const create = useCallback(
    async (data: Partial<T>): Promise<T> => {
      const res = await fetchWithOffline<T>(config.apiPath, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
        entity: config.entityOfflineKey,
      });
      await refresh();
      return res;
    },
    [config.apiPath, config.entityOfflineKey, refresh]
  );

  const update = useCallback(
    async (id: string, data: Partial<T>): Promise<T> => {
      const res = await fetchWithOffline<T>(`${config.apiPath}/${id}`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
        entity: config.entityOfflineKey,
      });
      await refresh();
      return res;
    },
    [config.apiPath, config.entityOfflineKey, refresh]
  );

  const remove = useCallback(
    async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      await fetchWithOffline<{ success: boolean }>(`${config.apiPath}/${id}`, {
        method: "DELETE",
      });
      await refresh();
    },
    [config.apiPath, refresh]
  );

  const handleSetSearch = useCallback((newSearch: string) => {
    setSearch(newSearch);
    setPage(1);
  }, []);

  const handleSetFilters = useCallback((newFilters: Record<string, any>) => {
    setFilters(newFilters);
    setPage(1);
  }, []);

  return {
    items,
    meta,
    loading,
    error,
    page,
    search,
    filters,
    setPage,
    setSearch: handleSetSearch,
    setFilters: handleSetFilters,
    refresh,
    getOne,
    create,
    update,
    remove,
  };
}
